###############################################################################
# The Order of Mysteries
# Namespace: verena (was DR_societies.1000 - 1122, 1200 - 1299, 1400 - 1481,
#   1600 - 1646, 1800 - 1855, 1900 - 1969 and 5107 - 5115 in
#   DR_societies_mnm_events.txt, and the same ids of MNM in
#   mnm_hermetics_events.txt; both files are gone)
# Faction: Old World faiths
# Serves: the Order of Mysteries (cult_of_verena), the Lorekeepers of
#   Verena who recover lost and forbidden knowledge
#
# Verena gave mankind the written word, and the Order of Mysteries goes
# after what was written and lost: in musty libraries, distant ruins and
# haunted tombs. Its brothers and sisters send each other on missions: to
# take on an apprentice, to build a library (from a reading room to a
# rival of the Grand Temple of Nuln, with copyists, lamps, bindings and
# books to choose, and fire, a Sigmarite mob or damp along the way), to
# recover a lost tome, scroll or tablet (once in a life, the Tablet of
# Lybaras from the sealed archives of Nehekhara), and to raid the hoard
# of a brother who locks his books away like the Scrollbearers. Its
# priests pray the Blind Maiden over still water to see what is to come,
# and its High Priests read the star signs of the Old World above a
# child's birth. Its brothers gather herbs, beast parts and alchemical
# salts, brew draughts against melancholy from the Principia Herbologium,
# watch the two moons and the twenty signs for a season, and give
# themselves to one of three mysteries: the stars, the alembic or the
# Goddess, as a Lorekeeper or a Scalebearer. Once in ten years a High
# Priest calls a symposium; members invoke the Goddess in trance, write
# treatises for the brethren to review, spend nights under the stars
# (sometimes under a waxing Morrslieb) and, at the end, put a life's
# knowledge into a ciphered magnum opus. The cult of Tzeentch shares the
# treatise and the magnum opus. Lore from WFRP 2E Tome of
# Salvation (the Cult of Verena, its orders, sects and miracles: the Blind
# Maiden, Owl's Wisdom) and The WFRP Companion (the star signs, the herbs
# and medicine of the Empire).
#
# Missions                             verena.1
# The library (also the Cult of Tzeentch's library of forbidden lore and a
#   Skaven warp-laboratory; voices in WHG_lab_voices.txt)
#                                      verena.10 - verena.22
# The apprentice                       verena.30 - verena.35
# The lost texts                       verena.40 - verena.49
# The Scrollbearer's hoard             verena.50 - verena.76
# The scrying                          verena.100 - verena.121
# The horoscope                        verena.130 - verena.141
# Gathering ingredients                verena.150 - verena.153
# The draught against melancholy       verena.160 - verena.164
# The three mysteries                  verena.170 - verena.179
# The apprenticeship                   verena.180 - verena.184
# Watching the stars                   verena.200 - verena.226
# The cabinet of ingredients           verena.230 - verena.233
# The symposium                        verena.250 - verena.271
# The invocation of Verena             verena.280 - verena.286
# The theory paper                     verena.300 - verena.308
# The magnum opus                      verena.320 - verena.325
# The night under the stars            verena.400 - verena.451
#
# Callers: on_society_bi_yearly_pulse (verena.1), the decision
#   build_library in decisions/wh_cult_societies_decisions.txt
#   (verena.10), the gain_effect of title_hermetics_apprentice in
#   common/minor_titles/00_minor_titles.txt (verena.35), the decisions
#   hermetics_perform_scrying (verena.100) and hermetics_make_horoscope
#   (verena.130) in decisions/mnm_hermetics_decisions.txt, on_birth
#   (verena.105), on_death (106), on_adulthood (136), on_new_holder,
#   on_new_holder_inheritance and on_new_holder_usurpation (110, 111),
#   GRANT_LANDED_TITLE_INTERACTION_ACCEPT_EVENT (112), the combat pulse in
#   events/WHG_battle_events.txt (102, 103), the pet deaths in
#   common/scripted_effects/wh_pet_effects.txt, WHG_battle_events and
#   WHG_hunting_events (108), and the sickness and recovery effects in
#   common/scripted_effects/00_scripted_effects.txt (115 - 118), the
#   decisions hermetics_acquire_ingredients_decision (verena.150) and
#   brew_happiness_potion (160) in decisions/mnm_hermetics_decisions.txt,
#   choose_hermetic_art in decisions/mnm_society_decisions.txt (170,
#   171), the gain_effect of title_hermetics_apprentice (180), on_death
#   (182, 184), on_character_leave_society (184), verena.1 (200),
#   on_artifact_inheritance (230), on_society_bi_yearly_pulse (233, 250,
#   280), the decisions hermetics_write_theory_paper in
#   decisions/wh_cult_societies_decisions.txt and
#   decisions/mnm_hermetics_decisions.txt (300), hermetics_write_magnum_opus
#   in wh_cult_societies_decisions.txt (320) and
#   invite_hermetic_to_stargazing in mnm_hermetics_decisions.txt (400).
#   Triggers
#   in common/scripted_triggers/WHG_verena_triggers.txt, effects in
#   common/scripted_effects/WHG_verena_effects.txt, modifiers in
#   common/event_modifiers/WHG_verena_modifiers.txt.
# Saved: event targets hermetic_collaborator, apprentice_candidate,
#   sacrifice_for_science, ruin_site, temple_site, infiltration_target,
#   infiltration_companion, mnm_scrying_dead, new_holder_target,
#   old_holder_target, verena_omen_title, verena_omen_courtier,
#   horoscope_child, horoscope_maker and inspiring_adult; variables
#   laboratory_level, lab_ambition and horoscope_destiny_points; flags
#   building_laboratory, built_laboratory, hidden_lab,
#   very_hidden_lab, verena_library_<fitting> and
#   verena_library_next_<fitting> (scribes, lamps, bindings, books),
#   verena_lybaras_map, verena_lybaras_sought, do_not_disturb,
#   A/B/C/D_SUCCESS and _FAIL, infiltration_rival,
#   infiltration_mutual_dislike, infiltration_rich, bribed_guards,
#   guards_wake_up, distraction_failed, caught_stealing,
#   caught_destroying, great_theft, good_theft, ok_theft, bad_theft,
#   lab_destruction, lab_damage, lab_explosion_death; for the scrying
#   battle_focused_scrying_flag, relationship_focused_scrying_flag,
#   power_hungry_scrying_flag, used_ingredient_flag,
#   desc_variation_A/B/C_flag, positive/negative_reaction_to_scrying_flag,
#   omen_opt1/2, omen_opt_<good/bad>_<battle/relationship/power>,
#   verena_omen_child_gifted/dull, verena_omen_was_good/bad,
#   verena_omen_death_<lover/friend/rival/parent/child>, cat_death_omen,
#   dog_death_omen, was_granted_title_voluntarily; for the horoscope
#   destined_<steward/fighter/diplomat/schemer/scholar>,
#   encouraged/discouraged_horoscope_pattern, embraced/refused_destiny,
#   avoided_destiny, horoscope_maker_died, the stage flags
#   horoscope_destiny_revealed, horoscope_child_receives_horoscope,
#   horoscope_maker_receives_update,
#   horoscope_child_contemplates_their_destiny,
#   horoscope_parent_is_informed, horoscope_child_evaluate,
#   child_fulfilled/avoided_destiny_flag and the result flags
#   <skill>_1/2 and upgraded_<skill>; for the ingredients
#   verena_gather_companion, verena_gather_herbs/beasts/market, the
#   variables verena_gather_try and verena_gather_found, the event
#   targets potion_target (set by the decision) and verena_potion_maker,
#   the variable current_potion_quality, the flags preparing_potion,
#   potion_target_is_self, dealing_with_inherited_ingredients,
#   choosing_hermetic_art, owns_observatory_equipment,
#   completed_apprenticeship; for the stars the quest
#   quest_verena_watch_stars, verena_star_spouse, verena_star_child,
#   verena_star_friend, verena_star_councillor_1/2 and the flags
#   verena_star_fall_court and verena_star_fall_seen; for the symposium the
#   event targets great_minds_guest_1 - 4, arguer_1/2, the variables
#   collective_learning(_temp), the flags verena_symposium(_held),
#   verena_minor/major_scandal, disliked_hermetic_colleague,
#   war/prosperity/knowledge_invention, safe/risky_invention,
#   brilliant_insight, best/good/ok/failure/bad_failure_outcome and
#   invention_failure_*; for the invocation had_a_divine_summoning,
#   used_ingredients_for_summoning, divine_summoning_<field>,
#   divine_summoning_success/failure and verena_summon_<grade>; for the
#   paper the event targets peer1 - 5, the variable peer_review_score, the
#   flags theory_paper_<effort/quality>, verena_paper_<subject>,
#   peer_review_temp, discovered_knowledge_theft, refuted_paper_@<writer>,
#   paper_success/failure and verena_paper_endorsed_success/failure; for the
#   magnum opus wrote_magnum_opus (set by the decision) and
#   verena_opus_<book>; for the stars the event targets stargazing_guest and
#   stargazing_host, the variable stargazing_satisfaction and the flags
#   inviting_guests_to_stargazing_timer, do_not_disturb,
#   stargazing_good/bad_outcome and stargazing_become_friends.
# Dropped: the alchemist's laboratory of the vanilla Hermetic Society
#   (glass, crucibles and smiths' tools, now the fittings of a library),
#   the four fitting events DR_societies.1103 - 1106 (merged into
#   verena.13), the five finished-library events 1108 - 1112 (merged into
#   verena.15 and 16) and the hidden tier event 1107 (verena.14). The
#   Alexandria and Emerald Tablet option, commented out in the copy, is
#   back as the sealed archive of Lybaras; emerald_tablet is now the
#   Tablet of Lybaras. The raid wrecks the scriptorium but never burns a
#   book on purpose: the Order wept more for the books of Wolfenburg than
#   for its dead. The two newborn omens MNM.1204 and 1205 (merged into
#   verena.104, which no longer reads omens it had already removed), the
#   five death omens 1208 - 1212 (merged into verena.107, everyone close
#   to the dead is told, not one lover or friend), the unreachable
#   "other pet" option of 1214, the horoscope gating break of 1297
#   (verena.140, which no longer drops the chain when the parent dies
#   before the child turns thirteen) and 1299 (merged into verena.141).
#   The real-world planets and zodiac of the horoscope are the moons and
#   star signs of the Old World, mod-wide. The gathering no longer needs
#   a companion (the vanilla events failed without one, and checked the
#   gatherer's skill twice instead of the companion's); its dead quest ping
#   MNM.1801 - 1802 and quest_hermetic_gather_ingredients; the three
#   gathering chains MNM.1810 - 1822 (merged into verena.151 and 152); the
#   alliance with demons of the theurgy path (now Lorekeeper or
#   Scalebearer); the two star-fall endings MNM.1853 - 1854 (merged into
#   verena.226); the break chains of 1842, 1844 and 1848 and of 5108;
#   the hermetics gates of 1413, 1415, 1611 and 5110 (the mod has no
#   Hermetic Society, so they never fired); and the unread
#   small/medium/large_defense_against_dark_forces flags (now the modifier
#   verena_warded_chambers). Hibiscus, ginger root and lemon grass are
#   now faxtoryll, salwort and earth root, herbs of the Old World. The
#   vanilla gathering of minds MNM.1460 - 1481 is the symposium, built on
#   the Runemoot; the sixteen copies of the summoning roll (one per field
#   and learning band) are one roll per band; the break of the paper's
#   verdict and the stargazing check; the paper subject flags cleared
#   before the reviewers read them, the refusal flags left on reviewers
#   forever, the copied paper left unfinished when found out; and the six
#   magnum opus endings 1604 - 1609 (merged into verena.325). The
#   real-world planets, theurgy and zodiacs are the moons, the wandering
#   stars, the gods and the star signs; the Philosopher's Stone is
#   warpstone. MNM.1750, the new grandmaster, is society.95; the Shallya
#   tail is monastic.190 - 196.
###############################################################################

namespace = verena

###############################################################################
# Part 1: missions
###############################################################################

# verena.1 The missions of the Order
# Hidden. Every other year a member gets a mission from a brother or sister
# of the Order, a Lorekeeper of his realm or his own apprentice: find an
# apprentice, build a library, recover a lost text, raid the hoard of a
# Scrollbearer or watch the stars. The AI mostly settles its missions
# without the chain.
# Scope: a member of the Order of Mysteries (cult_of_verena).
character_event = {
	id = verena.1
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_society_bi_yearly_pulse

	is_in_society = yes

	trigger = {
		society_member_of = cult_of_verena
		has_any_quest = no
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = quest_cooldown_timer }
		is_inaccessible_trigger = no
		prisoner = no
		society = {
			any_society_member = {
				NOT = { character = ROOT }
				prisoner = no
				is_within_diplo_range = ROOT
				opinion = { who = ROOT value = 0 }
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		# Who gives the mission
		random_list = {
			20 = { # A member anywhere
				society = {
					random_society_member = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { character = ROOT }
							prisoner = no
							is_within_diplo_range = ROOT
							opinion = { who = ROOT value = 0 }
						}
						save_event_target_as = hermetic_collaborator
					}
				}
			}
			40 = { # A member of the realm
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					society = {
						NOT = {
							any_society_member = {
								NOT = { character = ROOT }
								prisoner = no
								is_within_diplo_range = ROOT
								opinion = { who = ROOT value = 0 }
								same_realm = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
				}
				society = {
					random_society_member = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { character = ROOT }
							prisoner = no
							is_within_diplo_range = ROOT
							opinion = { who = ROOT value = 0 }
							same_realm = ROOT
						}
						save_event_target_as = hermetic_collaborator
					}
				}
			}
			40 = { # The apprentice
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					NOT = {
						any_courtier_or_vassal = {
							has_minor_title = title_hermetics_apprentice
							prisoner = no
						}
					}
				}
				random_courtier_or_vassal = {
					limit = { has_minor_title = title_hermetics_apprentice prisoner = no }
					save_event_target_as = hermetic_collaborator
				}
			}
		}

		# The mission
		random_list = {
			20 = { # Find an apprentice
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					OR = {
						ai = yes
						any_courtier_or_vassal = { has_minor_title = title_hermetics_apprentice }
					}
				}
				event_target:hermetic_collaborator = {
					character_event = { id = verena.30 days = 7 random = 7 }
				}
			}
			20 = { # Build a library
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					OR = {
						has_character_flag = building_laboratory
						has_character_flag = built_laboratory
						is_landed = no
						AND = { ai = yes NOT = { society_rank == 4 } }
					}
				}
				event_target:hermetic_collaborator = {
					character_event = { id = verena.20 days = 7 random = 7 }
				}
			}
			15 = { # Recover a lost text
				modifier = { factor = 0 is_landed = no }
				event_target:hermetic_collaborator = {
					character_event = { id = verena.40 days = 7 random = 7 }
				}
			}
			10 = { # Raid the hoard of a Scrollbearer
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					society = {
						NOT = {
							any_society_member = {
								is_within_diplo_range = ROOT
								NOR = {
									character = ROOT
									character = event_target:hermetic_collaborator
									reverse_opinion = {
										who = event_target:hermetic_collaborator
										value = 20
									}
									reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 20 }
									has_character_flag = infiltration_rival
									has_character_flag = infiltration_mutual_dislike
									has_character_flag = infiltration_rich
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				event_target:hermetic_collaborator = {
					character_event = { id = verena.50 days = 7 random = 7 }
				}
			}
			10 = { # Watch the stars
				event_target:hermetic_collaborator = {
					character_event = { id = verena.200 days = 7 random = 7 }
				}
			}
			105 = { # The AI settles the mission by itself
				modifier = { factor = 0 ai = no }
				random_list = {
					70 = {
						modifier = { factor = 0.9 society_influence >= 30 }
						modifier = { factor = 0.9 society_influence >= 40 }
						modifier = { factor = 0.8 society_influence >= 50 }
						modifier = { factor = 0.7 society_influence >= 70 }
						modifier = { factor = 0.6 society_influence >= 80 }
						modifier = { factor = 0.5 society_influence >= 90 }
						add_mission_succeed_influence_effect = yes
					}
					30 = { add_mission_fail_influence_effect = yes }
				}
			}
			175 = { # Nothing this time
				modifier = { factor = 0 ai = no }
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Part 2: the library
###############################################################################

# verena.10 A house of learning
# The decision build_library is taken: the member may go on with the work
# or take his gold back.
# Scope: the member.
character_event = {
	id = verena.10
	desc = EVTDESCverena10
	picture = GFX_evt_castle_construction
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # decision build_library

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAverena10
		set_variable = { which = laboratory_level value = 1 }
		hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = verena.11 days = 7 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBverena10
		clr_character_flag = building_laboratory
		clr_character_flag = lab_kind_tzeentch
		clr_character_flag = lab_kind_skryre
		wealth = 50
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

# verena.11 Where the books shall sleep
# Hidden in the castle (safe, but a fire there hurts the household), open to
# every reader (efficient) or in a guarded house far away (for the cunning
# and the fearful; on an island when the capital has a port).
# Scope: the member.
character_event = {
	id = verena.11
	desc = EVTDESCverena11
	picture = GFX_evt_castle_construction
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_character_flag = building_laboratory
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAverena11 # In the castle
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_library_hidden_tt set_character_flag = hidden_lab }
		hidden_effect = {
			change_variable = { which = laboratory_level value = 2 }
			character_event = { id = verena.12 days = 30 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBverena11 # Open to all
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_library_open_tt }
		hidden_effect = {
			change_variable = { which = laboratory_level value = 5 }
			character_event = { id = verena.12 days = 30 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = charitable }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = paranoid }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = {
			trigger = { capital_scope = { port = yes } }
			text = EVTOPTCverena11 # An island
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { capital_scope = { port = no } }
			text = EVTOPTDverena11 # A remote house
		}
		trigger = { OR = { intrigue = 15 trait = paranoid } }
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_library_remote_tt set_character_flag = very_hidden_lab }
		hidden_effect = {
			change_variable = { which = laboratory_level value = 3 }
			character_event = { id = verena.12 days = 30 }
		}
	}
}

# verena.12 How great a library
# A reading room, a scriptorium, a hall of learning or a library to rival
# the Grand Temple of Nuln. The greater the plan, the more gold, learning
# and fittings it takes (one to four of them, verena.13).
# Scope: the member.
character_event = {
	id = verena.12
	desc = EVTDESCverena12
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lab_kind_skryre }
		picture = GFX_evt_warpstone_vein
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = lab_kind_skryre } }
		picture = GFX_evt_library
	}
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAverena12 # A reading room
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_library_small_tt }
		hidden_effect = {
			change_variable = { which = laboratory_level value = 1 }
			set_variable = { which = lab_ambition value = 1 }
			verena_library_start_effect = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBverena12 # A scriptorium
		wealth = -20
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_library_medium_tt }
		hidden_effect = {
			change_variable = { which = laboratory_level value = 3 }
			set_variable = { which = lab_ambition value = 2 }
			verena_library_start_effect = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCverena12 # A hall of learning
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				learning = 15
				AND = {
					learning = 12
					any_courtier_or_vassal = {
						has_minor_title = title_hermetics_apprentice
						learning = 12
					}
				}
			}
		}
		wealth = -35
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_library_large_tt }
		hidden_effect = {
			change_variable = { which = laboratory_level value = 5 }
			set_variable = { which = lab_ambition value = 3 }
			verena_library_start_effect = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDverena12 # To rival Nuln
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				learning = 25
				AND = {
					learning = 15
					any_courtier_or_vassal = {
						has_minor_title = title_hermetics_apprentice
						learning = 15
					}
				}
			}
		}
		wealth = -50
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_library_grand_tt }
		hidden_effect = {
			change_variable = { which = laboratory_level value = 8 }
			set_variable = { which = lab_ambition value = 4 }
			verena_library_start_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

# verena.13 The fittings of the library
# One of the four fittings the plan still lacks: the copyists, the lamps,
# the bindings or the books themselves. Making do is cheap; the best costs
# gold and makes a better library. Fires itself again until every fitting
# of the plan is chosen, then the library is finished (verena.14).
# Scope: the member.
character_event = {
	id = verena.13
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_library_next_scribes }
		text = EVTDESCverena13_scribes
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_library_next_lamps }
		text = EVTDESCverena13_lamps
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_library_next_bindings }
		text = EVTDESCverena13_bindings
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_library_next_books }
		text = EVTDESCverena13_books
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lab_kind_skryre }
		picture = GFX_evt_warpstone_vein
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = lab_kind_skryre } }
		picture = GFX_evt_library
	}
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = {
			trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_library_next_scribes }
			text = EVTOPTAverena13_scribes
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_library_next_lamps }
			text = EVTOPTAverena13_lamps
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_library_next_bindings }
			text = EVTOPTAverena13_bindings
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_library_next_books }
			text = EVTOPTAverena13_books
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_library_cheap_tt }
		hidden_effect = {
			change_variable = { which = laboratory_level value = 1 }
			verena_library_fitting_done_effect = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 modifier = { factor = 2 trait = greedy } }
	}
	option = {
		name = {
			trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_library_next_scribes }
			text = EVTOPTBverena13_scribes
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_library_next_lamps }
			text = EVTOPTBverena13_lamps

			trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_library_next_books }
			text = EVTOPTBverena13_books
		}
		wealth = -20
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_library_fine_tt }
		hidden_effect = {
			change_variable = { which = laboratory_level value = 2 }
			verena_library_fitting_done_effect = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { wealth = 60 } } }
	}
}

# verena.14 The library is finished
# Hidden. A learned apprentice adds one level to the library. The level
# decides the library the member gets (verena.15 or verena.16).
# Scope: the member.
character_event = {
	id = verena.14
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = built_laboratory
		clr_character_flag = building_laboratory
		verena_library_clear_effect = yes
		# The Changer rewards a library of forbidden lore.
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = lab_kind_tzeentch
				society_member_of = cult_of_tzeentch
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = laboratory_level value = 18 } }
				add_society_currency_major_effect = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = laboratory_level value = 12 } }
				add_society_currency_medium_effect = yes
			}
			else = { add_society_currency_minor_effect = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_courtier_or_vassal = {
					has_minor_title = title_hermetics_apprentice
					learning = 12
				}
			}
			change_variable = { which = laboratory_level value = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = laboratory_level value = 18 } }
			narrative_event = { id = verena.16 }
		}
		else = { character_event = { id = verena.15 } }
	}
}

# verena.15 A library of my own
# Level below 6: a simple library; below 12: a library; else an impressive
# one, each with knowledge for the realm.
# Scope: the member.
character_event = {
	id = verena.15
	desc = {
		trigger = { NOT = { check_variable = { which = laboratory_level value = 6 } } }
		text = EVTDESCverena15_simple
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = laboratory_level value = 6 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = laboratory_level value = 12 } }
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena15_normal
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = laboratory_level value = 12 } }
		text = EVTDESCverena15_good
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lab_kind_skryre }
		picture = GFX_evt_warpstone_vein
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = lab_kind_skryre } }
		picture = GFX_evt_library
	}
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = {
			trigger = { NOT = { check_variable = { which = laboratory_level value = 6 } } }
			text = EVTOPTAverena15_simple
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { check_variable = { which = laboratory_level value = 6 } }
			text = EVTOPTAverena15
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = lab_kind_skryre }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { which = laboratory_level value = 6 } } }
				add_character_modifier = { name = skaven_warplab_1 duration = -1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { which = laboratory_level value = 12 } } }
				military_techpoints = 5
				add_character_modifier = { name = skaven_warplab_2 duration = -1 }
			}
			else = {
				military_techpoints = 15
				add_character_modifier = { name = skaven_warplab_3 duration = -1 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { which = laboratory_level value = 6 } } }
			add_character_modifier = { name = cheap_laboratory duration = -1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { which = laboratory_level value = 12 } } }
			economy_techpoints = 5
			add_character_modifier = { name = normal_laboratory duration = -1 }
		}
		else = {
			economy_techpoints = 15
			add_character_modifier = { name = good_laboratory duration = -1 }
		}
		verena_library_reward_effect = yes
	}
}

# verena.16 The house of learning
# Level 18 or more: a grand library; 23 or more: a famous one, spoken of
# in Nuln and Altdorf.
# Scope: the member.
narrative_event = {
	id = verena.16
	title = {
		trigger = { NOT = { check_variable = { which = laboratory_level value = 23 } } }
		text = EVTNAMEverena16_great
	}
	title = {
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = laboratory_level value = 23 } }
		text = EVTNAMEverena16_best
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { NOT = { check_variable = { which = laboratory_level value = 23 } } }
		text = EVTDESCverena16_great
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = laboratory_level value = 23 } }
		text = EVTDESCverena16_best
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lab_kind_skryre }
		picture = GFX_evt_warpstone_vein
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = lab_kind_skryre } }
		picture = GFX_evt_library
	}
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = {
			trigger = { NOT = { check_variable = { which = laboratory_level value = 23 } } }
			text = EVTOPTAverena16_great
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { check_variable = { which = laboratory_level value = 23 } }
			text = EVTOPTAverena16_best
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = lab_kind_skryre }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { which = laboratory_level value = 23 } } }
				military_techpoints = 30
				add_character_modifier = { name = skaven_warplab_4 duration = -1 }
			}
			else = {
				military_techpoints = 50
				add_character_modifier = { name = skaven_warplab_5 duration = -1 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { which = laboratory_level value = 23 } } }
			economy_techpoints = 30
			add_character_modifier = { name = great_laboratory duration = -1 }
		}
		else = {
			economy_techpoints = 50
			add_character_modifier = { name = best_laboratory duration = -1 }
		}
		verena_library_reward_effect = yes
	}
}

# verena.21 Mission: build a library

# Scope: ROOT is the member, FROM the mission giver.
society_quest_event = {
	id = verena.21
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { NOT = { has_minor_title = title_hermetics_apprentice } } }
		text = EVTDESCverena21
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_minor_title = title_hermetics_apprentice } }
		text = EVTDESCverena21_apprentice
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAverena21
		set_quest = quest_verena_build_library
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBverena21
		custom_tooltip = { text = decline_quest_tooltip }
		add_character_modifier = { name = quest_cooldown_timer hidden = yes days = 1000 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

# verena.22 Mission: the library stands
# The quest is done; a better library brings more favor of the Order.
# Scope: the member.
character_event = {
	id = verena.22
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				has_character_modifier = good_laboratory
				has_character_modifier = great_laboratory
				has_character_modifier = best_laboratory
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena22_normal
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_character_modifier = good_laboratory
				has_character_modifier = great_laboratory
				has_character_modifier = best_laboratory
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena22_rich
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_library
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_quest = quest_verena_build_library
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAverena22
		clr_quest = quest_verena_build_library
		sound_effect = hermetic_society_perform_experiment
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = best_laboratory }
			add_society_currency_major_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_character_modifier = good_laboratory
					has_character_modifier = great_laboratory
				}
			}
			add_society_currency_medium_effect = yes
		}
		else = { add_society_currency_minor_effect = yes }
	}
}

# verena.31 Mission: find an apprentice
# A brother boasts of his own apprentice. If the member names none within
# half a year, a bright servant is found for him (verena.32).
# Scope: ROOT is the member, FROM the mission giver.
society_quest_event = {
	id = verena.31
	desc = EVTDESCverena31

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAverena31
		set_quest = quest_verena_find_apprentice
		hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = verena.32 days = 150 random = 50 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBverena31
		custom_tooltip = { text = decline_quest_tooltip }
		add_character_modifier = { name = quest_cooldown_timer hidden = yes days = 1000 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

# verena.32 A servant with a quick mind
# Hidden. Still no apprentice: a young servant of the member's faith and
# culture turns up with a bright mind and a good trait (never a woman
# among races without women).
# Scope: the member.
character_event = {
	id = verena.32
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_quest = quest_verena_find_apprentice
	}

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			50 = { # A woman
				modifier = { factor = 0 is_no_females_race = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 is_female = no }
				create_random_priest = {
					random_traits = yes
					dynasty = none
					culture = ROOT
					religion = ROOT
					female = yes
					age = 24
				}
				new_character = {
					character_event = { id = aiclean.31 } # the race trait
					save_event_target_as = apprentice_candidate
				}
			}
			50 = { # A man
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 is_woman_trigger = yes }
				create_random_priest = {
					random_traits = yes
					dynasty = none
					culture = ROOT
					religion = ROOT
					female = no
					age = 24
				}
				new_character = {
					character_event = { id = aiclean.31 } # the race trait
					save_event_target_as = apprentice_candidate
				}
			}
		}
		event_target:apprentice_candidate = {
			remove_trait = slow
			remove_trait = imbecile
			remove_trait = inbred
			remove_trait = dull
			remove_trait = zealous
			remove_trait = cynical
			remove_trait = genius
			remove_trait = quick
			remove_trait = shrewd
			remove_trait = slothful
			random_list = { # A gift of the mind
				10 = { add_trait = genius }
				10 = { add_trait = quick }
				10 = { add_trait = shrewd }
				10 = { add_trait = scholar }
				10 = { add_trait = mystic }
				10 = { add_trait = gardener }
				10 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { has_dlc = "Reapers" } }
					add_trait = physician
				}
				40 = { }
			}
			random_list = { # A virtue of the scholar
				10 = { add_trait = erudite }
				10 = { add_trait = diligent }
				10 = { remove_trait = wroth add_trait = patient }
				10 = { remove_trait = lustful add_trait = chaste }
				30 = { remove_trait = content add_trait = ambitious }
			}
			while = { limit = { NOT = { learning = 10 } } change_learning = 1 }
			character_event = { id = verena.33 }
		}
	}
}

# verena.42 The letter (ping)

# Scope: ROOT is the mission giver, FROM the member.

	is_triggered_only = yes

		FROM = { letter_event = { id = verena.43 } }

# verena.43 Where shall we look?
# A tablet in foreign ruins (for players: Nehekhara, Araby or the Border
# Princes), a scroll in a temple of the realm, a tome at a local
# antiquarian: the further, the costlier, the rarer and the more favor.
# Once in a member's life, while no one holds it, the giver may have a
# map to a sealed archive of Lybaras, the city of scholars, where the
# Tablet of Lybaras lies; few who go come back.
# Scope: ROOT is the member, FROM the mission giver.
letter_event = {
	id = verena.43
	desc = {
		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = verena_lybaras_map } }
		text = EVTDESCverena43
	}
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_lybaras_map } text = EVTDESCverena43_lybaras }
	border = GFX_event_letter_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_quest = quest_verena_find_text
	}

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = do_not_disturb
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = verena_lybaras_sought }
				NOT = { any_character = { has_artifact = emerald_tablet } }
			}
			random = {
				chance = 10
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 12 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 16 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 20 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { learning = 16 } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { learning = 20 } }
				set_character_flag = verena_lybaras_map
			}
		}
		# The places
		random_independent_ruler = {
			limit = {
				is_within_diplo_range = ROOT
				NOT = { same_realm = ROOT }
				any_realm_province = { is_capital = no }
			}
			random_realm_province = { limit = { is_capital = no } save_event_target_as = ruin_site }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { ai = no }
			random_independent_ruler = {
				limit = {
					is_within_diplo_range = ROOT
					NOT = { same_realm = ROOT }
					any_realm_title = {
						tier = DUCHY
						OR = {
							region = world_nehekhara
							region = world_greater_araby_araby
							region = world_old_world_border_princes
						}
					}
				}
				random_realm_province = { save_event_target_as = ruin_site }
			}
		}
		random_realm_province = { save_event_target_as = temple_site }
		random_realm_province = {
			limit = { is_capital = no any_province_holding = { holding_type = temple } }
			save_event_target_as = temple_site
		}
	}

	option = {

			20 = {
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 8 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 11 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 14 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 17 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 20 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { learning = 8 } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { learning = 11 } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { learning = 14 } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { learning = 17 } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { learning = 20 } }
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = EVTOPTXverena43_SUCCESS

					}

				tooltip = {
					add_artifact = emerald_tablet
					add_society_currency_massive_effect = yes
				}
			}

					FROM = {
						set_character_flag = A_FAIL
						character_event = { id = verena.44 days = 300 random = 150 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = ambitious }
			modifier = { factor = 0.2 trait = craven }
		}
	}
	# The tablet in the ruins
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBverena43
		trigger = { OR = { ai = no scaled_wealth = 0.5 wealth = 100 } }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBverena43_TT }
		scaled_wealth = { value = -0.5 min = -100 }

				modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 14 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 17 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 20 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { learning = 8 } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { learning = 11 } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { learning = 14 } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { learning = 17 } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { learning = 20 } }
				custom_tooltip = {

	# The scroll in the temple
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCverena43
		trigger = { OR = { ai = no scaled_wealth = 0.2 wealth = 40 } }

# verena.44 The end of the search
# Hidden. The outcome flag on the mission giver picks the event the member
# gets; the flags are cleared afterwards.
# Scope: ROOT is the mission giver, FROM the member.
character_event = {
	id = verena.44
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_quest = quest_verena_find_text } }
			trigger_switch = {
				on_trigger = has_character_flag
				A_SUCCESS = { FROM = { narrative_event = { id = verena.45 } } }
				B_SUCCESS = { FROM = { character_event = { id = verena.46 } } }
				C_SUCCESS = { FROM = { character_event = { id = verena.47 } } }
				D_SUCCESS = { FROM = { character_event = { id = verena.48 } } }
				A_FAIL = { FROM = { character_event = { id = verena.49 } } }
				B_FAIL = { FROM = { character_event = { id = verena.49 } } }
				C_FAIL = { FROM = { character_event = { id = verena.49 } } }
				D_FAIL = { FROM = { character_event = { id = verena.49 } } }
			}
		}
		else = {
			clr_character_flag = A_FAIL
			clr_character_flag = B_FAIL
			clr_character_flag = C_FAIL
			clr_character_flag = D_FAIL
		}
		clr_character_flag = A_SUCCESS
		clr_character_flag = B_SUCCESS
		clr_character_flag = C_SUCCESS
		clr_character_flag = D_SUCCESS
		FROM = { clr_character_flag = verena_lybaras_map clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb }
	}
}

# verena.45 The Tablet of Lybaras
# The sealed archive is found and the Tablet of Lybaras (emerald_tablet)
# is carried out of the desert, if no one holds it yet; the Order's
# greatest favor and a bond with the giver.
# Scope: ROOT is the member, FROM the mission giver.
narrative_event = {
	id = verena.45
	title = EVTNAMEverena45
	desc = EVTDESCverena45
	picture = GFX_evt_religious_exultation
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAverena45
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { any_character = { has_artifact = emerald_tablet } } }
			add_artifact = emerald_tablet
		}
		clr_quest = quest_verena_find_text
		sound_effect = hermetic_society_recover_scroll
		add_society_currency_massive_effect = yes
		hidden_effect = { opinion = { who = FROM modifier = epic_adventure years = 30 } }
		reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = epic_adventure years = 30 }
	}
}

# verena.49 Nothing but dust

		picture = GFX_evt_desert
	}

		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = B_FAIL } }
		text = EVTDESCverena49_B
		picture = GFX_evt_pagan
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = C_FAIL } }
		text = EVTDESCverena49_C
		picture = GFX_evt_bishop
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = D_FAIL } }
		text = EVTDESCverena49_D
		picture = GFX_evt_market
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_desert
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

		name = CURSES
		clr_quest = { id = quest_verena_find_text failure = yes }

			add_rival = FROM
		}
		FROM = {
			clr_character_flag = A_FAIL
			clr_character_flag = B_FAIL
			clr_character_flag = C_FAIL
			clr_character_flag = D_FAIL
		}
	}

###############################################################################
# Part 5: the Scrollbearer's hoard
###############################################################################

# verena.50 Mission: raid a Scrollbearer's hoard (ping)
# Hidden. The mission giver writes to the member.
# Scope: ROOT is the mission giver, FROM the member.
character_event = {
	id = verena.50
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { society_quest_event = { id = verena.51 days = 7 random = 7 } }
	}
}

# verena.51 Mission: raid a Scrollbearer's hoard
# A brother of the Order names a member who hoards books like the
# Scrollbearers and shares nothing: the giver's rival, someone both of
# them dislike or a rich hoarder. Accepting makes the giver the member's
# companion for the break-in.
# Scope: ROOT is the member, FROM the mission giver.
society_quest_event = {
	id = verena.51
	desc = {
		trigger = { event_target:infiltration_target = { has_character_flag = infiltration_rival } }
		text = EVTDESCverena51_rival
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:infiltration_target = { has_character_flag = infiltration_mutual_dislike }
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena51_dislike
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { event_target:infiltration_target = { has_character_flag = infiltration_rich } }
		text = EVTDESCverena51_rich
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	quest_target = event_target:infiltration_target

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			30 = { # The giver's rival
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					society = {
						NOT = {
							any_society_member = {
								verena_infiltration_free_trigger = yes
								is_rival = FROM
							}
						}
					}
				}
				society = {
					random_society_member = {
						limit = { verena_infiltration_free_trigger = yes is_rival = FROM }
						save_event_target_as = infiltration_target
						set_character_flag = infiltration_rival
					}
				}
			}
			10 = { # Disliked by both
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					society = {
						NOT = {
							any_society_member = {
								verena_infiltration_free_trigger = yes
								NOT = { reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 0 } }
								NOT = { reverse_opinion = { who = FROM value = 0 } }
							}
						}
					}
				}
				society = {
					random_society_member = {
						limit = {
							verena_infiltration_free_trigger = yes
							NOT = { reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 0 } }
							NOT = { reverse_opinion = { who = FROM value = 0 } }
						}
						save_event_target_as = infiltration_target
						set_character_flag = infiltration_mutual_dislike
					}
				}
			}
			10 = { # A rich hoard
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					society = {
						NOT = {
							any_society_member = {
								verena_infiltration_free_trigger = yes
								NOT = { reverse_opinion = { who = FROM value = 20 } }
								verena_rich_hoard_trigger = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
				modifier = { factor = 2 OR = { trait = greedy trait = ambitious } }
				society = {
					random_society_member = {
						limit = {
							verena_infiltration_free_trigger = yes
							NOT = { reverse_opinion = { who = FROM value = 20 } }
							verena_rich_hoard_trigger = yes
						}
						save_event_target_as = infiltration_target
						set_character_flag = infiltration_rich
					}
				}
			}
			1 = { # Anyone
				society = {
					random_society_member = {
						limit = {
							verena_infiltration_free_trigger = yes
							NOT = { reverse_opinion = { who = FROM value = 20 } }
							NOT = { character = FROM }
						}
						save_event_target_as = infiltration_target
						set_character_flag = infiltration_rich
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAverena51
		event_target:infiltration_target = {
			set_quest_target = { id = quest_verena_infiltrate_library holder = ROOT }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = {
				save_event_target_as = infiltration_companion
				opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_infiltration_companion months = 100 }
				character_event = { id = verena.52 days = 14 }
			}
			event_target:infiltration_target = { character_event = { id = verena.76 days = 150 } }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBverena51
		custom_tooltip = { text = decline_quest_tooltip }
		add_character_modifier = { name = quest_cooldown_timer hidden = yes days = 1000 }
		hidden_effect = {
			event_target:infiltration_target = { character_event = { id = verena.76 } }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

# verena.52 Find the hoard (router)
# Hidden. A hidden or remote library must be found first (verena.53), an
# open one is entered at once (verena.55).
# Scope: ROOT is the companion, FROM the member.
character_event = {
	id = verena.52
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:infiltration_target = {
					OR = { has_character_flag = hidden_lab has_character_flag = very_hidden_lab }
				}
			}
			FROM = { letter_event = { id = verena.53 } }
		}
		else = { FROM = { character_event = { id = verena.55 } } }
	}
}

# verena.53 The companion's spies
# The companion's spies look for the hidden library; the better his
# intrigue, the sooner, the better hidden, the later.
# Scope: ROOT is the member, FROM the companion.
letter_event = {
	id = verena.53
	desc = EVTDESCverena53
	border = GFX_event_letter_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAverena53
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAverena53_TT }
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				33 = { # Quickly
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { intrigue = 12 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { intrigue = 14 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { intrigue = 16 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { intrigue = 18 } }
					FROM = { character_event = { id = verena.54 days = 15 random = 10 } }
				}
				33 = { # Slowly
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						event_target:infiltration_target = { has_character_flag = hidden_lab }
					}
					modifier = { factor = 1.1 FROM = { intrigue = 12 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.1 FROM = { intrigue = 14 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.1 FROM = { intrigue = 16 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.1 FROM = { intrigue = 18 } }
					FROM = { character_event = { id = verena.54 days = 45 random = 30 } }
				}
				33 = { # Very slowly
					modifier = {
						factor = 2
						event_target:infiltration_target = { has_character_flag = very_hidden_lab }
					}
					FROM = { character_event = { id = verena.54 days = 90 random = 45 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# verena.54 The hoard is found (ping)
# Hidden. Scope: ROOT is the companion, FROM the member.
character_event = {
	id = verena.54
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { has_quest = quest_verena_infiltrate_library }

	}
}

# verena.55 The Knights at the door
# The hoarder has hired guards, sometimes a Knight of the Scroll. Bribe
# them (diplomacy 12; dearer for a hidden library), drug them with a
# sleeping draught (learning), have the companion lure them away (his
# intrigue) or turn back. A half-failed draught or distraction lets the
# raid go on but the guards come back in time (flags guards_wake_up,
# distraction_failed).
# Scope: ROOT is the member, FROM the companion.
character_event = {
	id = verena.55
	desc = {
		trigger = { event_target:infiltration_target = { has_character_flag = very_hidden_lab } }
		text = EVTDESCverena55_remote
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { event_target:infiltration_target = { has_character_flag = hidden_lab } }
		text = EVTDESCverena55_hidden
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:infiltration_target = {
				NOR = { has_character_flag = very_hidden_lab has_character_flag = hidden_lab }
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena55_open
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_eavesdropping
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_quest = quest_verena_infiltrate_library
	}

	# Bribe
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAverena55
		trigger = { OR = { ai = no scaled_wealth = 0.5 } diplomacy = 12 }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAverena55_TT }
		tooltip_info = diplomacy
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:infiltration_target = { has_character_flag = very_hidden_lab } }
			scaled_wealth = { value = -0.75 min = -75 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { event_target:infiltration_target = { has_character_flag = hidden_lab } }
			scaled_wealth = { value = -0.5 min = -50 }
		}
		else = { scaled_wealth = { value = -0.3 min = -30 } }
		set_character_flag = bribed_guards
		character_event = { id = verena.56 days = 7 }
	}
	# The sleeping draught
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBverena55
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBverena55_TT }
		tooltip_info = learning
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 10 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 12 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 14 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 16 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 18 }
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = EVTOPTXverena55_SUCCESS_TT
					character_event = { id = verena.56 days = 7 }
				}
			}
			50 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.2
					event_target:infiltration_target = { has_character_flag = hidden_lab }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.5
					event_target:infiltration_target = { has_character_flag = very_hidden_lab }
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = EVTOPTXverena55_FAIL_TT
					hidden_effect = {
						random_list = {
							50 = { # They wake too early
								set_character_flag = guards_wake_up
								character_event = { id = verena.56 days = 7 }
							}
							50 = { # They never fall asleep
								character_event = { id = verena.57 days = 7 }
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	# The distraction
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCverena55
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTCverena55_TT }
		random_list = {
			60 = {
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { intrigue = 10 } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { intrigue = 12 } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { intrigue = 14 } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { intrigue = 16 } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { intrigue = 18 } }
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = EVTOPTXverena55_SUCCESS_TT
					character_event = { id = verena.58 days = 7 }
				}
			}
			40 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.2
					event_target:infiltration_target = { has_character_flag = hidden_lab }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.5
					event_target:infiltration_target = { has_character_flag = very_hidden_lab }
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = EVTOPTXverena55_FAIL_TT
					hidden_effect = {
						random_list = {
							50 = { # They come back too early
								set_character_flag = distraction_failed
								character_event = { id = verena.58 days = 7 }
							}
							50 = { # Nobody believes it
								character_event = { id = verena.59 days = 7 }
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	# Turn back
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDverena55
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTDverena55_TT }
		clr_quest = { id = quest_verena_infiltrate_library failure = yes }
		reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_very_disappointed years = 10 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 0.2 }
	}
}

# verena.56 Inside the hoard
# The guards are bought or asleep. Split up (one carries off books, one
# wrecks the scriptorium; the companion's share is verena.70 or 72) or
# stay together. Guards that wake up catch the member (verena.60).
# Scope: the member.
character_event = {
	id = verena.56
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = bribed_guards }
		text = EVTDESCverena56_bribed
		picture = GFX_evt_shady_meeting
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = bribed_guards } }
		text = EVTDESCverena56_asleep
		picture = GFX_evt_drunk
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_drunk
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_quest = quest_verena_infiltrate_library
	}

	# You wreck, I take
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAverena56
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAverena56_TT }
		hidden_effect = {
			verena_raid_steal_effect = yes
			if = {
				limit = { verena_raid_detected_trigger = no }
				event_target:infiltration_companion = {
					character_event = { id = verena.72 days = 14 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	# I wreck, you take
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBverena56
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAverena56_TT }
		hidden_effect = {
			verena_raid_wreck_effect = yes
			if = {
				limit = { verena_raid_detected_trigger = no }
				event_target:infiltration_companion = {
					character_event = { id = verena.70 days = 14 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	# We take
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCverena56
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTCverena56_TT }
		hidden_effect = { verena_raid_steal_effect = yes }
	}
	# We wreck
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDverena56
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTCverena56_TT }
		hidden_effect = { verena_raid_wreck_effect = yes }
	}

	after = {
		clr_character_flag = bribed_guards
	}
}

# verena.60 Caught
# The guards catch the member (and an AI companion) taking or wrecking, or
# the companion's clumsy theft gives them away (flag bad_theft). The
# hoarder imprisons them and decides their fate (verena.61).
# Scope: ROOT is the member, FROM the member or the companion.
character_event = {
	id = verena.60
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = guards_wake_up has_character_flag = caught_destroying }
		text = EVTDESCverena60_A
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = distraction_failed
					has_character_flag = caught_destroying
				}
				FROM = { has_character_flag = bad_theft }
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena60_B
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = guards_wake_up has_character_flag = caught_stealing }
		text = EVTDESCverena60_C
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = distraction_failed has_character_flag = caught_stealing }
		text = EVTDESCverena60_D
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_into_the_dungeon
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_quest = quest_verena_infiltrate_library
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAverena60
		event_target:infiltration_target = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_landed = yes }
				ROOT = { imprison = PREV }
				if = {
					limit = { event_target:infiltration_companion = { ai = yes } }
					event_target:infiltration_companion = { imprison = PREV }
				}
			}
			else = {
				host = {
					ROOT = { imprison = PREV }
					if = {
						limit = { event_target:infiltration_companion = { ai = yes } }
						event_target:infiltration_companion = { imprison = PREV }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		reverse_opinion = {
			who = event_target:infiltration_target
			modifier = opinion_thief
			years = 20
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			event_target:infiltration_companion = {
				reverse_opinion = {
					who = event_target:infiltration_target
					modifier = opinion_thief
					years = 20
				}
			}
			clr_character_flag = distraction_failed
			clr_character_flag = guards_wake_up
			clr_character_flag = caught_stealing
			clr_character_flag = caught_destroying
			FROM = { clr_character_flag = bad_theft }
		}
		clr_quest = { id = quest_verena_infiltrate_library failure = yes }
		hidden_effect = {
			event_target:infiltration_target = { character_event = { id = verena.61 days = 7 } }
			verena_raid_companion_done_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

# verena.61 Thieves in my library
# The hoarder may offer the thief freedom for his secrets (a ransom of the
# Order's favor) or let him rot.
# Scope: ROOT is the hoarder, FROM the member.
character_event = {
	id = verena.61
	desc = EVTDESCverena61
	picture = GFX_evt_catching_heretic
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	# Secrets for freedom
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAverena61
		trigger = { FROM = { has_society_currency_medium_trigger = yes prisoner = yes } }
		tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				60 = {
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = craven } }
					add_society_currency_major_effect = yes
				}
				40 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 2
						FROM = {
							OR = { trait = greedy trait = ambitious trait = wroth trait = paranoid }
						}
					}
					custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAverena61_FAIL_TT }
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = { FROM = { character_event = { id = verena.62 } } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 60
			modifier = { factor = 2 OR = { trait = greedy trait = ambitious learning = 14 } }
		}
	}
	# Let them rot
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBverena61
		if = { limit = { NOT = { is_rival = FROM } } tooltip = { add_rival = FROM } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:infiltration_companion = { always = yes }
				NOT = { is_rival = event_target:infiltration_companion }
			}
			tooltip = { add_rival = event_target:infiltration_companion }
		}
		hidden_effect = { FROM = { character_event = { id = verena.65 } } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 40 modifier = { factor = 2 is_rival = FROM } }
	}
}

# verena.66 The hoard is opened
# The member carries off a book of the Order (a great theft, not against a
# played hoarder so often), the hoarder's ingredients (a good one) or a
# purse (an ordinary one), with gold and the Order's favor. Then the
# hoarder learns of it (verena.67) and the member gets away (verena.75).
# Scope: the member.
character_event = {
	id = verena.66
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = great_theft } text = EVTDESCverena66_great }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = good_theft } text = EVTDESCverena66_good }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = ok_theft } text = EVTDESCverena66_ok }
	picture = GFX_evt_library
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	hide_from = yes

	trigger = {
		has_quest = quest_verena_infiltrate_library
	}

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			33 = { # A book of the Order
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					event_target:infiltration_target = {
						NOT = { any_artifact = { verena_stealable_text_trigger = yes } }
					}
				}
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 12 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 13 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 16 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 18 }
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 event_target:infiltration_target = { ai = no } }
				set_character_flag = great_theft
			}
			33 = { # Ingredients
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					event_target:infiltration_target = {
						NOT = { any_artifact = { has_artifact_flag = ingredient } }
					}
				}
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 10 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.1 learning = 12 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.1 learning = 13 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.1 learning = 16 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.1 learning = 18 }
				set_character_flag = good_theft
			}
			33 = { # A purse
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.2
					event_target:infiltration_target = { has_character_flag = hidden_lab }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.5
					event_target:infiltration_target = { has_character_flag = very_hidden_lab }
				}
				set_character_flag = ok_theft
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = { trigger = { has_character_flag = great_theft } text = EVTOPTAverena66_great }
		name = { trigger = { has_character_flag = good_theft } text = EVTOPTAverena66_good }
		name = { trigger = { has_character_flag = ok_theft } text = EVTOPTAverena66_ok }
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = great_theft }
			custom_tooltip = { text = verena_raid_steal_text_tt }
			if = {
				limit = {
					event_target:infiltration_target = {
						any_artifact = { has_artifact_flag = ingredient }
					}
				}
				custom_tooltip = { text = verena_raid_steal_ingredient_tt }
			}
			transfer_scaled_wealth = {
				from = event_target:infiltration_target
				value = 0.5
				min = 50
			}
			add_society_currency_massive_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = good_theft }
			custom_tooltip = { text = verena_raid_steal_ingredient_tt }
			transfer_scaled_wealth = {
				from = event_target:infiltration_target
				value = 0.25
				min = 25
			}
			add_society_currency_major_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			transfer_scaled_wealth = {
				from = event_target:infiltration_target
				value = 0.1
				min = 10
			}
			add_society_currency_medium_effect = yes
		}
	}

	after = {
		hidden_effect = {
			event_target:infiltration_target = { character_event = { id = verena.67 } }
			character_event = { id = verena.75 days = 40 }
		}
	}
}

# verena.67 My books are gone
# The hoarder learns of the theft: the book and an ingredient pass to the
# thief.
# Scope: ROOT is the hoarder, FROM the member.
character_event = {
	id = verena.67
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = great_theft } }
		text = EVTDESCverena67_great
	}
	desc = { trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = good_theft } } text = EVTDESCverena67_good }
	desc = { trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = ok_theft } } text = EVTDESCverena67_ok }
	picture = GFX_evt_burning_house
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = CURSES
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = great_theft } }
			random_artifact = {
				limit = { verena_stealable_text_trigger = yes }
				tooltip = { destroy_artifact = yes }
				hidden_effect = { transfer_artifact = { from = ROOT to = FROM } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = { OR = { has_character_flag = great_theft has_character_flag = good_theft } }
				any_artifact = { has_artifact_flag = ingredient }
			}
			random_artifact = {
				limit = { has_artifact_flag = ingredient }
				tooltip = { destroy_artifact = yes }
				hidden_effect = { transfer_artifact = { from = ROOT to = FROM } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = great_theft } }
			tooltip = { scaled_wealth = { value = -0.5 min = -50 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = good_theft } }
			tooltip = { scaled_wealth = { value = -0.25 min = -25 } }
		}
		else = { tooltip = { scaled_wealth = { value = -0.1 min = -10 } } }
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = {
				clr_character_flag = great_theft
				clr_character_flag = good_theft
				clr_character_flag = ok_theft
			}
		}
	}
}

# verena.68 The scriptorium is wrecked
# No book is burned: the member wrecks the hoarder's scriptorium, scatters
# his catalogue and spoils his work. With a library built, it may be ruined
# outright (the library is lost); else damaged (gold and ingredients); and
# a clumsy member may die when the lamp oil catches. Then the hoarder
# learns of it (verena.69) and the member gets away (verena.75).
# Scope: the member.
character_event = {
	id = verena.68
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = lab_destruction } text = EVTDESCverena68_ruin }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = lab_damage } text = EVTDESCverena68_damage }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = lab_explosion_death } text = EVTDESCverena68_death }
	picture = GFX_evt_burning_house
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	hide_from = yes

	trigger = {
		has_quest = quest_verena_infiltrate_library
	}

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			40 = { # Ruin
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					event_target:infiltration_target = {
						NOT = { has_character_flag = built_laboratory }
					}
				}
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 12 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 13 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 16 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 18 }
				set_character_flag = lab_destruction
			}
			55 = { # Damage
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 10 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.1 learning = 12 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.1 learning = 13 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.1 learning = 16 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.1 learning = 18 }
				set_character_flag = lab_damage
			}
			5 = { # The fire takes the member
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					event_target:infiltration_target = {
						NOT = { has_character_flag = built_laboratory }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.2
					event_target:infiltration_target = { has_character_flag = hidden_lab }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.5
					event_target:infiltration_target = { has_character_flag = very_hidden_lab }
				}
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 NOT = { learning = 10 } }
				modifier = { factor = 2 NOT = { learning = 8 } }
				modifier = { factor = 2 NOT = { learning = 6 } }
				set_character_flag = lab_explosion_death
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAverena68
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lab_destruction }
		event_target:infiltration_target = {
			tooltip = { verena_library_remove_effect = yes destroy_all_ingredients_effect = yes }
		}
		add_society_currency_major_effect = yes
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAverena68
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lab_damage }
		tooltip = {
			event_target:infiltration_target = {
				scaled_wealth = { value = -0.3 min = -30 }
				destroy_random_ingredients_effect = yes
			}
		}
		add_society_currency_medium_effect = yes
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCverena68
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lab_explosion_death }
		death = { death_reason = death_accident }
	}

	after = {
		hidden_effect = {
			event_target:infiltration_target = { character_event = { id = verena.69 } }
			character_event = { id = verena.75 days = 40 }
		}
	}
}

# verena.69 My scriptorium is wrecked
# The hoarder learns of it: his library is lost, or he pays for the
# damage; or the burned body of the thief is found.
# Scope: ROOT is the hoarder, FROM the member.
character_event = {
	id = verena.69
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = lab_destruction } }
		text = EVTDESCverena69_ruin
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = lab_damage } }
		text = EVTDESCverena69_damage
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				NOR = { has_character_flag = lab_destruction has_character_flag = lab_damage }
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena69_death
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_burning_house
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = CURSES
		trigger = {
			FROM = { OR = { has_character_flag = lab_destruction has_character_flag = lab_damage } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = lab_destruction } }
			destroy_all_ingredients_effect = yes
			verena_library_remove_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			scaled_wealth = { value = -0.3 min = -30 }
			destroy_random_ingredients_effect = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBverena69
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				NOR = { has_character_flag = lab_destruction has_character_flag = lab_damage }
			}
		}
		FROM = { clr_character_flag = lab_explosion_death }
	}

	after = {
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { clr_character_flag = lab_destruction clr_character_flag = lab_damage }
		}
	}
}

# verena.70 The companion takes (router)
# Hidden. While the member wrecks, the companion takes: ingredients, a
# purse, or he is caught and gives them both away (verena.60).
# Scope: ROOT is the companion, FROM the member.
character_event = {
	id = verena.70
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { has_quest = quest_verena_infiltrate_library }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			40 = { # Ingredients
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					event_target:infiltration_target = {
						NOT = { any_artifact = { has_artifact_flag = ingredient } }
					}
				}
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 10 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.1 learning = 12 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.1 learning = 13 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.1 learning = 16 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.1 learning = 18 }
				set_character_flag = good_theft
				FROM = { character_event = { id = verena.71 days = 20 } }
			}
			40 = { # A purse
				set_character_flag = ok_theft
				FROM = { character_event = { id = verena.71 days = 20 } }
			}
			20 = { # Caught
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.2
					event_target:infiltration_target = { has_character_flag = hidden_lab }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.5
					event_target:infiltration_target = { has_character_flag = very_hidden_lab }
				}
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 NOT = { learning = 10 } }
				modifier = { factor = 2 NOT = { learning = 8 } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 NOT = { intrigue = 10 } }
				modifier = { factor = 2 NOT = { intrigue = 8 } }
				set_character_flag = bad_theft
				FROM = { character_event = { id = verena.60 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

}

# verena.75 Away into the night
# The mission is done. Fond companions become friends, the rest
# accomplices.
# Scope: the member.
character_event = {
	id = verena.75
	desc = EVTDESCverena75
	picture = GFX_evt_shadowy_cabal
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_quest = quest_verena_infiltrate_library
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAverena75
		clr_quest = quest_verena_infiltrate_library
		sound_effect = hermetic_society_perform_experiment
		hidden_effect = { verena_raid_companion_done_effect = yes }
		if = {
			limit = {
				opinion = { who = event_target:infiltration_companion value = 40 }
				reverse_opinion = { who = event_target:infiltration_companion value = 40 }
				NOT = { is_friend = event_target:infiltration_companion }
			}
			add_friend = event_target:infiltration_companion
		}
		else = {
			reverse_opinion = {
				who = event_target:infiltration_companion
				modifier = opinion_accomplice
				years = 10
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				opinion = {
					who = event_target:infiltration_companion
					modifier = opinion_accomplice
					years = 10
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
