# verena.100 The Blind Maiden
# The decision hermetics_perform_scrying: the member ties on a silk
# blindfold, prays the Blind Maiden over a bowl of still water and asks
# about battles, the people close to [Root.GetHerHim] or [Root.GetHerHis]
# power. A rare substance thrown into the water strengthens the vision,
# but the member no longer chooses what it shows.
# Scope: a member of the Order of Mysteries (rank 2 or more).
character_event = {
	id = verena.100
	picture = GFX_evt_dark_prayer
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	desc = {
		trigger = { NOT = { any_artifact = { has_artifact_flag = ingredient } } }
		text = EVTDESCverena100
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { any_artifact = { has_artifact_flag = ingredient } }
		text = EVTDESCverena100_ingredient
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes # hermetics_perform_scrying

	trigger = {
		has_an_omen_modifier_trigger = no
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { any_artifact = { has_artifact_flag = ingredient } }
			select_random_hermetic_ingredient_effect = yes
		}
	}

	option = { # Battles
		name = EVTOPTAverena100
		set_character_flag = battle_focused_scrying_flag
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 modifier = { factor = 2 war = yes } }
	}
	option = { # Relationships
		name = EVTOPTBverena100
		set_character_flag = relationship_focused_scrying_flag
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
	option = { # Power
		name = EVTOPTCverena100
		set_character_flag = power_hungry_scrying_flag
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = ambitious } }
	}
	option = { # A rare substance
		trigger = { any_artifact = { has_artifact_flag = ingredient } }
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTDverena100_herb
			trigger = {
				any_artifact = {
					has_artifact_flag = selected_ingredient_1
					is_ingredient_herb_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTDverena100_animal_part
			trigger = {
				any_artifact = {
					has_artifact_flag = selected_ingredient_1
					is_ingredient_animal_part_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTDverena100_powder
			trigger = {
				any_artifact = {
					has_artifact_flag = selected_ingredient_1
					is_ingredient_powder_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTDverena100_liquid
			trigger = {
				any_artifact = {
					has_artifact_flag = selected_ingredient_1
					is_ingredient_liquid_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_scrying_ingredient_tt }
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				10 = { set_character_flag = battle_focused_scrying_flag }
				10 = { set_character_flag = relationship_focused_scrying_flag }
				10 = { set_character_flag = power_hungry_scrying_flag }
			}
			set_character_flag = used_ingredient_flag
		}
		destroy_selected_ingredient_1_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { factor = 200 modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = ambitious } }
	}

	after = {
		hidden_effect = {
			remove_selection_of_ingredients_effect = yes
			character_event = { id = verena.101 days = 5 random = 1 }
		}
	}
}

# verena.101 The vision in the water
# The water shows one of three visions of what the member asked about, and
# the member reads it as a good or a bad omen (a rare substance makes a good
# reading likelier). The omen waits for the moment that fulfils or belies
# it: a battle, a birth, a death, a title, a sickness. If nothing does
# within about four years, a fallback settles it (verena.119 - 121).
# Scope: the member.
character_event = {
	id = verena.101
	picture = GFX_evt_dark_prayer
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = battle_focused_scrying_flag
			has_character_flag = desc_variation_A_flag
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena101_battle_A
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = battle_focused_scrying_flag
			has_character_flag = desc_variation_B_flag
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena101_battle_B
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = battle_focused_scrying_flag
			has_character_flag = desc_variation_C_flag
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena101_battle_C
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = relationship_focused_scrying_flag
			has_character_flag = desc_variation_A_flag
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena101_relationship_A
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = relationship_focused_scrying_flag
			has_character_flag = desc_variation_B_flag
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena101_relationship_B
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = relationship_focused_scrying_flag
			has_character_flag = desc_variation_C_flag
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena101_relationship_C
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = power_hungry_scrying_flag
			has_character_flag = desc_variation_A_flag
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena101_power_A
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = power_hungry_scrying_flag
			has_character_flag = desc_variation_B_flag
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena101_power_B
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = power_hungry_scrying_flag
			has_character_flag = desc_variation_C_flag
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena101_power_C
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		society_member_of = cult_of_verena
		has_an_omen_modifier_trigger = no
	}

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			10 = { set_character_flag = desc_variation_A_flag }
			10 = { set_character_flag = desc_variation_B_flag }
			10 = { set_character_flag = desc_variation_C_flag }
		}
		random_list = {
			30 = {
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_character_flag = used_ingredient_flag }
				modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = mystic }
				set_character_flag = positive_reaction_to_scrying_flag
			}
			30 = {
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = paranoid }
				set_character_flag = negative_reaction_to_scrying_flag
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # A good omen
		trigger = { has_character_flag = positive_reaction_to_scrying_flag }
		name = EVTOPTAverena101
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = battle_focused_scrying_flag }
			add_character_modifier = { name = good_battle_omen duration = -1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = relationship_focused_scrying_flag }
			add_character_modifier = { name = good_relationship_omen duration = -1 }
		}
		else = { add_character_modifier = { name = good_power_omen duration = -1 } }
	}
	option = { # A bad omen
		trigger = { has_character_flag = negative_reaction_to_scrying_flag }
		name = EVTOPTBverena101
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = battle_focused_scrying_flag }
			add_character_modifier = { name = bad_battle_omen duration = -1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = relationship_focused_scrying_flag }
			add_character_modifier = { name = bad_relationship_omen duration = -1 }
		}
		else = { add_character_modifier = { name = bad_power_omen duration = -1 } }
	}

	after = {
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				10 = { character_event = { id = verena.119 days = 1500 random = 300 } }
				10 = { character_event = { id = verena.120 days = 1500 random = 300 } }
				10 = { character_event = { id = verena.121 days = 1500 random = 300 } }
			}
			clr_character_flag = desc_variation_A_flag
			clr_character_flag = desc_variation_B_flag
			clr_character_flag = desc_variation_C_flag
			clr_character_flag = battle_focused_scrying_flag
			clr_character_flag = relationship_focused_scrying_flag
			clr_character_flag = power_hungry_scrying_flag
			clr_character_flag = used_ingredient_flag
		}
	}
}

# verena.102 The battle foretold: glory
# In battle a member with a battle omen learns a new way to lead: as
# foretold, or despite a bad omen.
# Scope: the member, in battle. Fired from the combat pulse in
# events/WHG_battle_events.txt.
character_event = {
	id = verena.102
	title = the_battle_at_location
	picture = GFX_evt_battle
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_modifier = good_battle_omen } text = EVTDESCverena102_good }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_modifier = bad_battle_omen } text = EVTDESCverena102_bad }

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_battle_omen_modifier_trigger = yes
		is_alive = yes
		can_have_more_leadership_traits = yes
		NAND = { trait = experimenter trait = unyielding_leader trait = inspiring_leader }
	}

	immediate = {
		disallow_new_battle_events_effect = yes
		random_list = {
			10 = {
				trigger = { NOT = { trait = experimenter } }
				set_character_flag = gain_direct_leader
			}
			10 = {
				trigger = { NOT = { trait = unyielding_leader } }
				set_character_flag = gain_unyielding_leader
			}
			10 = {
				trigger = { NOT = { trait = inspiring_leader } }
				set_character_flag = gain_inspiring_leader
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = gain_direct_leader }
		name = EVTOPTAverena102
		add_trait = experimenter
	}
	option = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = gain_unyielding_leader }
		name = EVTOPTBverena102
		add_trait = unyielding_leader
	}
	option = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = gain_inspiring_leader }
		name = EVTOPTCverena102
		add_trait = inspiring_leader
	}

	after = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = good_battle_omen }
			upgrade_seer_modifier_effect = yes
		}
		else = { downgrade_seer_modifier_effect = yes }
		remove_scrying_omen_modifiers_effect = yes
		clr_character_flag = gain_direct_leader
		clr_character_flag = gain_unyielding_leader
		clr_character_flag = gain_inspiring_leader
		allow_new_battle_events_effect = yes
	}
}

# verena.103 The battle foretold: the wound
# In battle a member with a battle omen is struck down: as foretold, or
# despite a good omen. Wounded, crippled or, rarely, killed.
# Scope: the member, in battle. Fired from the combat pulse in
# events/WHG_battle_events.txt.
character_event = {
	id = verena.103
	title = the_battle_at_location
	picture = GFX_evt_battle
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_modifier = bad_battle_omen } text = EVTDESCverena103_bad }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_modifier = good_battle_omen } text = EVTDESCverena103_good }

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_battle_omen_modifier_trigger = yes
		is_alive = yes
	}

	immediate = {
		disallow_new_battle_events_effect = yes
	}

	option = { # The omen was right
		trigger = { has_character_modifier = bad_battle_omen }
		name = EVTOPTAverena103
		upgrade_seer_modifier_effect = yes
	}
	option = { # The omen was wrong
		trigger = { has_character_modifier = good_battle_omen }
		name = EVTOPTBverena103
		downgrade_seer_modifier_effect = yes
	}

	after = {
		random_list = {
			40 = { add_trait_wounded_effect = yes }
			40 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = one_legged NOT = { has_dlc = "Reapers" } } }
				add_trait = one_legged
				character_event = { id = maimed.3 } # One-legged
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { trait = severely_injured } }
					add_trait = severely_injured
					resolve_severely_injured_effect = yes
				}
			}
			5 = { death = { death_reason = death_battle } }
		}
		remove_scrying_omen_modifiers_effect = yes
		allow_new_battle_events_effect = yes
	}
}

# verena.104 The child foretold
# A parent with a relationship omen meets a newborn who is either clearly
# gifted (smart or strong, no inborn flaw) or clearly not (an inborn flaw,
# neither smart nor strong). A good omen and a gifted child, or a bad omen
# and a dull one, prove the vision right. Was MNM.1204 and 1205, merged.
# Scope: the parent. FROM is the newborn.
character_event = {
	id = verena.104
	picture = GFX_evt_state_diplomacy
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = verena_omen_child_gifted
			has_character_flag = verena_omen_was_good
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena104_gifted_good
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = verena_omen_child_gifted
			has_character_flag = verena_omen_was_bad
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena104_gifted_bad
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = verena_omen_child_dull
			has_character_flag = verena_omen_was_bad
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena104_dull_bad
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = verena_omen_child_dull
			has_character_flag = verena_omen_was_good
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena104_dull_good
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_relationship_omen_modifier_trigger = yes
		is_alive = yes
		NOT = { trait = incapable }
		FROM = {
			is_alive = yes
			OR = {
				AND = {
					OR = { is_smart_trigger = yes is_strong_trigger = yes }
					has_negative_congenital_trigger = no
				}
				AND = {
					has_negative_congenital_trigger = yes
					is_smart_trigger = no
					is_strong_trigger = no
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_negative_congenital_trigger = no } }
			set_character_flag = verena_omen_child_gifted
		}
		else = { set_character_flag = verena_omen_child_dull }
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = good_relationship_omen }
			set_character_flag = verena_omen_was_good
		}
		else = { set_character_flag = verena_omen_was_bad }
		remove_scrying_omen_modifiers_effect = yes
	}

	option = { # The vision was right
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = verena_omen_child_gifted
					has_character_flag = verena_omen_was_good
				}
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = verena_omen_child_dull
					has_character_flag = verena_omen_was_bad
				}
			}
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTAverena104_gifted
			trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_omen_child_gifted }
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTAverena104_dull
			trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_omen_child_dull }
		}
		upgrade_seer_modifier_effect = yes
	}
	option = { # The vision was wrong
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = verena_omen_child_gifted
					has_character_flag = verena_omen_was_bad
				}
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = verena_omen_child_dull
					has_character_flag = verena_omen_was_good
				}
			}
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTBverena104_gifted
			trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_omen_child_gifted }
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTBverena104_dull
			trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_omen_child_dull }
		}
		downgrade_seer_modifier_effect = yes
	}

	after = {
		clr_character_flag = verena_omen_child_gifted
		clr_character_flag = verena_omen_child_dull
		clr_character_flag = verena_omen_was_good
		clr_character_flag = verena_omen_was_bad
	}
}

# verena.105 The child foretold (ping)
# Hidden. A newborn whose father or mother carries a relationship omen
# shows itself to them (verena.104).
# Scope: the newborn. Fired from the on_birth random events.
character_event = {
	id = verena.105
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_birth

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			father = { has_relationship_omen_modifier_trigger = yes }
			mother = { has_relationship_omen_modifier_trigger = yes }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		father = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_relationship_omen_modifier_trigger = yes }
				character_event = { id = verena.104 days = 15 }
			}
		}
		mother = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_relationship_omen_modifier_trigger = yes }
				character_event = { id = verena.104 days = 15 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# verena.106 A death foretold (ping)
# Hidden. Someone dies, and the people close to [Root.GetHerHim] who carry a
# relationship omen learn of it: a lover, a friend, a rival, a grown child
# or a parent, each once (verena.107).
# Scope: the dead character. Fired from the on_death random events.
character_event = {
	id = verena.106
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_death

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			any_lover = { verena_death_omen_recipient_trigger = yes }
			any_friend = { verena_death_omen_recipient_trigger = yes }
			AND = { is_adult = yes any_rival = { verena_death_omen_recipient_trigger = yes } }
			any_child = { is_adult = yes verena_death_omen_recipient_trigger = yes }
			mother = { verena_death_omen_recipient_trigger = yes }
			father = { verena_death_omen_recipient_trigger = yes }
		}
		NOR = { death_no_trace_trigger = yes death_in_cathay_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = mnm_scrying_dead
		any_lover = {
			limit = { verena_death_omen_recipient_trigger = yes }
			set_character_flag = verena_omen_death_lover
			character_event = { id = verena.107 days = 20 }
		}
		any_friend = {
			limit = { verena_death_omen_recipient_trigger = yes }
			set_character_flag = verena_omen_death_friend
			character_event = { id = verena.107 days = 30 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_adult = yes }
			any_rival = {
				limit = { verena_death_omen_recipient_trigger = yes }
				set_character_flag = verena_omen_death_rival
				character_event = { id = verena.107 days = 30 }
			}
		}
		any_child = {
			limit = { is_adult = yes verena_death_omen_recipient_trigger = yes }
			set_character_flag = verena_omen_death_parent
			character_event = { id = verena.107 days = 30 }
		}
		mother = {
			if = {
				limit = { verena_death_omen_recipient_trigger = yes }
				set_character_flag = verena_omen_death_child
				character_event = { id = verena.107 days = 30 }
			}
		}
		father = {
			if = {
				limit = { verena_death_omen_recipient_trigger = yes }
				set_character_flag = verena_omen_death_child
				character_event = { id = verena.107 days = 30 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# verena.107 A death foretold
# A lover, friend, rival, parent or child has died. Grief proves a bad
# omen right and a good one wrong. The death of a rival does the opposite.
# Was MNM.1208 - 1212, merged.
# Scope: the one who carried the omen. Event target mnm_scrying_dead.
character_event = {
	id = verena.107
	picture = GFX_evt_secluded_in_prayer
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = verena_omen_death_lover
			has_character_modifier = bad_relationship_omen
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena107_lover_right
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = verena_omen_death_lover
			has_character_modifier = good_relationship_omen
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena107_lover_wrong
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = verena_omen_death_friend
			has_character_modifier = bad_relationship_omen
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena107_friend_right
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = verena_omen_death_friend
			has_character_modifier = good_relationship_omen
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena107_friend_wrong
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = verena_omen_death_rival
			has_character_modifier = good_relationship_omen
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena107_rival_right
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = verena_omen_death_rival
			has_character_modifier = bad_relationship_omen
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena107_rival_wrong
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = verena_omen_death_parent
			has_character_modifier = bad_relationship_omen
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena107_parent_right
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = verena_omen_death_parent
			has_character_modifier = good_relationship_omen
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena107_parent_wrong
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = verena_omen_death_child
			has_character_modifier = bad_relationship_omen
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena107_child_right
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = verena_omen_death_child
			has_character_modifier = good_relationship_omen
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena107_child_wrong
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_relationship_omen_modifier_trigger = yes
		NOT = { trait = incapable }
	}

	option = { # The vision was right
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = verena_omen_death_rival
					has_character_modifier = good_relationship_omen
				}
				AND = {
					NOT = { has_character_flag = verena_omen_death_rival }
					has_character_modifier = bad_relationship_omen
				}
			}
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTAverena107_rival
			trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_omen_death_rival }
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTAverena107
			trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = verena_omen_death_rival } }
		}
		upgrade_seer_modifier_effect = yes
	}
	option = { # The vision was wrong
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = verena_omen_death_rival
					has_character_modifier = bad_relationship_omen
				}
				AND = {
					NOT = { has_character_flag = verena_omen_death_rival }
					has_character_modifier = good_relationship_omen
				}
			}
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTBverena107_rival
			trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_omen_death_rival }
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTBverena107
			trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = verena_omen_death_rival } }
		}
		downgrade_seer_modifier_effect = yes
	}

	after = {
		remove_scrying_omen_modifiers_effect = yes
	}
}

# verena.108 A pet foretold (ping)
# Hidden. A cat or a dog has died, and its owner may carry a relationship
# omen (verena.109).
# Scope: the owner. Fired from pet_cat_death_omen_effect in
# common/scripted_effects/wh_pet_effects.txt, events/WHG_battle_events.txt
# and events/WHG_hunting_events.txt.
character_event = {
	id = verena.108
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = { has_character_flag = cat_death_omen has_character_flag = dog_death_omen }
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_relationship_omen_modifier_trigger = yes NOT = { trait = incapable } }
			character_event = { id = verena.109 }
		}
		else = { clr_character_flag = cat_death_omen clr_character_flag = dog_death_omen }
	}
}

# verena.109 A pet foretold
# The owner wonders whether the death of the cat or dog was what the vision
# was about. Madness makes it a curse, or the owner's own doing.
# Scope: the owner.
character_event = {
	id = verena.109
	picture = GFX_evt_secluded_in_prayer
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_modifier = bad_relationship_omen is_lunatic_trigger = no }
		text = EVTDESCverena109_right
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_modifier = good_relationship_omen is_lunatic_trigger = no }
		text = EVTDESCverena109_wrong
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_modifier = bad_relationship_omen is_lunatic_trigger = yes }
		text = EVTDESCverena109_right_lunatic
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_modifier = good_relationship_omen is_lunatic_trigger = yes }
		text = EVTDESCverena109_wrong_lunatic
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_relationship_omen_modifier_trigger = yes
		NOT = { trait = incapable }
	}

	option = { # The vision was right
		trigger = { has_character_modifier = bad_relationship_omen }
		name = { text = EVTOPTAverena109_cat trigger = { has_character_flag = cat_death_omen } }
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTAverena109_dog
			trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = cat_death_omen } }
		}
		upgrade_seer_modifier_effect = yes
	}
	option = { # The vision was wrong
		trigger = { has_character_modifier = good_relationship_omen }
		name = { text = EVTOPTBverena109_cat trigger = { has_character_flag = cat_death_omen } }
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTBverena109_dog
			trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = cat_death_omen } }
		}
		downgrade_seer_modifier_effect = yes
	}

	after = {
		clr_character_flag = cat_death_omen
		clr_character_flag = dog_death_omen
		remove_scrying_omen_modifiers_effect = yes
	}
}

# verena.110 A title foretold
# The member gains a title while carrying a power omen. A good omen was
# right.
# Scope: the new holder. FROM is the title. Fired from on_new_holder,
# on_new_holder_inheritance and on_new_holder_usurpation.
character_event = {
	id = verena.110
	picture = GFX_evt_gathering_of_minds
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = omen_opt1 } text = EVTDESCverena110_right }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = omen_opt2 } text = EVTDESCverena110_wrong }

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_new_holder
	only_rulers = yes

	trigger = {
		has_power_omen_modifier_trigger = yes
		NOT = { trait = incapable }
	}

	immediate = {
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = good_power_omen } set_character_flag = omen_opt1 }
		else = { set_character_flag = omen_opt2 }
		remove_scrying_omen_modifiers_effect = yes
	}

	option = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = omen_opt1 }
		name = EVTOPTAverena110
		upgrade_seer_modifier_effect = yes
	}
	option = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = omen_opt2 }
		name = EVTOPTBverena110
		downgrade_seer_modifier_effect = yes
	}

	after = {
		clr_character_flag = omen_opt1
		clr_character_flag = omen_opt2
	}
}

# verena.111 A loss foretold (ping)
# Hidden. A title changes hands while the old holder lives. The new holder
# checks a day later whether it was a free grant (verena.113).
# Scope: the new holder. FROM is the title, FROMFROM the old holder. Fired
# from on_new_holder, on_new_holder_inheritance and on_new_holder_usurpation.
character_event = {
	id = verena.111
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_new_holder
	only_rulers = yes

	trigger = {
		FROMFROM = { is_alive = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = new_holder_target
		FROM = { save_event_target_as = verena_omen_title }
		FROMFROM = { save_event_target_as = old_holder_target }
		character_event = { id = verena.113 days = 1 }
	}
}

# verena.112 A free grant
# Hidden. A title granted of free will is no loss foretold.
# Scope: diplomatic response; FROM is the new holder. Fired from
# GRANT_LANDED_TITLE_INTERACTION_ACCEPT_EVENT.
diploresponse_event = {
	id = verena.112
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { set_character_flag = was_granted_title_voluntarily }
	}
}

# verena.113 A loss foretold (check)
# Hidden. Unless the title was granted of free will, the old holder learns
# of the loss (verena.114).
# Scope: the new holder.
character_event = {
	id = verena.113
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = was_granted_title_voluntarily }
			clr_character_flag = was_granted_title_voluntarily
		}
		else = {
			event_target:old_holder_target = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_power_omen_modifier_trigger = yes }
					character_event = { id = verena.114 days = 3 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# verena.116 A sickness foretold: someone close
# A relative or friend falls sick, or the member, while the member carries
# a relationship omen. A bad omen was right.

# scrying_omen_fulfilled_by_gaining_disease_effect.

	id = verena.116
	picture = GFX_evt_recovery
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { NOT = { character = ROOT } } has_character_flag = omen_opt1 }
		text = EVTDESCverena116_right
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { NOT = { character = ROOT } } has_character_flag = omen_opt2 }
		text = EVTDESCverena116_wrong
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { character = ROOT } has_character_flag = omen_opt1 }
		text = EVTDESCverena116_self_right
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { character = ROOT } has_character_flag = omen_opt2 }
		text = EVTDESCverena116_self_wrong
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_relationship_omen_modifier_trigger = yes
		FROM = { is_alive = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = bad_relationship_omen }
			set_character_flag = omen_opt1
		}
		else = { set_character_flag = omen_opt2 }
		remove_scrying_omen_modifiers_effect = yes

	option = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = omen_opt1 }
		name = EVTOPTAverena115
		upgrade_seer_modifier_effect = yes
	}
	option = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = omen_opt2 }
		name = EVTOPTBverena115
		downgrade_seer_modifier_effect = yes
	}

	after = {
		clr_character_flag = omen_opt1

	}
}

# verena.119 The omen comes true: a blessing
# Fallback, about four years after the vision. Something good happens: a
# feat of arms, a new friend, or one of Verena's owls settling in the
# castle. A good omen was right, a bad one wrong.
# Scope: the member.
character_event = {
	id = verena.119
	picture = GFX_evt_state_diplomacy
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = omen_opt_good_battle }
		picture = GFX_evt_hunt
		text = EVTDESCverena119_battle_right
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = omen_opt_bad_battle }
		picture = GFX_evt_hunt
		text = EVTDESCverena119_battle_wrong
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = omen_opt_good_relationship }
		text = EVTDESCverena119_relationship_right
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = omen_opt_bad_relationship }
		text = EVTDESCverena119_relationship_wrong
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = omen_opt_good_power }
		picture = GFX_evt_prospering_province
		text = EVTDESCverena119_power_right
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = omen_opt_bad_power }
		picture = GFX_evt_prospering_province
		text = EVTDESCverena119_power_wrong
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_an_omen_modifier_trigger = yes
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_relationship_omen_modifier_trigger = yes }
			verena_omen_find_courtier_effect = yes
		}
		verena_omen_flags_effect = yes
		remove_scrying_omen_modifiers_effect = yes
	}

	option = { # The vision was right
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_character_flag = omen_opt_good_battle
				has_character_flag = omen_opt_good_relationship
				has_character_flag = omen_opt_good_power
			}
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTAverena119_battle
			trigger = { has_character_flag = omen_opt_good_battle }
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTAverena119_relationship
			trigger = { has_character_flag = omen_opt_good_relationship }
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTAverena119_power
			trigger = { has_character_flag = omen_opt_good_power }
		}
		verena_omen_blessing_effect = yes
		upgrade_seer_modifier_effect = yes
	}
	option = { # The vision was wrong
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_character_flag = omen_opt_bad_battle
				has_character_flag = omen_opt_bad_relationship
				has_character_flag = omen_opt_bad_power
			}
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTBverena119_battle
			trigger = { has_character_flag = omen_opt_bad_battle }
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTBverena119_relationship
			trigger = { has_character_flag = omen_opt_bad_relationship }
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTBverena119_power
			trigger = { has_character_flag = omen_opt_bad_power }
		}
		verena_omen_blessing_effect = yes
		downgrade_seer_modifier_effect = yes
	}

	after = {
		verena_omen_clear_flags_effect = yes
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Part 7: the horoscope

# verena.130 The horoscope
# The decision hermetics_make_horoscope: a High Priest of the Order reads
# the stars above a child of [Root.GetHerHis] blood. The reading takes many
# nights (verena.140, then verena.131).
# Scope: the parent; event target horoscope_child.
character_event = {
	id = verena.130
	desc = EVTDESCverena130
	picture = GFX_evt_secluded_in_prayer
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # hermetics_make_horoscope

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = horoscope_maker
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAverena130
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAverena130_tt }
	}

	after = {
		hidden_effect = {
			event_target:horoscope_child = {
				set_variable = { which = horoscope_destiny_points value = 0 }
				set_character_flag = horoscope_destiny_revealed
				character_event = { id = verena.140 days = 5 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# verena.131 The stars have spoken
# The stars above the child's birth sign point to a destiny: a steward, a
# warrior, a diplomat, a schemer or a scholar. The sign weighs on which:
# Dragomas the Drake breeds warriors, Mammit the Wise scholars, the Dancer
# diplomats, the Gloaming schemers, Cackelfax the Cockerel stewards. The
# parent means to encourage the destiny, or to fight it.
# Scope: the parent; event target horoscope_child.
character_event = {
	id = verena.131
	picture = GFX_evt_secluded_in_prayer
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	desc = {
		trigger = { event_target:horoscope_child = { has_character_flag = destined_steward } }
		text = EVTDESCverena131_steward
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { event_target:horoscope_child = { has_character_flag = destined_fighter } }
		text = EVTDESCverena131_fighter
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { event_target:horoscope_child = { has_character_flag = destined_diplomat } }
		text = EVTDESCverena131_diplomat
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { event_target:horoscope_child = { has_character_flag = destined_schemer } }
		text = EVTDESCverena131_schemer
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { event_target:horoscope_child = { has_character_flag = destined_scholar } }
		text = EVTDESCverena131_scholar
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		event_target:horoscope_child = {
			random_list = {
				10 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 3
						OR = {
							is_born_under_cackelfax_the_cockerel_trigger = yes
							is_born_under_gnuthus_the_ox_trigger = yes
							is_born_under_the_two_bullocks_trigger = yes
							is_born_under_wymund_the_anchorite_trigger = yes
						}
					}
					set_character_flag = destined_steward
				}
				10 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 3
						OR = {
							is_born_under_dragomas_the_drake_trigger = yes
							is_born_under_grungi_baldric_trigger = yes
							is_born_under_the_limner_line_trigger = yes
						}
					}
					set_character_flag = destined_fighter
				}
				10 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 3
						OR = {
							is_born_under_the_dancer_trigger = yes
							is_born_under_the_drummer_trigger = yes
							is_born_under_rhya_cauldron_trigger = yes
						}
					}
					set_character_flag = destined_diplomat
				}
				10 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 3
						OR = {
							is_born_under_the_gloaming_trigger = yes
							is_born_under_the_piper_trigger = yes
							is_born_under_vobist_the_faint_trigger = yes
							is_born_under_the_greased_goat_trigger = yes
							is_born_under_the_broken_cart_trigger = yes
						}
					}
					set_character_flag = destined_schemer
				}
				10 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 3
						OR = {
							is_born_under_mammit_the_wise_trigger = yes
							is_born_under_the_big_cross_trigger = yes
							is_born_under_the_bonesaw_trigger = yes
							is_born_under_the_witchling_star_trigger = yes
						}
					}
					set_character_flag = destined_scholar
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Encourage it
		name = EVTOPTAverena131
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAverena131_tt }
		event_target:horoscope_child = {
			set_character_flag = encouraged_horoscope_pattern
			hidden_effect = { change_variable = { which = horoscope_destiny_points value = 5 } }
		}
	}
	option = { # Fight it
		name = EVTOPTBverena131
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBverena131_tt }
		event_target:horoscope_child = {
			set_character_flag = discouraged_horoscope_pattern
			hidden_effect = { change_variable = { which = horoscope_destiny_points value = -5 } }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 25 }
	}

	after = {
		hidden_effect = {
			event_target:horoscope_child = {
				set_character_flag = horoscope_child_receives_horoscope
				character_event = { id = verena.140 days = 60 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# verena.132 What the stars hold for me
# The child, four or older, hears of the horoscope: from the parent, or
# from the servants if the parent has died. With the parent alive the
# child's education starts to lean to the foretold path.
# Scope: the child; event target horoscope_maker.
character_event = {
	id = verena.132
	picture = GFX_evt_gathering_of_minds
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = encouraged_horoscope_pattern
			NOT = { has_character_flag = horoscope_maker_died }
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena132_encouraged
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = discouraged_horoscope_pattern
			NOT = { has_character_flag = horoscope_maker_died }
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena132_discouraged
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = encouraged_horoscope_pattern
			has_character_flag = horoscope_maker_died
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena132_encouraged_dead
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = discouraged_horoscope_pattern
			has_character_flag = horoscope_maker_died
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena132_discouraged_dead
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # The chain goes on
		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = horoscope_maker_died } }
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTAverena132_encouraged
			trigger = { has_character_flag = encouraged_horoscope_pattern }
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTAverena132_discouraged
			trigger = { has_character_flag = discouraged_horoscope_pattern }
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAverena132_tt }
		set_horoscope_modifier_effect = yes
		hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = verena.140 days = 10 } }
	}
	option = { # The parent is dead: the chain ends
		trigger = { has_character_flag = horoscope_maker_died }
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTBverena132_encouraged
			trigger = { has_character_flag = encouraged_horoscope_pattern }
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTBverena132_discouraged
			trigger = { has_character_flag = discouraged_horoscope_pattern }
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAverena132_tt }
		hidden_effect = { clean_up_horoscope_effect = yes }
	}
}

# verena.133 The first signs
# The parent sees the child, six or older, show a talent for the foretold
# path, and is glad of it or steers against it.
# Scope: the parent; event target horoscope_child.
character_event = {
	id = verena.133
	picture = GFX_evt_gathering_of_minds
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	desc = {
		trigger = { event_target:horoscope_child = { has_character_flag = destined_steward } }
		text = EVTDESCverena133_steward
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { event_target:horoscope_child = { has_character_flag = destined_fighter } }
		text = EVTDESCverena133_fighter
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { event_target:horoscope_child = { has_character_flag = destined_diplomat } }
		text = EVTDESCverena133_diplomat
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { event_target:horoscope_child = { has_character_flag = destined_schemer } }
		text = EVTDESCverena133_schemer
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { event_target:horoscope_child = { has_character_flag = destined_scholar } }
		text = EVTDESCverena133_scholar
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		event_target:horoscope_child = { is_alive = yes }
	}

	option = { # Encouraged, and glad
		trigger = {
			event_target:horoscope_child = { has_character_flag = encouraged_horoscope_pattern }
		}
		name = EVTOPTAverena133
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAverena133_tt }
		hidden_effect = {
			event_target:horoscope_child = {
				change_variable = { which = horoscope_destiny_points value = 3 }
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # Encouraged, but no longer
		trigger = {
			event_target:horoscope_child = { has_character_flag = encouraged_horoscope_pattern }
		}
		name = EVTOPTBverena133
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBverena133_tt }
		hidden_effect = {
			event_target:horoscope_child = {
				change_variable = { which = horoscope_destiny_points value = -7 }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 25 }
	}
	option = { # Discouraged, but giving in
		trigger = {
			event_target:horoscope_child = { has_character_flag = discouraged_horoscope_pattern }
		}
		name = EVTOPTCverena133
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTCverena133_tt }
		hidden_effect = {
			event_target:horoscope_child = {
				change_variable = { which = horoscope_destiny_points value = 3 }
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # Discouraged, and fighting on
		trigger = {
			event_target:horoscope_child = { has_character_flag = discouraged_horoscope_pattern }
		}
		name = EVTOPTDverena133
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTDverena133_tt }
		hidden_effect = {
			event_target:horoscope_child = {
				change_variable = { which = horoscope_destiny_points value = -7 }
			}
		}
	}

	after = {
		hidden_effect = {
			event_target:horoscope_child = { character_event = { id = verena.140 days = 10 } }
		}
	}
}

# verena.134 Is this my path?
# At thirteen the child spends time with an adult of the court, best one
# skilled in the foretold path, and embraces the destiny or turns from it.
# Scope: the child; event target horoscope_maker.
character_event = {
	id = verena.134
	picture = GFX_evt_ritual_scroll
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = destined_steward } text = EVTDESCverena134_steward }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = destined_fighter } text = EVTDESCverena134_fighter }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = destined_diplomat } text = EVTDESCverena134_diplomat }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = destined_schemer } text = EVTDESCverena134_schemer }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = destined_scholar } text = EVTDESCverena134_scholar }

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		is_alive = yes
		verena_horoscope_has_mentor_trigger = yes
	}

	immediate = {
		host = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { character = ROOT } is_adult = yes }
				save_event_target_as = inspiring_adult
			}
			if = {
				limit = { any_courtier = { is_adult = yes prisoner = no } }
				random_courtier = {
					limit = { is_adult = yes prisoner = no }
					save_event_target_as = inspiring_adult
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { any_courtier = { verena_horoscope_mentor_trigger = yes } }
				random_courtier = {
					limit = { verena_horoscope_mentor_trigger = yes }
					preferred_limit = { has_top_tier_education_trait_trigger = yes }
					save_event_target_as = inspiring_adult
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # This is my path
		name = EVTOPTAverena134
		hidden_effect = { change_variable = { which = horoscope_destiny_points value = 5 } }
		set_character_flag = embraced_destiny
		event_target:inspiring_adult = {
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_delighted years = 10 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				OR = {
					AND = {
						has_character_flag = destined_steward
						OR = { trait = conscientious trait = fussy trait = curious }
					}
					AND = {
						has_character_flag = destined_fighter
						OR = { trait = rowdy trait = haughty }
					}
					AND = {
						has_character_flag = destined_diplomat
						OR = { trait = playful trait = affectionate }
					}
					AND = {
						has_character_flag = destined_schemer
						OR = { trait = willful trait = brooding trait = indolent }
					}
					AND = {
						has_character_flag = destined_scholar
						OR = { trait = idolizer trait = timid }
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				OR = {
					AND = { stewardship = 5 has_character_flag = destined_steward }
					AND = { martial = 5 has_character_flag = destined_fighter }
					AND = { diplomacy = 5 has_character_flag = destined_diplomat }
					AND = { intrigue = 5 has_character_flag = destined_schemer }
					AND = { learning = 5 has_character_flag = destined_scholar }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				OR = {
					AND = { stewardship = 9 has_character_flag = destined_steward }
					AND = { martial = 9 has_character_flag = destined_fighter }
					AND = { diplomacy = 9 has_character_flag = destined_diplomat }
					AND = { intrigue = 9 has_character_flag = destined_schemer }
					AND = { learning = 9 has_character_flag = destined_scholar }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # I make my own path
		name = EVTOPTBverena134
		random = { chance = 50 add_trait_stressed_effect = yes }
		hidden_effect = { change_variable = { which = horoscope_destiny_points value = -5 } }
		set_character_flag = refused_destiny
		ai_chance = { factor = 30 }
	}

	after = {
		hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = verena.140 days = 100 } }
	}
}

# verena.135 The child's choice
# The parent hears whether the child took to the foretold path.
# Scope: the parent; event target horoscope_child.
character_event = {
	id = verena.135
	picture = GFX_evt_gathering_of_minds
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:horoscope_child = {
				has_character_flag = encouraged_horoscope_pattern
				has_character_flag = embraced_destiny
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena135_encouraged_embraced
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:horoscope_child = {
				has_character_flag = discouraged_horoscope_pattern
				has_character_flag = embraced_destiny
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena135_discouraged_embraced
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:horoscope_child = {
				has_character_flag = encouraged_horoscope_pattern
				has_character_flag = refused_destiny
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena135_encouraged_refused
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:horoscope_child = {
				has_character_flag = discouraged_horoscope_pattern
				has_character_flag = refused_destiny
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena135_discouraged_refused
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		event_target:horoscope_child = { is_alive = yes }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAverena135
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAverena135_tt }
		hidden_effect = {
			event_target:horoscope_child = {
				change_variable = { which = horoscope_destiny_points value = 2 }
				character_event = { id = verena.140 days = 30 }
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBverena135
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBverena135_tt }
		hidden_effect = {
			event_target:horoscope_child = {
				change_variable = { which = horoscope_destiny_points value = -2 }
				character_event = { id = verena.140 days = 30 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# verena.136 Grown under the stars
# Hidden. At adulthood the childhood horoscope modifier gives way to the
# adult one.
# Scope: the child. Fired from on_adulthood.
character_event = {
	id = verena.136
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_adulthood

	trigger = {
		has_childhood_horoscope_modifier_trigger = yes
		NOT = { has_character_flag = avoided_destiny }
	}

	immediate = {
		set_horoscope_modifier_effect = yes
	}
}

# verena.137 Destiny fulfilled
# The grown child has lived up to the stars: the education of the foretold
# path improves, or the skill does.
# Scope: the child; event target horoscope_maker.
character_event = {
	id = verena.137
	picture = GFX_evt_state_diplomacy
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = encouraged_horoscope_pattern
			has_character_flag = embraced_destiny
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena137_encouraged_embraced
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = discouraged_horoscope_pattern
			has_character_flag = embraced_destiny
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena137_discouraged_embraced
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = encouraged_horoscope_pattern
			has_character_flag = refused_destiny
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena137_encouraged_refused
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = discouraged_horoscope_pattern
			has_character_flag = refused_destiny
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena137_discouraged_refused
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # The education improves
		trigger = { has_top_tier_education_trait_trigger = no }
		name = EVTOPTAverena137
		trigger_switch = {
			on_trigger = has_character_flag
			destined_steward = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_education_stewardship_trigger = yes }
					upgrade_education_effect = yes
					set_character_flag = upgraded_stewardship
				}
				else = { change_stewardship = 1 set_character_flag = stewardship_1 }
			}
			destined_fighter = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_education_martial_trigger = yes }
					upgrade_education_effect = yes
					set_character_flag = upgraded_martial
				}
				else = { change_martial = 1 set_character_flag = martial_1 }
			}
			destined_diplomat = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_education_diplomacy_trigger = yes }
					upgrade_education_effect = yes
					set_character_flag = upgraded_diplomacy
				}
				else = { change_diplomacy = 1 set_character_flag = diplomacy_1 }
			}
			destined_schemer = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_education_intrigue_trigger = yes }
					upgrade_education_effect = yes
					set_character_flag = upgraded_intrigue
				}
				else = { change_intrigue = 1 set_character_flag = intrigue_1 }
			}
			destined_scholar = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_education_learning_trigger = yes }
					upgrade_education_effect = yes
					set_character_flag = upgraded_learning
				}
				else = { change_learning = 1 set_character_flag = learning_1 }
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # The education is already the best: the skill improves
		trigger = { has_top_tier_education_trait_trigger = yes }
		name = EVTOPTBverena137
		trigger_switch = {
			on_trigger = has_character_flag
			destined_steward = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_education_stewardship_trigger = yes }
					change_stewardship = 2
					set_character_flag = stewardship_2
				}
				else = { change_stewardship = 1 set_character_flag = stewardship_1 }
			}
			destined_fighter = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_education_martial_trigger = yes }
					change_martial = 2
					set_character_flag = martial_2
				}
				else = { change_martial = 1 set_character_flag = martial_1 }
			}
			destined_diplomat = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_education_diplomacy_trigger = yes }
					change_diplomacy = 2
					set_character_flag = diplomacy_2
				}
				else = { change_diplomacy = 1 set_character_flag = diplomacy_1 }
			}
			destined_schemer = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_education_intrigue_trigger = yes }
					change_intrigue = 2
					set_character_flag = intrigue_2
				}
				else = { change_intrigue = 1 set_character_flag = intrigue_1 }
			}
			destined_scholar = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_education_learning_trigger = yes }
					change_learning = 2
					set_character_flag = learning_2
				}
				else = { change_learning = 1 set_character_flag = learning_1 }
			}
		}
	}

	after = {
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:horoscope_maker = { is_alive = yes } }
				event_target:horoscope_maker = {
					set_character_flag = child_fulfilled_destiny_flag
					character_event = { id = verena.139 }
				}
			}
			else = { verena_horoscope_clear_result_effect = yes }
			clean_up_horoscope_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

# verena.138 Destiny avoided
# The grown child has turned from the stars, and the horoscope's influence
# fades.
# Scope: the child; event target horoscope_maker.
character_event = {
	id = verena.138
	picture = GFX_evt_secluded_in_prayer
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = horoscope_maker_died } text = EVTDESCverena138_dead }
	desc = {
		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = horoscope_maker_died } }
		text = EVTDESCverena138
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = avoided_destiny
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAverena138
		set_horoscope_modifier_effect = yes
	}

	after = {
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:horoscope_maker = { is_alive = yes } }
				event_target:horoscope_maker = {
					set_character_flag = child_avoided_destiny_flag
					character_event = { id = verena.139 }
				}
			}
			clean_up_horoscope_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

# verena.139 The stars were right, or wrong
# The parent sees what became of the child the horoscope was made for.
# Scope: the parent; event target horoscope_child.
character_event = {
	id = verena.139
	picture = GFX_evt_state_diplomacy
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_avoided_destiny_flag }
		text = EVTDESCverena139_avoided
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_fulfilled_destiny_flag }
		text = EVTDESCverena139_fulfilled
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAverena139
		event_target:horoscope_child = {
			show_scope_change = no
			trigger_switch = {
				on_trigger = has_character_flag
				martial_1 = { custom_tooltip = { text = improved_martial_1_tt } }
				martial_2 = { custom_tooltip = { text = improved_martial_2_tt } }
				diplomacy_1 = { custom_tooltip = { text = improved_diplo_1_tt } }
				diplomacy_2 = { custom_tooltip = { text = improved_diplo_2_tt } }
				stewardship_1 = { custom_tooltip = { text = improved_stew_1_tt } }
				stewardship_2 = { custom_tooltip = { text = improved_stew_2_tt } }
				intrigue_1 = { custom_tooltip = { text = improved_int_1_tt } }
				intrigue_2 = { custom_tooltip = { text = improved_int_2_tt } }
				learning_1 = { custom_tooltip = { text = improved_learn_1_tt } }
				learning_2 = { custom_tooltip = { text = improved_learn_2_tt } }
				upgraded_martial = { custom_tooltip = { text = upgraded_martial_tt } }
				upgraded_diplomacy = { custom_tooltip = { text = upgraded_diplo_tt } }
				upgraded_stewardship = { custom_tooltip = { text = upgraded_stew_tt } }
				upgraded_intrigue = { custom_tooltip = { text = upgraded_int_tt } }
				upgraded_learning = { custom_tooltip = { text = upgraded_learn_tt } }
			}
		}
		clr_character_flag = child_fulfilled_destiny_flag
		clr_character_flag = child_avoided_destiny_flag
		event_target:horoscope_child = { verena_horoscope_clear_result_effect = yes }
	}
}

# verena.140 The horoscope chain
# Hidden. Moves the chain on to its next stage: the reading (verena.131),
# the child hears of it at four (132), the first signs at six (133), the
# child's choice at thirteen (134, 135) and the yearly reckoning from then
# on (141). If the parent dies on the way, the child hears of it from
# others and the parent's stages are skipped.
# Scope: the child; event target horoscope_maker.
character_event = {
	id = verena.140
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		is_alive = yes
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:horoscope_maker = { is_alive = yes } }
			trigger_switch = {
				on_trigger = has_character_flag
				horoscope_destiny_revealed = {
					clr_character_flag = horoscope_destiny_revealed
					event_target:horoscope_maker = { character_event = { id = verena.131 } }
				}
				horoscope_child_receives_horoscope = {
					if = {
						limit = { age = 4 }
						clr_character_flag = horoscope_child_receives_horoscope
						set_character_flag = horoscope_maker_receives_update
						character_event = { id = verena.132 }
					}
					else = { character_event = { id = verena.140 days = 365 } }
				}
				horoscope_maker_receives_update = {
					if = {
						limit = { age = 6 }
						clr_character_flag = horoscope_maker_receives_update
						set_character_flag = horoscope_child_contemplates_their_destiny
						event_target:horoscope_maker = { character_event = { id = verena.133 } }
					}
					else = { character_event = { id = verena.140 days = 365 } }
				}
				horoscope_child_contemplates_their_destiny = {
					verena_horoscope_contemplate_effect = yes
				}
				horoscope_parent_is_informed = {
					clr_character_flag = horoscope_parent_is_informed
					set_character_flag = horoscope_child_evaluate
					event_target:horoscope_maker = { character_event = { id = verena.135 } }
				}
				horoscope_child_evaluate = { character_event = { id = verena.141 } }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_character_flag = horoscope_maker_died
			trigger_switch = {
				on_trigger = has_character_flag
				horoscope_destiny_revealed = {
					clr_character_flag = horoscope_destiny_revealed
					clean_up_horoscope_effect = yes
				}
				horoscope_child_receives_horoscope = {
					clr_character_flag = horoscope_child_receives_horoscope
					character_event = { id = verena.132 }
				}
				horoscope_maker_receives_update = {
					clr_character_flag = horoscope_maker_receives_update
					set_character_flag = horoscope_child_contemplates_their_destiny
					character_event = { id = verena.140 days = 365 }
				}
				horoscope_child_contemplates_their_destiny = {
					verena_horoscope_contemplate_effect = yes
				}
				horoscope_parent_is_informed = {
					clr_character_flag = horoscope_parent_is_informed
					set_character_flag = horoscope_child_evaluate
					character_event = { id = verena.140 days = 365 }
				}
				horoscope_child_evaluate = { character_event = { id = verena.141 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

# verena.141 The yearly reckoning
# Hidden. From seventeen on, enough destiny points fulfil the destiny
# (verena.137) and few enough avoid it (138). Until then the points drift
# a little each year: up for a bright child, down for a dull one.
# Was MNM.1298 and 1299, merged.
# Scope: the child.
character_event = {
	id = verena.141
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { age = 17 check_variable = { which = horoscope_destiny_points value = 10 } }
			character_event = { id = verena.137 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				age = 17
				NOT = { check_variable = { which = horoscope_destiny_points value = -10 } }
			}
			character_event = { id = verena.138 }
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_dumb_trigger = no }
				change_variable = { which = horoscope_destiny_points value = 1 }
			}
			else = { subtract_variable = { which = horoscope_destiny_points value = 1 } }
			character_event = { id = verena.140 days = 365 random = 100 }
		}
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Part 8: gathering ingredients
# From the decision hermetics_acquire_ingredients_decision. A brother of the
# Order goes out for the herbs, beast parts and alchemical salts his rites
# and draughts need: into the woods with basket and sickle (stewardship), on
# the hunt (martial) or among the apothecaries of the town (diplomacy). An
# apprentice or a brother of his court may come along and helps the search,
# but he can go alone. Five tries decide how much he brings home.
###############################################################################

# verena.150 Gathering ingredients
# Picks a companion, if there is one, and the kind of search.
# Scope: ROOT is the member. From the decision.
character_event = {
	id = verena.150
	desc = {
		trigger = { NOT = { event_target:verena_gather_companion = { always = yes } } }
		text = EVTDESCverena150
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { event_target:verena_gather_companion = { always = yes } }
		text = EVTDESCverena150_companion
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_experiment
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # hermetics_acquire_ingredients_decision

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_courtier_or_vassal = {
					has_minor_title = title_hermetics_apprentice
					liege = { character = ROOT }
					prisoner = no
					is_incapable = no
				}
			}
			random_courtier_or_vassal = {
				limit = {
					has_minor_title = title_hermetics_apprentice
					liege = { character = ROOT }
					prisoner = no
					is_incapable = no
				}
				save_event_target_as = verena_gather_companion
			}
		}
		else = {
			random_courtier = {
				limit = {
					society_member_of = cult_of_verena
					is_adult = yes
					prisoner = no
					is_incapable = no
				}
				save_event_target_as = verena_gather_companion
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Into the woods
		name = EVTOPTAverena150
		set_character_flag = verena_gather_herbs
		character_event = { id = verena.151 days = 50 random = 40 tooltip = verena_gather_herbs_tt }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = gardener }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 stewardship = 12 }
		}
	}
	option = { # The hunt
		name = EVTOPTBverena150
		set_character_flag = verena_gather_beasts
		character_event = {
			id = verena.151
			days = 50
			random = 40
			tooltip = verena_gather_beasts_tt
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = hunter }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 martial = 12 }
		}
	}
	option = { # The apothecaries
		name = EVTOPTCverena150
		set_character_flag = verena_gather_market
		character_event = {
			id = verena.151
			days = 50
			random = 40
			tooltip = verena_gather_market_tt
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { wealth = 50 } }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = gregarious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 diplomacy = 12 }
		}
	}
	option = { # Not now
		name = EVTOPTDverena150
		remove_character_modifier = acquire_ingredients_decision_cooldown_timer
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}

	after = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_character_flag = verena_gather_herbs
					has_character_flag = verena_gather_beasts
					has_character_flag = verena_gather_market
				}
			}
			set_character_flag = do_not_disturb
			hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = verena.153 days = 140 } }
		}
	}
}

# verena.151 The search
# Hidden. Five tries; each finds something more often for a gatherer, and
# a companion, skilled in the search he chose, with the knack for it
# (gardener, hunter or gregarious), and less often for a drunkard.
# Scope: ROOT is the member.
character_event = {
	id = verena.151
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.150

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			has_character_flag = verena_gather_herbs
			has_character_flag = verena_gather_beasts
			has_character_flag = verena_gather_market
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = { which = verena_gather_try value = 5 }
		set_variable = { which = verena_gather_found value = 0 }
		while = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = verena_gather_try value = 1 } }
			random = {
				chance = 50
				modifier = { factor = 0.6 verena_gather_skill_low_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 1.4 verena_gather_skill_high_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 verena_gather_knack_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 0.7 trait = drunkard }
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.3
					event_target:verena_gather_companion = {
						is_alive = yes
						verena_gather_skill_low_trigger = no
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.2
					event_target:verena_gather_companion = {
						is_alive = yes
						verena_gather_skill_high_trigger = yes
					}
				}
				change_variable = { which = verena_gather_found value = 1 }
			}
			subtract_variable = { which = verena_gather_try value = 1 }
		}
		character_event = { id = verena.152 }
	}
}

# verena.152 Home from the search
# What he found: four or more, one to three, or nothing. A failed herb walk
# ends with a bad mushroom, a failed hunt with mockery, and the
# apothecaries are paid either way.
# Scope: ROOT is the member.
character_event = {
	id = verena.152
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = verena_gather_herbs
			check_variable = { which = verena_gather_found value = 4 }
		}
		picture = GFX_evt_bandits
		text = EVTDESCverena152_herbs_A
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = verena_gather_herbs
			check_variable = { which = verena_gather_found value = 1 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = verena_gather_found value = 4 } }
		}
		picture = GFX_evt_bandits
		text = EVTDESCverena152_herbs_B
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = verena_gather_herbs
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = verena_gather_found value = 1 } }
		}
		picture = GFX_evt_bandits
		text = EVTDESCverena152_herbs_C
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = verena_gather_beasts
			check_variable = { which = verena_gather_found value = 4 }
		}
		picture = GFX_evt_hunt
		text = EVTDESCverena152_beasts_A
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = verena_gather_beasts
			check_variable = { which = verena_gather_found value = 1 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = verena_gather_found value = 4 } }
		}
		picture = GFX_evt_hunt
		text = EVTDESCverena152_beasts_B
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = verena_gather_beasts
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = verena_gather_found value = 1 } }
		}
		picture = GFX_evt_hunt
		text = EVTDESCverena152_beasts_C
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = verena_gather_market
			check_variable = { which = verena_gather_found value = 4 }
		}
		picture = GFX_evt_market
		text = EVTDESCverena152_market_A
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = verena_gather_market
			check_variable = { which = verena_gather_found value = 1 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = verena_gather_found value = 4 } }
		}
		picture = GFX_evt_market
		text = EVTDESCverena152_market_B
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = verena_gather_market
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = verena_gather_found value = 1 } }
		}
		picture = GFX_evt_market
		text = EVTDESCverena152_market_C
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_experiment
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.151

	option = { # A full basket
		name = EVTOPTAverena152
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = verena_gather_found value = 4 } }
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_gather_found_tt }
		add_society_currency_trivial_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = verena_gather_market }
			scaled_wealth = { value = -0.3 min = -35 }
		}
	}
	option = { # Something at least
		name = EVTOPTBverena152
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = verena_gather_found value = 1 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = verena_gather_found value = 4 } }
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_gather_found_tt }
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = verena_gather_market }
			scaled_wealth = { value = -0.2 min = -25 }
		}
	}
	option = { # Nothing
		name = EVTOPTCverena152
		trigger = { NOT = { check_variable = { which = verena_gather_found value = 1 } } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = verena_gather_herbs }
			random = {
				chance = 35
				if = { limit = { has_dlc = "Reapers" } give_food_poisoning_effect = yes }
				else_if = { limit = { is_disease_immune_race = no } add_trait = ill }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = verena_gather_beasts }
			prestige = -25
			add_character_modifier = { modifier = disappointing_hunt duration = 1095 }
		}
		else = { scaled_wealth = { value = -0.1 min = -15 } }
	}

	after = {
		while = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = verena_gather_found value = 1 } }
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = verena_gather_herbs }
				hermetic_add_herbal_ingredient_effect = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = verena_gather_beasts }
				hermetic_add_animal_ingredient_effect = yes
			}
			else = { hermetic_add_misc_ingredient_effect = yes }
			subtract_variable = { which = verena_gather_found value = 1 }
		}
		event_target:verena_gather_companion = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_alive = yes has_minor_title = title_hermetics_apprentice }
				opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_grateful years = 5 }
			}
		}
		clear_delayed_event = { id = verena.153 }
		character_event = { id = verena.153 }
	}
}

# verena.153 The search is over
# Hidden. Clears the search, also as a safety catch 140 days after it began.
# Scope: ROOT is the member.
character_event = {
	id = verena.153
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.150, verena.152

	immediate = {
		clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb
		clr_character_flag = verena_gather_herbs
		clr_character_flag = verena_gather_beasts
		clr_character_flag = verena_gather_market
		set_variable = { which = verena_gather_try value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = verena_gather_found value = 0 }
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Part 9: the draught against melancholy
# From the decision brew_happiness_potion. A priest of the Order of Mysteries
# reads the Principia Herbologium and the sign of Mammit the Wise, who rules
# the mind, and brews a draught for himself or someone close to him who is
# stressed or melancholy. Healthy ingredients make a lasting cure; a bad
# brew lets the gloom come back a few years later.
###############################################################################

# verena.160 Brewing the draught
# He chooses which of his ingredients go into the pot; the quality of the
# brew (the variable current_potion_quality) follows from them.
# Scope: ROOT is the brewer; the event target potion_target is the patient
# when it is not himself (flag potion_target_is_self).
character_event = {
	id = verena.160
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = potion_target_is_self } text = EVTDESCverena160_self }
	desc = {
		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = potion_target_is_self } }
		text = EVTDESCverena160_other
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_experiment
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # brew_happiness_potion

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = verena_potion_maker
		select_random_hermetic_ingredient_effect = yes
		select_random_hermetic_ingredient_effect = yes
	}

	option = { # The first ingredient
		name = {
			trigger = { has_selected_herb_ingredient_1_trigger = yes }
			text = EVTOPTAverena160_herb
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { has_selected_animal_part_ingredient_1_trigger = yes }
			text = EVTOPTAverena160_animal_part
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { has_selected_powder_ingredient_1_trigger = yes }
			text = EVTOPTAverena160_powder
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { has_selected_liquid_ingredient_1_trigger = yes }
			text = EVTOPTAverena160_liquid
		}
		trigger = { selected_ingredient_1_and_2_are_same = no }
		calculate_health_level_of_ingredient_1_effect = yes
		destroy_selected_ingredient_1_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
	option = { # The second ingredient
		name = {
			trigger = { has_selected_herb_ingredient_2_trigger = yes }
			text = EVTOPTBverena160_herb
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { has_selected_animal_part_ingredient_2_trigger = yes }
			text = EVTOPTBverena160_animal_part
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { has_selected_powder_ingredient_2_trigger = yes }
			text = EVTOPTBverena160_powder
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { has_selected_liquid_ingredient_2_trigger = yes }
			text = EVTOPTBverena160_liquid
		}
		trigger = {
			has_selected_ingredient_2_trigger = yes
			selected_ingredient_1_and_2_are_same = no
		}
		calculate_health_level_of_ingredient_2_effect = yes
		destroy_selected_ingredient_2_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
	option = { # Just one of the pair
		name = EVTOPTCverena160
		trigger = {
			has_selected_ingredient_2_trigger = yes
			selected_ingredient_1_and_2_are_same = yes
		}
		calculate_health_level_of_ingredient_1_effect = yes
		destroy_selected_ingredient_1_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
	option = { # Both
		name = { trigger = { selected_ingredient_1_and_2_are_same = no } text = EVTOPTDverena160 }
		name = {
			trigger = { selected_ingredient_1_and_2_are_same = yes }
			text = EVTOPTDverena160_pair
		}
		trigger = { has_selected_ingredient_2_trigger = yes }
		calculate_health_level_of_ingredient_1_and_2_effect = yes
		destroy_selected_ingredient_1_and_2_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { factor = 25 }
	}
	option = { # A bit of everything
		name = EVTOPTEverena160
		trigger = { any_artifact = { count = 4 has_artifact_flag = ingredient } }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTEverena160_tt }
		calculate_health_level_of_all_ingredients_effect = yes
		destroy_all_ingredients_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}

	after = {
		remove_selection_of_ingredients_effect = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = potion_target_is_self }
				character_event = { id = verena.161 days = 15 }
			}
			else = {
				clr_character_flag = preparing_potion
				event_target:potion_target = { character_event = { id = verena.162 days = 10 } }
			}
			clr_character_flag = potion_target_is_self
		}
	}
}

# verena.161 I drink the draught
# Scope: ROOT is the brewer, who is also the patient.

		}
	}

# verena.162 A draught is offered
# The patient may drink it or send it back; the brewer's own liege always
# drinks it, and the zealous trust prayer over potions.
# Scope: ROOT is the patient, FROM the brewer.
character_event = {
	id = verena.162
	desc = EVTDESCverena162
	picture = GFX_evt_experiment
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.160

	option = { # Drink it
		name = EVTOPTAverena162
		verena_potion_cure_effect = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			verena_potion_relapse_effect = yes
			FROM = {
				set_variable = { which = current_potion_quality value = 0 }
				character_event = { id = verena.163 days = 5 }
			}
		}
		reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_grateful years = 5 }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 3 society_member_of = cult_of_verena }
			modifier = { factor = 2 opinion = { who = FROM value = 25 } }
		}
	}
	option = { # Send it back
		name = EVTOPTBverena162
		reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_ungrateful years = 5 }
		hidden_effect = { FROM = { set_variable = { which = current_potion_quality value = 0 } } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 3 trait = zealous }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = paranoid }
			modifier = { factor = 0 liege = { character = FROM } }
		}
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Part 10: the three mysteries
# From the decision choose_hermetic_art (High Priests of the Order; the
# runesmiths have their own in WHG_dwarf_guild_events). A High Priest gives
# himself to one of three mysteries: the stars, where the Lorekeepers read
# the twenty signs of the Old World (Scholar), the alembic of the learned
# alchemists of Nuln (Mystic), or the Goddess Herself, as a Lorekeeper who
# guards what was written or a Scalebearer who weighs the truth in
# judgement (Theologian). A first choice goes through a short study with a
# ten-year modifier; a later one just swaps.
###############################################################################

# verena.170 The three mysteries
# Scope: ROOT is the High Priest.
character_event = {
	id = verena.170
	desc = EVTDESCverena170
	picture = GFX_evt_experiment
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # choose_hermetic_art

	# One time in four a voice in the margins offers a shortcut (verena.178).
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = verena_daemon_refused }
				NOT = { trait = zealous }
			}
			random = {
				chance = 25
				set_character_flag = verena_daemon_whisper
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # The stars
		name = EVTOPTAverena170
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = verena_mystery_stars_tt
			character_event = { id = verena.172 days = 10 random = 20 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 3 trait = erudite }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = patient }
		}
	}
	option = { # The alembic
		name = EVTOPTBverena170
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = verena_mystery_alembic_tt
			character_event = { id = verena.174 days = 10 random = 20 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 3 trait = cynical }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = gardener }
		}
	}
	option = { # The Goddess
		name = EVTOPTCverena170
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = verena_mystery_goddess_tt
			character_event = { id = verena.176 days = 10 random = 20 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 5 trait = zealous }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = just }
		}
	}
	option = { # The voice in the margins
		name = EVTOPTEverena170
		trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_daemon_whisper }
		clr_character_flag = verena_daemon_whisper
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = verena_mystery_daemon_tt
			character_event = { id = verena.178 days = 5 random = 10 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 0.2
			modifier = { factor = 5 trait = ambitious }
			modifier = { factor = 3 trait = cynical }
			modifier = { factor = 0 trait = zealous }
		}
	}
	option = { # Not yet
		name = EVTOPTDverena170
		clr_character_flag = verena_daemon_whisper
		add_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
		clr_character_flag = choosing_hermetic_art
	}
}

# verena.171 Another mystery
# Swaps one path trait for another.
# Scope: ROOT is the High Priest.
character_event = {
	id = verena.171
	desc = { trigger = { trait = scholar } picture = GFX_evt_comet text = EVTDESCverena171_scholar }
	desc = {
		trigger = { trait = mystic }
		picture = GFX_evt_experiment
		text = EVTDESCverena171_mystic
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { trait = theologian }
		picture = GFX_evt_secluded_in_prayer
		text = EVTDESCverena171_theologian
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_experiment
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # choose_hermetic_art

	option = { # The stars
		name = EVTOPTAverena171
		trigger = { NOT = { trait = scholar } }
		remove_trait = mystic
		remove_trait = theologian
		add_trait = scholar
		clr_character_flag = choosing_hermetic_art
	}
	option = { # The alembic
		name = EVTOPTBverena171
		trigger = { NOT = { trait = mystic } }
		remove_trait = scholar
		remove_trait = theologian
		add_trait = mystic
		clr_character_flag = choosing_hermetic_art
	}
	option = { # The Goddess
		name = EVTOPTCverena171
		trigger = { NOT = { trait = theologian } }
		remove_trait = scholar
		remove_trait = mystic
		add_trait = theologian
		clr_character_flag = choosing_hermetic_art
	}
	option = { # Keep my mystery
		name = EVTOPTDverena171
		add_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
		clr_character_flag = choosing_hermetic_art
	}
}

# verena.172 The mystery of the stars
# Build an observatory on the temple roof (paid once), or read the star
# charts of the old masters in the Order's libraries.
# Scope: ROOT is the High Priest.
character_event = {
	id = verena.172
	desc = EVTDESCverena172
	picture = GFX_evt_comet
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.170

	option = { # The observatory
		name = EVTOPTAverena172
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = owns_observatory_equipment }
			custom_tooltip = { text = CUSTOM_TOOLTIP_OWNS_OBSERVATORY_EQUIPMENT }
			prestige = 25
		}
		else = {
			custom_tooltip = { text = CUSTOM_TOOLTIP_BUY_OBSERVATORY_EQUIPMENT }
			set_character_flag = owns_observatory_equipment
			scaled_wealth = { value = -0.4 min = -40 }
		}
		if = { limit = { trait = slothful } remove_trait = slothful }
		else_if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = diligent } } add_trait = diligent }
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = stargazer duration = 3650 }
		character_event = { id = verena.173 days = 60 random = 30 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
	option = { # The old star charts
		name = EVTOPTBverena172
		random_list = {
			50 = { change_learning = 1 }
			40 = { modifier = { factor = 0 trait = erudite } add_trait = erudite }
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = shrewd trait = dull } }
				add_trait = shrewd
			}
		}
		character_event = { id = verena.173 days = 60 random = 30 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 modifier = { factor = 2 trait = erudite } }
	}
}
