###############################################################################
# Part 11: the apprenticeship
# An apprentice of the Order (the minor title title_hermetics_apprentice,
# granted by a player member) serves twelve to fifteen years. Then he leaves
# with what his master taught him, and the master gains standing in the
# Order. If he dies first, the master is told; if the master dies or leaves
# the Order, his apprentices are released.
###############################################################################

# verena.178 The voice in the margins
# A daemon of the Changer offers the three mysteries at once, for a small
# favour later. Take it (the price comes in one to two years, verena.179) or
# drive it off and choose honestly (verena.170 again, the offer gone).
# Scope: ROOT is the High Priest.
character_event = {
	id = verena.178
	desc = EVTDESCverena178
	picture = GFX_evt_dark_prayer
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.170

	option = { # The bargain
		name = EVTOPTAverena178
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 30
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = ambitious }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = { limit = { lifestyle_traits = 1 } remove_lifestyle_trait_effect = yes }
		}
		add_trait = mystic
		change_learning = 2
		set_character_flag = verena_daemon_debt
		clr_character_flag = choosing_hermetic_art
		hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = verena.179 days = 365 random = 365 } }
	}
	option = { # Begone
		name = EVTOPTBverena178
		ai_chance = { factor = 70 }
		set_character_flag = verena_daemon_refused
		piety = 25
		character_event = { id = verena.170 days = 3 }
	}
}

# verena.179 The price
# The daemon returns for its favour. Serve the Changer (secretly join the
# Cult of Tzeentch, a road into Chaos), or refuse the debt: half the time
# the gift was a trick and is taken back with interest, half the time the
# daemon starts to whisper (chaoscult.494).
# Scope: ROOT is the High Priest (flag verena_daemon_debt).
character_event = {
	id = verena.179
	desc = EVTDESCverena179
	picture = GFX_evt_dark_prayer
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.178

	trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_daemon_debt }

	option = { # Serve the Changer
		name = EVTOPTAverena179
		trigger = { NOT = { religion = tzeentch } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 30
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = ambitious }
			modifier = { factor = 0.2 trait = zealous }
		}
		clr_character_flag = verena_daemon_debt
		if = {
			limit = { is_in_society = yes }
			leave_society = yes
		}
		join_society = cult_of_tzeentch
		set_secret_religion = tzeentch
	}
	option = { # Refuse
		name = EVTOPTBverena179
		ai_chance = { factor = 70 }
		clr_character_flag = verena_daemon_debt
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = verena179_tricked_tt }
				remove_trait = mystic
				change_learning = -3
				add_trait = stressed
			}
			50 = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = verena179_whispers_tt }
				hidden_effect = {
					set_character_flag = chaoscult_soul_whispers
					character_event = { id = chaoscult.494 days = 30 random = 30 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# verena.180 The apprenticeship ends
# Hidden. Bounces to the master, if he is still the apprentice's liege and a
# brother of the Order.
# Scope: ROOT is the apprentice. From the gain_effect of the title, 4500 -
#   5500 days after it was granted.
character_event = {
	id = verena.180
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes # title_hermetics_apprentice gain_effect

	trigger = {
		has_minor_title = title_hermetics_apprentice
		liege = { is_alive = yes society_member_of = cult_of_verena }
	}

	immediate = {
		liege = { character_event = { id = verena.181 } }
	}
}

# verena.181 Go with the Owl
# The apprentice is ready. He leaves with two points of learning, and the
# master gains standing in the Order.
# Scope: ROOT is the master, FROM the apprentice.
character_event = {
	id = verena.181
	desc = EVTDESCverena181
	picture = GFX_evt_gathering_of_minds
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.180

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAverena181
		add_society_currency_major_effect = yes
		FROM = {
			set_character_flag = completed_apprenticeship
			remove_title = title_hermetics_apprentice
			change_learning = 2
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_grateful years = 10 }
		}
	}
}

# verena.184 The apprentices are released
# Hidden. A master who dies or leaves the Order releases his apprentices.
# Scope: ROOT is the master. From on_death and on_character_leave_society.
character_event = {
	id = verena.184
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_death, on_character_leave_society
	only_rulers = yes

	trigger = {
		any_courtier_or_vassal = { has_minor_title = title_hermetics_apprentice }
	}

	immediate = {
		any_courtier_or_vassal = {
			limit = { has_minor_title = title_hermetics_apprentice }
			remove_title = title_hermetics_apprentice
		}
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Part 12: watching the stars
# A mission from verena.1. A brother of the Order asks the member to watch
# the night sky of the Old World for a season. Each night brings him closer
# to an insight (the modifiers astronomical_insights_1 - 4), and costs him
# something at home: a neglected spouse, a child frightened of Morrslieb, a
# missed feast, unread accounts, a council he sleeps through. When he stops,
# the sky may show him the dance of the two moons, a falling star, a new
# star to name, the twenty signs of the Old World, or nothing at all.
###############################################################################

# verena.200 The mission is sent
# Hidden. Bounces from the mission giver to the member.
# Scope: ROOT is the mission giver, FROM the member. From verena.1.
character_event = {
	id = verena.200
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.1

	trigger = {
		FROM = { is_alive = yes society_member_of = cult_of_verena has_any_quest = no }
	}

	immediate = {
		FROM = { society_quest_event = { id = verena.201 } }
	}
}

# verena.201 Mission: watch the stars
# Scope: ROOT is the member, FROM the mission giver.
society_quest_event = {
	id = verena.201
	desc = EVTDESCverena201

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.200

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAverena201
		set_quest = quest_verena_watch_stars
		hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = verena.202 days = 5 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBverena201
		custom_tooltip = { text = decline_quest_tooltip }
		add_character_modifier = { name = quest_cooldown_timer hidden = yes days = 1000 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

# verena.202 The first night
# Scope: ROOT is the member.
character_event = {
	id = verena.202
	desc = EVTDESCverena202
	picture = GFX_evt_book_carolingian_script
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.201

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAverena202
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = astronomical_insights_1 duration = -1 }
		hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = verena.203 days = 40 random = 15 } }
	}
}

# verena.203 Another night
# Hidden. Picks what this night costs him.
# Scope: ROOT is the member.
character_event = {
	id = verena.203
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.202, verena.205

	trigger = {
		has_quest = quest_verena_watch_stars
	}

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			30 = { # A good night
				character_event = { id = verena.210 }
			}
			30 = { # The spouse
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					NOT = {
						any_spouse = { NOT = { character = event_target:hermetic_collaborator } }
					}
				}
				character_event = { id = verena.211 }
			}
			30 = { # A child who cannot sleep
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					NOT = { any_child = { is_alive = yes age = 4 NOT = { age = 14 } } }
				}
				character_event = { id = verena.212 }
			}
			30 = { # The accounts
				modifier = { factor = 0 is_ruler = no }
				character_event = { id = verena.213 }
			}
			30 = { # A friend's feast
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { any_friend = { always = yes } } }
				character_event = { id = verena.214 }
			}
			30 = { # The council
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					NOT = { any_courtier_or_vassal = { count = 2 has_job_title = yes } }
				}
				character_event = { id = verena.215 }
			}
			30 = { # The cabinet
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					NOR = {
						has_a_herb_ingredient_trigger = yes
						has_an_animal_part_ingredient_trigger = yes
						has_a_powder_ingredient_trigger = yes
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					NOT = { any_artifact = { count = 2 has_artifact_flag = ingredient } }
				}
				character_event = { id = verena.216 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# verena.204 The last night
# Hidden. Picks what the sky shows him when he stops; the deeper his insight,
# the less likely it shows nothing.
# Scope: ROOT is the member.
character_event = {
	id = verena.204
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.205

	trigger = {
		has_quest = quest_verena_watch_stars
	}

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			30 = { character_event = { id = verena.220 } } # The two moons
			30 = { character_event = { id = verena.221 } } # A falling star
			30 = { character_event = { id = verena.222 } } # A new star
			30 = { character_event = { id = verena.224 } } # The twenty signs
			120 = { # Nothing
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 has_character_modifier = astronomical_insights_2 }
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 has_character_modifier = astronomical_insights_3 }
				modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_character_modifier = astronomical_insights_4 }
				character_event = { id = verena.225 }
			}
		}
	}
}

		trigger = { has_character_modifier = astronomical_insights_2 }
		text = EVTDESCverena205_B

		trigger = { has_character_modifier = astronomical_insights_3 }
		text = EVTDESCverena205_C

		trigger = { has_character_modifier = astronomical_insights_4 }
		text = EVTDESCverena205_D

	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

			trigger = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = astronomical_insights_4 } }
			text = EVTOPTBverena205
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { has_character_modifier = astronomical_insights_4 }
			text = EVTOPTCverena205
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBverena205_tt }
		hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = verena.204 days = 20 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}

		name = EVTOPTBverena210
		trigger = { trait = slothful }
		random = { chance = 30 remove_trait_slothful_effect = yes }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCverena210
		trigger = { trait = depressed }
		random = { chance = 30 remove_trait = depressed }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDverena210
		trigger = { trait = envious }
		random = { chance = 30 remove_trait = envious }
	}

	after = {
		increase_astronomical_insights_modifier_effect = yes
		hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = verena.205 days = 5 } }
	}
}

# verena.211 The cold side of the bed
# His spouse has had enough of a husband (or wife) on the roof.
# Scope: ROOT is the member.
character_event = {
	id = verena.211
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				event_target:verena_star_spouse = { verena_out_of_touch_with_root_trigger = yes }
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena211_A
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:verena_star_spouse = { verena_out_of_touch_with_root_trigger = yes }
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena211_B
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_quarrel
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.203

	immediate = {
		random_spouse = {
			limit = { NOT = { character = event_target:hermetic_collaborator } }
			save_event_target_as = verena_star_spouse
		}
	}

	option = { # The stars come first
		name = {
			trigger = {
				NOT = {
					event_target:verena_star_spouse = {
						verena_out_of_touch_with_root_trigger = yes
					}
				}
			}
			text = EVTOPTAverena211
		}
		name = {
			trigger = {
				event_target:verena_star_spouse = { verena_out_of_touch_with_root_trigger = yes }
			}
			text = EVTOPTAverena211_again
		}
		increase_astronomical_insights_modifier_effect = yes
		event_target:verena_star_spouse = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_lover = ROOT }
				random = {
					chance = 35
					modifier = { factor = 2 verena_out_of_touch_with_root_trigger = yes }
					remove_lover = ROOT
				}
			}
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_out_of_touch years = 10 }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 modifier = { factor = 2 trait = ambitious } }
	}
	option = { # Come down from the roof
		name = {
			trigger = {
				NOT = {
					event_target:verena_star_spouse = {
						verena_out_of_touch_with_root_trigger = yes
					}
				}
			}
			text = EVTOPTBverena211
		}
		name = {
			trigger = {
				event_target:verena_star_spouse = { verena_out_of_touch_with_root_trigger = yes }
			}
			text = EVTOPTBverena211_again
		}
		event_target:verena_star_spouse = {
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_relieved years = 10 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 2 is_lover = event_target:verena_star_spouse }
		}
	}

	after = {
		hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = verena.205 days = 5 } }
	}
}

# verena.212 Afraid of the green moon
# A child cannot sleep for fear of Morrslieb. Send it away, sit with it
# and tell it of Verena's owl, or brew it a sleeping draught from the
# cabinet.
# Scope: ROOT is the member.
character_event = {
	id = verena.212
	desc = EVTDESCverena212
	picture = GFX_evt_daughter_asking_father
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.203

	immediate = {
		random_child = {
			limit = { is_alive = yes age = 4 NOT = { age = 14 } }
			save_event_target_as = verena_star_child
		}
		verena_select_ingredient_by_kind_effect = yes
	}

	option = { # Back to bed
		name = EVTOPTAverena212
		increase_astronomical_insights_modifier_effect = yes
		event_target:verena_star_child = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_dlc = "Conclave" }
				random_list = {
					20 = { add_trait = timid }
					20 = { add_trait = indolent }
					20 = { add_trait = fussy }
					40 = { }
				}
			}
			else = {
				random_list = {
					20 = { add_trait = slothful }
					20 = { add_trait = envious }
					20 = { add_trait = arbitrary }
					40 = { }
				}
			}
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_bad_parent years = 25 }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
	option = { # A tale of the owl
		name = EVTOPTBverena212
		event_target:verena_star_child = {
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = comforted_opinion years = 5 }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 modifier = { factor = 2 trait = kind } }
	}
	option = { # A sleeping draught
		name = {
			trigger = { has_selected_herb_ingredient_1_trigger = yes }
			text = EVTOPTCverena212_herb
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { has_selected_animal_part_ingredient_1_trigger = yes }
			text = EVTOPTCverena212_animal_part
		}
		name = {
			trigger = {
				OR = {
					has_selected_powder_ingredient_1_trigger = yes
					has_selected_liquid_ingredient_1_trigger = yes
				}
			}
			text = EVTOPTCverena212_other
		}
		trigger = { has_selected_ingredient_1_trigger = yes }
		tooltip_info = yes
		custom_tooltip = { text = ingredient_option_available_tt }
		increase_astronomical_insights_modifier_effect = yes
		event_target:verena_star_child = {
			if = {
				limit = { ROOT = { has_selected_herb_ingredient_1_trigger = yes } }
				opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = comforted_opinion years = 5 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { ROOT = { has_selected_animal_part_ingredient_1_trigger = yes } }
				opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_cheered_up years = 2 }
			}
			else = { add_character_modifier = { modifier = nauseous_from_potion years = 1 } }
		}
		destroy_selected_ingredient_1_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}

	after = {
		hidden_effect = {
			remove_selection_of_ingredients_effect = yes
			character_event = { id = verena.205 days = 5 }
		}
	}
}

				limit = { ROOT = { has_selected_liquid_ingredient_1_trigger = yes } }

			}
			else = { opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_cheered_up years = 5 } }
		}

		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }

		}

# verena.215 Asleep at the council table
# He nods off in council and, half awake, dismisses the wrong councillor,
# or wakes up and has to make amends to the one who woke him.
# Scope: ROOT is the member, a ruler.
character_event = {
	id = verena.215
	desc = EVTDESCverena215
	picture = GFX_evt_state_diplomacy
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.203

	immediate = {
		random_courtier_or_vassal = {
			limit = { has_job_title = yes }
			save_event_target_as = verena_star_councillor_1
		}
		random_courtier_or_vassal = {
			limit = {
				has_job_title = yes
				NOT = { character = event_target:verena_star_councillor_1 }
			}
			save_event_target_as = verena_star_councillor_2
		}
	}

	option = { # So be it
		name = EVTOPTAverena215
		increase_astronomical_insights_modifier_effect = yes
		event_target:verena_star_councillor_2 = {
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_fired_from_council years = 5 }
			trigger_switch = {
				on_trigger = has_job_title
				job_chancellor = { remove_title = job_chancellor }
				job_marshal = { remove_title = job_marshal }
				job_treasurer = { remove_title = job_treasurer }
				job_spymaster = { remove_title = job_spymaster }
				job_spiritual = { remove_title = job_spiritual }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 0.5 }
	}
	option = { # Undo it
		name = EVTOPTBverena215
		event_target:verena_star_councillor_1 = {
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_angry years = 5 }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}

	after = {
		hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = verena.205 days = 5 } }
	}
}

# verena.216 The cabinet
# The old astrologers burned herbs, cooked broths and slept on powders to
# see further. He may try one, or two together, at some risk.
# Scope: ROOT is the member.
character_event = {
	id = verena.216
	desc = EVTDESCverena216
	picture = GFX_evt_experiment
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.203

	immediate = {
		select_random_hermetic_ingredient_herb_effect = yes
		select_random_hermetic_ingredient_animal_part_effect = yes
		select_random_hermetic_ingredient_powder_effect = yes
		select_random_hermetic_ingredient_liquid_effect = yes
	}

	option = { # Burn the herb
		name = EVTOPTAverena216
		trigger = { has_selected_herb_ingredient_1_trigger = yes }
		random = { chance = 30 increase_astronomical_insights_modifier_effect = yes }
		destroy_selected_ingredient_1_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
	option = { # A broth
		name = EVTOPTBverena216
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_selected_animal_part_ingredient_1_trigger = yes
				has_selected_animal_part_ingredient_2_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		random = { chance = 40 increase_astronomical_insights_modifier_effect = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { has_selected_animal_part_ingredient_1_trigger = yes }
			destroy_selected_ingredient_1_effect = yes
		}
		else = { destroy_selected_ingredient_2_effect = yes }
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
	option = { # Under the pillow
		name = EVTOPTCverena216
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_selected_powder_ingredient_1_trigger = yes
				has_selected_powder_ingredient_2_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		random = { chance = 50 increase_astronomical_insights_modifier_effect = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { has_selected_powder_ingredient_1_trigger = yes }
			destroy_selected_ingredient_1_effect = yes
		}
		else = { destroy_selected_ingredient_2_effect = yes }
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
	option = { # Both at once
		name = EVTOPTDverena216
		trigger = { has_selected_ingredient_2_trigger = yes }
		tooltip_info = yes
		custom_tooltip = { text = multiple_ingredient_option_available_tt }
		random_list = {
			85 = { increase_astronomical_insights_modifier_effect = yes }
			15 = { add_maimed_trait_effect = yes }
		}
		destroy_selected_ingredient_1_and_2_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 modifier = { factor = 0 trait = craven } }
	}
	option = { # Better not
		name = EVTOPTEverena216
		ai_chance = { factor = 0.5 }
	}

	after = {
		hidden_effect = {
			remove_selection_of_ingredients_effect = yes
			character_event = { id = verena.205 days = 5 }
		}
	}
}

# verena.221 A falling star
# He reckons when the next star will fall, and may invite the court to see
# it (verena.226).
# Scope: ROOT is the member.
character_event = {
	id = verena.221
	desc = EVTDESCverena221
	picture = GFX_evt_comet
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.204

	option = { # Invite the court
		name = EVTOPTAverena221
		set_character_flag = verena_star_fall_court
		character_event = { id = verena.226 days = 190 random = 70 tooltip = verena_star_fall_tt }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = proud }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = gregarious }
		}
	}
	option = { # Watch alone
		name = EVTOPTBverena221
		character_event = { id = verena.226 days = 190 random = 70 tooltip = verena_star_fall_tt }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = humble }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = shy }
		}
	}
}

# verena.222 A new star
# He names it after a child, his spouse, a friend or himself. A player
# namesake is told (verena.223).
# Scope: ROOT is the member.
character_event = {
	id = verena.222
	desc = EVTDESCverena222
	picture = GFX_evt_comet
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.204

	immediate = {
		random_child = {
			limit = { is_alive = yes NOT = { has_character_modifier = got_named_star } }
			save_event_target_as = verena_star_child
		}
		random_spouse = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = got_named_star } }
			save_event_target_as = verena_star_spouse
		}
		random_friend = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = got_named_star } }
			save_event_target_as = verena_star_friend
		}
	}

	option = { # My child
		name = EVTOPTAverena222
		trigger = { event_target:verena_star_child = { always = yes } }
		event_target:verena_star_child = {
			add_character_modifier = { modifier = got_named_star duration = -1 }
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_gift_from_above years = 20 }
			if = { limit = { ai = no } character_event = { id = verena.223 } }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
	option = { # My spouse
		name = EVTOPTBverena222
		trigger = { event_target:verena_star_spouse = { always = yes } }
		event_target:verena_star_spouse = {
			add_character_modifier = { modifier = got_named_star duration = -1 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					opinion = { who = ROOT value = 20 }
					NOT = { is_lover = ROOT }
					is_no_females_race = no
					ROOT = { is_no_females_race = no }
				}
				add_lover = ROOT
			}
			else = { opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_gift_from_above years = 20 } }
			if = { limit = { ai = no } character_event = { id = verena.223 } }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
	option = { # A friend
		name = EVTOPTCverena222
		trigger = { event_target:verena_star_friend = { always = yes } }
		event_target:verena_star_friend = {
			add_character_modifier = { modifier = got_named_star duration = -1 }
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_gift_from_above years = 20 }
			if = { limit = { ai = no } character_event = { id = verena.223 } }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
	option = { # Myself
		name = EVTOPTDverena222
		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = got_named_star } }
		add_trait = proud
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = got_named_star duration = -1 }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 3 trait = proud }
			modifier = { factor = 0 trait = humble }
		}
	}
	option = { # Leave it nameless
		name = EVTOPTEverena222
		trigger = { has_character_modifier = got_named_star }
		prestige = 25
		ai_chance = { factor = 0.1 }
	}

	after = {
		verena_star_quest_success_effect = yes
	}
}

# verena.226 The night of the falling star
# The star falls when he said it would, or it does not; with the court
# watching, either one is louder.
# Scope: ROOT is the member.
long_character_event = {
	id = verena.226
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = verena_star_fall_seen
			has_character_flag = verena_star_fall_court
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena226_court_seen
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = verena_star_fall_seen
			NOT = { has_character_flag = verena_star_fall_court }
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena226_alone_seen
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_character_flag = verena_star_fall_seen }
			has_character_flag = verena_star_fall_court
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena226_court_missed
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_character_flag = verena_star_fall_seen }
			NOT = { has_character_flag = verena_star_fall_court }
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena226_alone_missed
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_comet
	border = GFX_event_long_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.221

	immediate = {
		random = {
			chance = 50
			modifier = { factor = 1.3 has_character_modifier = astronomical_insights_2 }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_character_modifier = astronomical_insights_3 }
			modifier = { factor = 1.8 has_character_modifier = astronomical_insights_4 }
			set_character_flag = verena_star_fall_seen
		}
	}

	option = { # It falls
		name = EVTOPTAverena226
		trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_star_fall_seen }
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = verena_star_fall_court }
			add_trait = proud
			prestige = 100
		}
		verena_star_quest_success_effect = yes
	}
	option = { # It does not
		name = EVTOPTBverena226
		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = verena_star_fall_seen } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = verena_star_fall_court }
			add_character_modifier = { modifier = failed_to_predict_star_fall years = 5 }
		}
		clr_quest = { id = quest_verena_watch_stars failure = yes }
		add_mission_fail_influence_effect = yes
		remove_astronomical_insight_modifier_effect = yes
	}

	after = {
		clr_character_flag = verena_star_fall_seen
		clr_character_flag = verena_star_fall_court
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Part 13: the cabinet of ingredients
# What happens to a cabinet of ingredients when its owner dies, and the
# folk charms a brother of the Order may hang about his chambers against
# the dark. The Lorekeepers call it superstition, and frown.
###############################################################################

# verena.230 Inherited ingredients
# Hidden. An AI heir outside the Order throws them out at once; a player
# looks through them ten days later, when the whole cabinet has come.
# Scope: ROOT is the heir, FROM the artifact. From on_artifact_inheritance.
character_event = {
	id = verena.230
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_artifact_inheritance

	trigger = {
		FROM = { has_artifact_flag = ingredient }
		NOT = { has_character_flag = dealing_with_inherited_ingredients }
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { ai = yes NOT = { society_member_of = cult_of_verena } }
			FROM = { destroy_artifact = yes }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { ai = no }
			set_character_flag = dealing_with_inherited_ingredients
			character_event = { id = verena.231 days = 10 }
		}
	}
}

# verena.231 The whole cabinet has come
# Hidden. Shows verena.232 if there are at least two ingredients to sort.
# Scope: ROOT is the heir.
character_event = {
	id = verena.231
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.230

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { any_artifact = { count = 2 has_artifact_flag = ingredient } }
			character_event = { id = verena.232 }
		}
		else = { clr_character_flag = dealing_with_inherited_ingredients }
	}
}

# verena.232 The dead man's cabinet
# Keep the ingredients, give a few to the temple, or give them all away. A
# brother of the Order can give them to the Order instead.
# Scope: ROOT is the heir.
character_event = {
	id = verena.232
	desc = { trigger = { NOT = { age = 16 } } text = EVTDESCverena232_child }
	desc = {
		trigger = { age = 16 society_member_of = cult_of_verena }
		text = EVTDESCverena232_order
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { age = 16 is_in_society = yes NOT = { society_member_of = cult_of_verena } }
		text = EVTDESCverena232_other_society
	}
	desc = { trigger = { age = 16 is_in_society = no } text = EVTDESCverena232_no_society }
	picture = GFX_evt_experiment
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.231

	immediate = {
		select_random_hermetic_ingredient_effect = yes
		select_random_hermetic_ingredient_effect = yes
	}

	option = { # A few to the temple
		name = EVTOPTAverena232
		destroy_selected_ingredient_1_and_2_effect = yes
		piety = 25
	}
	option = { # All of it to the temple
		name = EVTOPTBverena232
		destroy_all_ingredients_effect = yes
		piety = 25
		prestige = 25
	}
	option = { # To the Order
		name = EVTOPTCverena232
		trigger = { society_member_of = cult_of_verena }
		destroy_all_ingredients_effect = yes
		add_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
	}
	option = { # Keep it
		name = EVTOPTDverena232
	}

	after = {
		remove_selection_of_ingredients_effect = yes
		clr_character_flag = dealing_with_inherited_ingredients
	}
}

# verena.233 Charms against the dark
# A brother of the Order with a full cabinet may hang charms about his
# chambers against witches and worse: one, two or everything he has. It
# makes his chambers harder to reach for plotters, for a while, and costs
# him piety: Verena's priests call such things superstition.
# Scope: ROOT is a member. From on_society_bi_yearly_pulse.
character_event = {
	id = verena.233
	desc = EVTDESCverena233
	picture = GFX_evt_shady_meeting
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_society_bi_yearly_pulse
	is_in_society = yes
	capable_only = yes

	trigger = {
		society_member_of = cult_of_verena
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = verena_warded_chambers }
		any_artifact = { count = 3 has_artifact_flag = ingredient }
	}

	immediate = {
		select_random_hermetic_ingredient_effect = yes
		select_random_hermetic_ingredient_effect = yes
	}

	option = { # One charm
		name = EVTOPTAverena233
		destroy_selected_ingredient_1_effect = yes
		prestige = 15
		piety = -10
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = verena_warded_chambers years = 2 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 10 }
	}
	option = { # Two
		name = EVTOPTBverena233
		destroy_selected_ingredient_1_and_2_effect = yes
		prestige = 25
		piety = -20
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = verena_warded_chambers years = 4 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 10 }
	}
	option = { # Everything
		name = EVTOPTCverena233
		destroy_all_ingredients_effect = yes
		prestige = 35
		piety = -30
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = verena_warded_chambers years = 6 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 10 modifier = { factor = 5 trait = paranoid } }
	}
	option = { # Superstition
		name = EVTOPTDverena233
		piety = 10
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 20
			modifier = { factor = 3 trait = cynical }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = zealous }
		}
	}

	after = {
		remove_selection_of_ingredients_effect = yes
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Part 14: the symposium
# Once in ten years a High Priest with a laboratory may call fellow
# scholars of the Order to make one great work together: of war, of the
# realm's wealth or of lore. Guests quarrel and bring friends nobody wants,
# the host must choose between the proven authorities and an untried
# theory, an owl may bring insight at midnight, and three scandals end it.
# The best works are a handgun after the Nuln designs, a compass or a
# radius astronomicus; the bold road can also end in fire and broken glass.
# Built on the Runemoot of WHG_dwarf_guild_events (runesmith.110 - 131),
# itself the refactor of the vanilla gathering of minds.
###############################################################################

# verena.250 Call a symposium
# A host of rank 2 or more with a laboratory invites fellow scholars of the
# Order to make one great work together. At most once in ten years.
# Scope: ROOT is the host.
character_event = {
	id = verena.250
	desc = EVTDESCverena250
	picture = GFX_evt_gathering_of_minds
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_society_bi_yearly_pulse random list
	capable_only = yes
	is_in_society = yes

	trigger = {
		society_member_of = cult_of_verena
		society_rank >= 2
		learning = 10
		OR = { martial = 8 stewardship = 8 }
		is_landed = yes
		has_character_flag = built_laboratory
		NOT = { has_character_flag = verena_symposium }
		OR = {
			NOT = { has_character_flag = verena_symposium_held }
			had_character_flag = { flag = verena_symposium_held days = 3650 }
		}
		war = no
		block_general_event_trigger = no
		any_society_member = {
			count = 4
			opinion = { who = ROOT value = 15 }
			reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 15 }
		}
	}

	option = { # Call them
		name = EVTOPTAverena250
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_call_symposium_tt }
		scaled_wealth = { value = -0.5 min = -20 }
		hidden_effect = {
			clr_character_flag = verena_symposium_held
			set_character_flag = verena_symposium_held
			set_character_flag = verena_symposium
			character_event = { id = verena.251 days = 7 }
		}
	}
	option = { # Not now
		name = EVTOPTBverena250
	}
}

# verena.251 The guests are chosen
# A friend or a famous scholar as guest of honour, a second guest, and either
# two more or a guest who brings someone the host dislikes.
# Scope: ROOT is the host.
character_event = {
	id = verena.251
	desc = EVTDESCverena251
	picture = GFX_evt_recieve_letter
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.250
	hide_from = yes

	trigger = {
		has_character_flag = verena_symposium
	}

	immediate = {
		random_list = { # Guest of honour
			50 = {
				trigger = { society = { any_society_member = { is_friend = ROOT ai = yes } } }
				society = {
					random_society_member = {
						limit = { is_friend = ROOT ai = yes }
						save_event_target_as = great_minds_guest_1
					}
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					society = {
						any_society_member = {
							ai = yes
							OR = { learning = 16 society_rank == 4 }
							is_within_diplo_range = ROOT
							opinion = { who = ROOT value = 20 }
							reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 20 }
						}
					}
				}
				society = {
					random_society_member = {
						limit = {
							ai = yes
							OR = { learning = 16 society_rank == 4 }
							is_within_diplo_range = ROOT
							opinion = { who = ROOT value = 20 }
							reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 20 }
						}
						save_event_target_as = great_minds_guest_1
					}
				}
			}
			1 = {
				society = {
					random_society_member = {
						limit = {
							ai = yes
							opinion = { who = ROOT value = 15 }
							reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 15 }
						}
						save_event_target_as = great_minds_guest_1
					}
				}
			}
		}
		society = { # Second guest
			random_society_member = {
				limit = {
					ai = yes
					NOT = { character = event_target:great_minds_guest_1 }
					opinion = { who = ROOT value = 15 }
					reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 15 }
				}
				save_event_target_as = great_minds_guest_2
			}
		}
		random_list = { # Guests three and four
			70 = { # A guest wants someone the host dislikes
				trigger = {
					society = {
						any_society_member = {
							ai = yes
							NOT = { reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = -30 } }
						}
					}
				}
				set_character_flag = disliked_hermetic_colleague
				society = {
					random_society_member = {
						limit = { ai = yes NOT = { reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = -30 } } }
						save_event_target_as = great_minds_guest_4
					}
					random_society_member = {
						limit = {
							ai = yes
							opinion = { who = event_target:great_minds_guest_4 value = 20 }
							reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 15 }
							NOR = {
								character = event_target:great_minds_guest_1
								character = event_target:great_minds_guest_2
							}
						}
						save_event_target_as = great_minds_guest_3
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { event_target:great_minds_guest_3 = { always = yes } } }
					society = {
						random_society_member = {
							limit = {
								ai = yes
								NOR = {
									character = event_target:great_minds_guest_1
									character = event_target:great_minds_guest_2
								}
								opinion = { who = ROOT value = 15 }
								reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 15 }
							}
							save_event_target_as = great_minds_guest_3
						}
					}
				}
			}
			30 = { # Two more friends of the Order
				society = {
					random_society_member = {
						limit = {
							ai = yes
							NOR = {
								character = event_target:great_minds_guest_1
								character = event_target:great_minds_guest_2
							}
							opinion = { who = ROOT value = 15 }
							reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 15 }
						}
						save_event_target_as = great_minds_guest_3
					}
				}
				society = {
					random_society_member = {
						limit = {
							ai = yes
							NOR = {
								character = event_target:great_minds_guest_1
								character = event_target:great_minds_guest_2
								character = event_target:great_minds_guest_3
							}
							opinion = { who = ROOT value = 15 }
							reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 15 }
						}
						save_event_target_as = great_minds_guest_4
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAverena251
		reverse_opinion = {
			who = event_target:great_minds_guest_1
			modifier = opinion_grateful_invitation
			months = 3
		}
		reverse_opinion = {
			who = event_target:great_minds_guest_2
			modifier = opinion_grateful_invitation
			months = 3
		}
		reverse_opinion = {
			who = event_target:great_minds_guest_3
			modifier = opinion_grateful_invitation
			months = 3
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = disliked_hermetic_colleague } }
			reverse_opinion = {
				who = event_target:great_minds_guest_4
				modifier = opinion_grateful_invitation
				months = 3
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = disliked_hermetic_colleague }
				clr_character_flag = disliked_hermetic_colleague
				event_target:great_minds_guest_3 = {
					character_event = { id = verena.252 days = 10 }
				}
			}
			else = { character_event = { id = verena.254 days = 30 } }
		}
	}
}

# verena.253 Bring my friend
# The third guest asks the host to invite someone the host dislikes. Turning
# them away (or both) is a scandal.
# Scope: ROOT is the host, FROM is the third guest.
letter_event = {
	id = verena.253
	desc = EVTDESCverena253
	border = GFX_event_letter_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.252

	trigger = {
		has_character_flag = verena_symposium
	}

	option = { # A polite excuse
		name = EVTOPTAverena253
		trigger = { diplomacy = 12 }
		tooltip_info = diplomacy
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_symposium_excuse_tt }
		clear_event_target = great_minds_guest_4
	}
	option = { # Let them come
		name = EVTOPTBverena253
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_symposium_invite_tt }
		reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_grateful years = 3 }
	}
	option = { # Not them
		name = EVTOPTCverena253
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_symposium_refuse_tt }
		clear_event_target = great_minds_guest_4
		hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = verena.270 } }
		reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_very_disappointed years = 3 }
	}
	option = { # Neither of you
		name = EVTOPTDverena253
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_symposium_disinvite_tt }
		clear_event_target = great_minds_guest_3
		clear_event_target = great_minds_guest_4
		set_character_flag = verena_minor_scandal
		hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = verena.270 } }
		reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_offended years = 10 }
	}

	after = {
		hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = verena.254 days = 20 } }
	}
}

# verena.254 What shall we make?
# The host proposes a work of war, of the realm's wealth, or of lore. A guest
# may object (verena.255) or all may agree (verena.257).
# Scope: ROOT is the host.
character_event = {
	id = verena.254
	desc = EVTDESCverena254
	picture = GFX_evt_gathering_of_minds
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.251, 253
	hide_from = yes

	trigger = {
		has_character_flag = verena_symposium
	}

	option = { # War
		name = EVTOPTAverena254
		trigger = { martial = 8 }
		tooltip_info = martial
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_symposium_war_tt }
		set_character_flag = war_invention
	}
	option = { # The realm's wealth
		name = EVTOPTBverena254
		trigger = { stewardship = 8 }
		tooltip_info = stewardship
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_symposium_wealth_tt }
		set_character_flag = prosperity_invention
	}
	option = { # Lore
		name = EVTOPTCverena254
		tooltip_info = learning
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_symposium_lore_tt }
		set_character_flag = knowledge_invention
	}

	after = {
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					trigger = {
						event_target:great_minds_guest_1 = {
							OR = {
								NOT = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = 10 } }
								is_dumb_trigger = yes
								trait = ambitious
							}
						}
					}
					modifier = { is_rival = event_target:great_minds_guest_1 factor = 3 }
					event_target:great_minds_guest_1 = {
						character_event = { id = verena.255 days = 3 }
					}
				}
				50 = {
					trigger = {
						event_target:great_minds_guest_2 = {
							OR = {
								NOT = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = 10 } }
								is_dumb_trigger = yes
								trait = ambitious
							}
						}
					}
					modifier = { is_rival = event_target:great_minds_guest_2 factor = 3 }
					event_target:great_minds_guest_2 = {
						character_event = { id = verena.255 days = 3 }
					}
				}
				50 = {
					trigger = {
						event_target:great_minds_guest_3 = {
							OR = {
								NOT = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = 10 } }
								is_dumb_trigger = yes
								trait = ambitious
							}
						}
					}
					modifier = { is_rival = event_target:great_minds_guest_3 factor = 3 }
					event_target:great_minds_guest_3 = {
						character_event = { id = verena.255 days = 3 }
					}
				}
				50 = {
					trigger = {
						event_target:great_minds_guest_4 = {
							OR = {
								NOT = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = 10 } }
								is_dumb_trigger = yes
								trait = ambitious
							}
						}
					}
					modifier = { is_rival = event_target:great_minds_guest_4 factor = 3 }
					event_target:great_minds_guest_4 = {
						character_event = { id = verena.255 days = 3 }
					}
				}
				50 = { # All agree
					character_event = { id = verena.257 days = 3 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# verena.255 Objection (ping)
# Scope: ROOT is the objecting guest, FROM is the host.
character_event = {

	immediate = {
		FROM = { character_event = { id = verena.256 } }
	}
}

# verena.256 A guest objects
# The guest wants a different work. Argue it out, give way, or overrule them
# as host (a scandal).
# Scope: ROOT is the host, FROM is the objecting guest.
character_event = {
	id = verena.256
	desc = EVTDESCverena256
	picture = GFX_evt_quarrel
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.255

	trigger = {
		has_character_flag = verena_symposium
	}

	immediate = {
		FROM = {
			random_list = {
				33 = {
					trigger = { NOT = { ROOT = { has_character_flag = war_invention } } }
					modifier = { has_education_martial_trigger = yes factor = 1.5 }
					modifier = { has_lifestyle_martial_trigger = yes factor = 1.5 }
					set_character_flag = war_invention
				}
				33 = {
					trigger = { NOT = { ROOT = { has_character_flag = prosperity_invention } } }
					modifier = { has_education_stewardship_trigger = yes factor = 1.5 }
					modifier = { has_lifestyle_stewardship_trigger = yes factor = 1.5 }
					set_character_flag = prosperity_invention
				}
				33 = {
					trigger = { NOT = { ROOT = { has_character_flag = knowledge_invention } } }
					modifier = { has_education_learning_trigger = yes factor = 1.5 }
					modifier = { has_lifestyle_learning_trigger = yes factor = 1.5 }
					set_character_flag = knowledge_invention
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Argue it out
		name = EVTOPTAverena256
		trigger = { learning = 12 }
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_symposium_debate_tt }
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				modifier = { learning = 8 factor = 1.2 }
				modifier = { learning = 10 factor = 1.2 }
				modifier = { learning = 12 factor = 1.2 }
				modifier = { learning = 14 factor = 1.2 }
				modifier = { learning = 16 factor = 1.2 }
				modifier = { learning = 18 factor = 1.2 }
				modifier = { learning = 20 factor = 1.2 }
				custom_tooltip = { text = verena_symposium_debate_won_tt }
				hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = verena.258 days = 7 } }
			}
			50 = {
				modifier = { FROM = { learning = 8 } factor = 1.2 }
				modifier = { FROM = { learning = 10 } factor = 1.2 }
				modifier = { FROM = { learning = 12 } factor = 1.2 }
				modifier = { FROM = { learning = 14 } factor = 1.2 }
				modifier = { FROM = { learning = 16 } factor = 1.2 }
				modifier = { FROM = { learning = 18 } factor = 1.2 }
				modifier = { FROM = { learning = 20 } factor = 1.2 }
				custom_tooltip = { text = verena_symposium_debate_lost_tt }
				hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = verena.259 days = 7 } }
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # Give way
		name = EVTOPTBverena256
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_symposium_give_way_tt }
		hidden_effect = {
			clr_character_flag = war_invention
			clr_character_flag = prosperity_invention
			clr_character_flag = knowledge_invention
			if = {
				limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = war_invention } }
				FROM = { clr_character_flag = war_invention }
				set_character_flag = war_invention
			}
			if = {
				limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = prosperity_invention } }
				FROM = { clr_character_flag = prosperity_invention }
				set_character_flag = prosperity_invention
			}
			if = {
				limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = knowledge_invention } }
				FROM = { clr_character_flag = knowledge_invention }
				set_character_flag = knowledge_invention
			}
			character_event = { id = verena.260 days = 30 }
		}
	}
	option = { # Overrule them
		name = EVTOPTCverena256
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_symposium_overrule_tt }
		reverse_opinion = {
			who = event_target:great_minds_guest_1
			modifier = opinion_unreasonable
			years = 5
		}
		reverse_opinion = {
			who = event_target:great_minds_guest_2
			modifier = opinion_unreasonable
			years = 5
		}
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:great_minds_guest_3 = { always = yes } }
			reverse_opinion = {
				who = event_target:great_minds_guest_3
				modifier = opinion_unreasonable
				years = 5
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:great_minds_guest_4 = { always = yes } }
			reverse_opinion = {
				who = event_target:great_minds_guest_4
				modifier = opinion_unreasonable
				years = 5
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			clr_character_flag = war_invention
			clr_character_flag = prosperity_invention
			clr_character_flag = knowledge_invention
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			character_event = { id = verena.270 }
			character_event = { id = verena.260 days = 30 }
		}
	}
}

# verena.260 Authority or daring
# Two guests split over the work: the first wants to test an untried theory,
# the second to keep to the proven authorities of the Order.
# Then a quarrel, a friendship or a flash of insight may follow.
# Scope: ROOT is the host.
character_event = {
	id = verena.260
	desc = EVTDESCverena260
	picture = GFX_evt_rival_rulers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.256 - 259
	hide_from = yes

	trigger = {
		has_character_flag = verena_symposium
	}

	option = { # The proven authorities
		name = EVTOPTAverena260
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_symposium_safe_tt }
		reverse_opinion = {
			who = event_target:great_minds_guest_2
			modifier = opinion_grateful
			years = 5
		}
		reverse_opinion = {
			who = event_target:great_minds_guest_1
			modifier = opinion_disappointed
			years = 5
		}
		hidden_effect = { set_character_flag = safe_invention }
	}
	option = { # The untried theory
		name = EVTOPTBverena260
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_symposium_risky_tt }
		reverse_opinion = {
			who = event_target:great_minds_guest_1
			modifier = opinion_grateful
			years = 5
		}
		reverse_opinion = {
			who = event_target:great_minds_guest_2
			modifier = opinion_disappointed
			years = 5
		}
		hidden_effect = { set_character_flag = risky_invention }
	}

	after = {
		hidden_effect = {
			event_target:great_minds_guest_1 = {
				opinion = {
					who = event_target:great_minds_guest_2
					modifier = opinion_argued
					years = 5
				}
				reverse_opinion = {
					who = event_target:great_minds_guest_2
					modifier = opinion_argued
					years = 5
				}
			}
			random_list = {
				40 = { # Two guests quarrel
					trigger = {
						society = {
							any_society_member = {
								OR = {
									character = event_target:great_minds_guest_1
									character = event_target:great_minds_guest_2
									character = event_target:great_minds_guest_3
									character = event_target:great_minds_guest_4
								}
								society = {
									any_society_member = {
										OR = {
											character = event_target:great_minds_guest_1
											character = event_target:great_minds_guest_2
											character = event_target:great_minds_guest_3
											character = event_target:great_minds_guest_4
										}
										NOT = { opinion = { who = PREVPREV value = 10 } }
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
					society = {
						random_society_member = {
							limit = {
								OR = {
									character = event_target:great_minds_guest_1
									character = event_target:great_minds_guest_2
									character = event_target:great_minds_guest_3
									character = event_target:great_minds_guest_4
								}
								society = {
									any_society_member = {
										OR = {
											character = event_target:great_minds_guest_1
											character = event_target:great_minds_guest_2
											character = event_target:great_minds_guest_3
											character = event_target:great_minds_guest_4
										}
										NOT = { opinion = { who = PREVPREV value = 10 } }
									}
								}
							}
							save_event_target_as = arguer_2
							society = {
								random_society_member = {
									limit = {
										OR = {
											character = event_target:great_minds_guest_1
											character = event_target:great_minds_guest_2
											character = event_target:great_minds_guest_3
											character = event_target:great_minds_guest_4
										}
										NOT = { opinion = { who = PREVPREV value = 10 } }
									}
									save_event_target_as = arguer_1
								}
							}
						}
					}
					character_event = { id = verena.261 days = 20 random = 10 }
				}
				35 = { # The host works well with a guest
					trigger = {
						OR = {
							opinion = { who = event_target:great_minds_guest_1 value = 20 }
							opinion = { who = event_target:great_minds_guest_2 value = 20 }
							opinion = { who = event_target:great_minds_guest_3 value = 20 }
							opinion = { who = event_target:great_minds_guest_4 value = 20 }
						}
					}
					society = {
						random_society_member = {
							limit = {
								OR = {
									character = event_target:great_minds_guest_1
									character = event_target:great_minds_guest_2
									character = event_target:great_minds_guest_3
									character = event_target:great_minds_guest_4
								}
								reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 20 }
							}
							character_event = { id = verena.264 days = 20 random = 10 }
						}
					}
				}
				25 = { # A flash of insight
					trigger = { learning = 12 }
					modifier = { learning = 14 factor = 1.2 }
					modifier = { learning = 16 factor = 1.2 }
					modifier = { learning = 18 factor = 1.2 }
					character_event = { id = verena.266 days = 20 random = 10 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# verena.261 Two guests quarrel
# Make peace between them, or send one home (a scandal).
# Scope: ROOT is the host.
character_event = {
	id = verena.261
	desc = EVTDESCverena261
	picture = GFX_evt_quarrel
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.260
	hide_from = yes

	trigger = {
		has_character_flag = verena_symposium
	}

	option = { # Make peace
		name = EVTOPTAverena261
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_symposium_mediate_tt }
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				modifier = { society_rank >= 3 factor = 1.2 }
				modifier = {
					reverse_opinion = { who = event_target:arguer_1 value = 25 }
					reverse_opinion = { who = event_target:arguer_2 value = 25 }
					factor = 1.4
				}
				modifier = { diplomacy = 12 factor = 1.2 }
				modifier = { diplomacy = 14 factor = 1.2 }
				modifier = { diplomacy = 16 factor = 1.2 }
				custom_tooltip = { text = verena_symposium_mediate_won_tt }
				hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = verena.262 days = 5 } }
			}
			50 = {
				modifier = { NOT = { diplomacy = 10 } factor = 1.2 }
				modifier = { has_unpleasant_personality_trigger = yes factor = 1.3 }
				custom_tooltip = { text = verena_symposium_mediate_lost_tt }
				hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = verena.263 days = 5 } }
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # Send the first home
		name = EVTOPTBverena261
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_symposium_send_first_home_tt }
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:arguer_1 = { character = event_target:great_minds_guest_1 } }
				clear_event_target = great_minds_guest_1
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { event_target:arguer_1 = { character = event_target:great_minds_guest_2 } }
				clear_event_target = great_minds_guest_2
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { event_target:arguer_1 = { character = event_target:great_minds_guest_3 } }
				clear_event_target = great_minds_guest_3
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { event_target:arguer_1 = { character = event_target:great_minds_guest_4 } }
				clear_event_target = great_minds_guest_4
			}
			character_event = { id = verena.270 }
			character_event = { id = verena.267 days = 30 random = 20 }
		}
	}
	option = { # Send the second home
		name = EVTOPTCverena261
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_symposium_send_second_home_tt }
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:arguer_2 = { character = event_target:great_minds_guest_1 } }
				clear_event_target = great_minds_guest_1
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { event_target:arguer_2 = { character = event_target:great_minds_guest_2 } }
				clear_event_target = great_minds_guest_2
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { event_target:arguer_2 = { character = event_target:great_minds_guest_3 } }
				clear_event_target = great_minds_guest_3
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { event_target:arguer_2 = { character = event_target:great_minds_guest_4 } }
				clear_event_target = great_minds_guest_4
			}
			character_event = { id = verena.270 }
			character_event = { id = verena.267 days = 30 random = 20 }
		}
	}
}

# verena.265 Kindred minds
# The host and a guest work well together and may become friends.
# Scope: ROOT is the host, FROM is the guest.
character_event = {
	id = verena.265
	desc = EVTDESCverena265
	picture = GFX_evt_gathering_of_minds
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.264

	trigger = {
		has_character_flag = verena_symposium
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAverena265
		if = {
			limit = {
				opinion = { who = FROM value = 40 }
				reverse_opinion = { who = FROM value = 40 }
				NOT = { is_friend = FROM }
			}
			add_friend = FROM
		}
		else = {
			reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_great_minds years = 10 }
			hidden_effect = { opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_great_minds years = 10 } }
		}
		hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = verena.267 days = 30 random = 20 } }
	}
}

# verena.267 The outcome
# Adds up the guests' learning and rolls the result. The bold road can reach
# the best work but can also end in disaster.
# Scope: ROOT is the host.
character_event = {
	id = verena.267
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.261 - 266

	trigger = {
		has_character_flag = verena_symposium
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = { which = collective_learning value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:great_minds_guest_1 = { always = yes } }
			export_to_variable = {
				which = collective_learning_temp
				value = learning
				who = event_target:great_minds_guest_1
			}
			change_variable = { which = collective_learning which = collective_learning_temp }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:great_minds_guest_2 = { always = yes } }
			export_to_variable = {
				which = collective_learning_temp
				value = learning
				who = event_target:great_minds_guest_2
			}
			change_variable = { which = collective_learning which = collective_learning_temp }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:great_minds_guest_3 = { always = yes } }
			export_to_variable = {
				which = collective_learning_temp
				value = learning
				who = event_target:great_minds_guest_3
			}
			change_variable = { which = collective_learning which = collective_learning_temp }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:great_minds_guest_4 = { always = yes } }
			export_to_variable = {
				which = collective_learning_temp
				value = learning
				who = event_target:great_minds_guest_4
			}
			change_variable = { which = collective_learning which = collective_learning_temp }
		}
		random_list = {
			20 = { # Best
				trigger = { has_character_flag = risky_invention }
				modifier = { learning = 10 factor = 1.3 }
				modifier = { learning = 12 factor = 1.3 }
				modifier = { learning = 14 factor = 1.3 }
				modifier = { learning = 16 factor = 1.3 }
				modifier = { learning = 18 factor = 1.3 }
				modifier = { learning = 20 factor = 1.3 }
				modifier = { has_character_flag = brilliant_insight factor = 100 }
				modifier = { has_character_modifier = verena_lorekeeper_vigil factor = 1.3 }
				modifier = {
					check_variable = { which = collective_learning value = 40 }
					factor = 1.4
				}
				modifier = {
					check_variable = { which = collective_learning value = 50 }
					factor = 1.4
				}
				modifier = {
					check_variable = { which = collective_learning value = 60 }
					factor = 1.4
				}
				set_character_flag = best_outcome
				character_event = { id = verena.268 }
			}
			30 = { # Good
				modifier = { learning = 10 factor = 1.2 }
				modifier = { learning = 12 factor = 1.2 }
				modifier = { learning = 14 factor = 1.2 }
				modifier = { learning = 16 factor = 1.2 }
				modifier = { learning = 18 factor = 1.2 }
				modifier = { learning = 20 factor = 1.2 }
				modifier = { has_character_flag = brilliant_insight factor = 10 }
				modifier = { has_character_modifier = verena_lorekeeper_vigil factor = 1.3 }
				modifier = {
					check_variable = { which = collective_learning value = 40 }
					factor = 1.3
				}
				modifier = {
					check_variable = { which = collective_learning value = 50 }
					factor = 1.3
				}
				modifier = {
					check_variable = { which = collective_learning value = 60 }
					factor = 1.3
				}
				set_character_flag = good_outcome
				character_event = { id = verena.268 }
			}
			45 = { # Fair
				trigger = { has_character_flag = safe_invention }
				modifier = { learning = 10 factor = 1.1 }
				modifier = { learning = 12 factor = 1.1 }
				modifier = { learning = 14 factor = 1.1 }
				modifier = { learning = 16 factor = 1.1 }
				modifier = { learning = 18 factor = 1.1 }
				modifier = { learning = 20 factor = 1.1 }
				modifier = {
					check_variable = { which = collective_learning value = 40 }
					factor = 1.2
				}
				modifier = {
					check_variable = { which = collective_learning value = 50 }
					factor = 1.2
				}
				modifier = {
					check_variable = { which = collective_learning value = 60 }
					factor = 1.2
				}
				set_character_flag = ok_outcome
				character_event = { id = verena.268 }
			}
			25 = { # Nothing comes of it
				trigger = { has_character_flag = safe_invention }
				set_character_flag = failure_outcome
				character_event = { id = verena.269 }
			}
			25 = { # Disaster
				trigger = { has_character_flag = risky_invention }
				set_character_flag = bad_failure_outcome
				character_event = { id = verena.269 }
			}
		}
		clr_character_flag = brilliant_insight
	}
}

# verena.269 The work fails
# Nothing comes of the safe road; the bold one can end in an explosion
# that maims or kills host and guests.
# Scope: ROOT is the host.
character_event = {
	id = verena.269
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = failure_outcome }
		text = EVTDESCverena269
		picture = GFX_evt_quarrel
	}
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = bad_failure_outcome } text = EVTDESCverena269b }
	picture = GFX_evt_burning_house
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.267
	hide_from = yes

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			20 = { set_character_flag = invention_failure_maimed }
			20 = { set_character_flag = invention_failure_wounded }
			60 = { }
		}
		event_target:great_minds_guest_1 = { runesmith_moot_blast_roll_effect = yes }
		event_target:great_minds_guest_2 = { runesmith_moot_blast_roll_effect = yes }
		event_target:great_minds_guest_3 = { runesmith_moot_blast_roll_effect = yes }
		event_target:great_minds_guest_4 = { runesmith_moot_blast_roll_effect = yes }
	}

	option = { # Nothing comes of it
		name = EVTOPTAverena269
		trigger = { has_character_flag = failure_outcome }
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_symposium_failed_tt }
	}
	option = { # Disaster
		name = EVTOPTBverena269
		trigger = { has_character_flag = bad_failure_outcome }
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_symposium_disaster_tt }
		trigger_switch = {
			on_trigger = has_character_flag
			invention_failure_maimed = { add_maimed_trait_effect = yes }
			invention_failure_wounded = { add_trait_silently_wounded_effect = yes }
		}
		event_target:great_minds_guest_1 = { runesmith_moot_blast_effect = yes }
		event_target:great_minds_guest_2 = { runesmith_moot_blast_effect = yes }
		event_target:great_minds_guest_3 = { runesmith_moot_blast_effect = yes }
		event_target:great_minds_guest_4 = { runesmith_moot_blast_effect = yes }
	}

	after = {
		hidden_effect = {
			clr_character_flag = verena_symposium
			clr_character_flag = war_invention
			clr_character_flag = prosperity_invention
			clr_character_flag = knowledge_invention
			clr_character_flag = safe_invention
			clr_character_flag = risky_invention
			clr_character_flag = verena_minor_scandal
			clr_character_flag = verena_major_scandal
			clr_character_flag = failure_outcome
			clr_character_flag = bad_failure_outcome
			clr_character_flag = invention_failure_maimed
			clr_character_flag = invention_failure_wounded
			event_target:great_minds_guest_1 = { runesmith_moot_blast_clear_effect = yes }
			event_target:great_minds_guest_2 = { runesmith_moot_blast_clear_effect = yes }
			event_target:great_minds_guest_3 = { runesmith_moot_blast_clear_effect = yes }
			event_target:great_minds_guest_4 = { runesmith_moot_blast_clear_effect = yes }
		}
	}
}

# verena.270 Scandal
# A first scandal is minor, a second major, a third ends the symposium.
# Scope: ROOT is the host.
character_event = {
	id = verena.270
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.253, 256, 261, 263

	trigger = {
		has_character_flag = verena_symposium
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOR = {
					has_character_flag = verena_minor_scandal
					has_character_flag = verena_major_scandal
				}
			}
			set_character_flag = verena_minor_scandal
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = verena_minor_scandal }
			clr_character_flag = verena_minor_scandal
			set_character_flag = verena_major_scandal
		}
		else = { clr_character_flag = verena_major_scandal character_event = { id = verena.271 } }
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Part 15: the invocation of Verena
# A brother or sister of the Order leads an invocation of the Goddess and
# asks a member to take part. In the trance the member may ask one
# question: of substances, the stars, the gods or hidden truths. Learning
# decides the answer, ingredients steady the rite, Lorekeepers and
# Scalebearers are more often heard, and a false answer about hidden
# truths makes the member wonder whose voice it was.
###############################################################################

# verena.280 The invocation is proposed
# A brother or sister of the Order (at court first, then in the realm,
# then abroad) means to lead an invocation of Verena and invites the member.
# Scope: ROOT is the invited member.
character_event = {
	id = verena.280
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_society_bi_yearly_pulse random list
	only_playable = yes
	only_capable = yes
	prisoner = no
	is_in_society = yes

	trigger = {
		society_member_of = cult_of_verena
		is_inaccessible_trigger = no
		society = {
			any_society_member = {
				ai = yes
				is_incapable = no
				prisoner = no
				in_command = no
				NOT = { character = ROOT }
				is_inaccessible_trigger = no
				is_within_diplo_range = ROOT
			}
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
		modifier = { factor = 2 society_rank >= 3 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 society_rank == 1 }
		modifier = { factor = 3 OR = { has_focus = focus_scholarship has_focus = focus_theology } }
		modifier = { factor = 3 trait = genius }
		modifier = { factor = 3 trait = mystic }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = quick }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = shrewd }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = dull }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = slow }
		modifier = { factor = 0.1 trait = imbecile }
		modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_character_flag = had_a_divine_summoning }
	}

	immediate = {
		society = {
			random_society_member = {
				limit = {
					ai = yes
					is_incapable = no
					prisoner = no
					in_command = no
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					is_inaccessible_trigger = no
					is_within_diplo_range = ROOT
				}
				preferred_limit = { host = { character = ROOT } }
				preferred_limit = { same_realm = ROOT }
				character_event = { id = verena.281 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# verena.281 Ping to the member
# Scope: ROOT is the leader of the rite, FROM is the member.
character_event = {
	id = verena.281
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.280

	immediate = {
		FROM = { character_event = { id = verena.282 } }
	}
}

# verena.282 Will you take part?
# Join the rite, send ingredients from the cabinet to make it safer, or
# stay away and lose face in the Order.
# Scope: ROOT is the member, FROM is the leader of the rite.
character_event = {
	id = verena.282
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { NOT = { host = { character = ROOT } } } }
		text = EVTDESCverena282
	}
	desc = { trigger = { FROM = { host = { character = ROOT } } } text = EVTDESCverena282b }
	picture = GFX_evt_shadowy_cabal
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.281

	option = { # Take part
		name = EVTOPTAverena282
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = { factor = 50 trait = ambitious }
			modifier = { factor = 50 trait = mystic }
			modifier = { factor = 0 any_artifact = { has_artifact_flag = ingredient } }
		}
		set_character_flag = had_a_divine_summoning
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_summoning_unworthy_tt }
		hidden_effect = { FROM = { character_event = { id = verena.283 } } }
	}
	option = { # Send ingredients
		name = EVTOPTBverena282
		trigger = { any_artifact = { has_artifact_flag = ingredient } }
		tooltip_info = yes
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 500
			modifier = { factor = 50 trait = ambitious }
			modifier = { factor = 50 trait = mystic }
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = ingredient_option_available_tt }
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = verena_summoning_ingredients_tt
			hidden_effect = { destroy_random_ingredients_effect = yes }
		}
		set_character_flag = had_a_divine_summoning
		set_character_flag = used_ingredients_for_summoning
		hidden_effect = { FROM = { character_event = { id = verena.283 } } }
	}
	option = { # Stay away
		name = EVTOPTCverena282
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 50 trait = craven }
			modifier = { factor = 25 trait = content }
		}
		detract_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
		reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_coward years = 5 }
	}
}

# verena.284 The invocation
# In the trance the member asks the presence about the weighing of
# substances (stewardship), the stars (diplomacy), the gods (learning) or
# hidden truths (intrigue).
# Scope: ROOT is the member, FROM is the leader of the rite.
narrative_event = {
	id = verena.284

	desc = EVTDESCverena284
	picture = GFX_evt_magic_ritual
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_religion

			set_character_flag = divine_summoning_alchemy

		}
	}
	option = { # The stars
		name = EVTOPTBverena284
		hidden_effect = {
			set_character_flag = divine_summoning_astrology
			character_event = { id = verena.285 }
		}

		}
	}
