		hidden_effect = {
			set_character_flag = divine_summoning_hidden_truth
			character_event = { id = verena.285 }
		}
	}
}

# verena.285 The answer
# Rolls the answer by learning: a revelation, a boon or a curse in the
# chosen field, or nothing understood. Ingredients the member sent halve
# the worst rolls. Verena's own priests (a Lorekeeper or a
# Scalebearer) are more often heard.
# Scope: ROOT is the member, FROM is the leader of the rite.
character_event = {
	id = verena.285
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.284

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { learning = 21 }
			random_list = {
				15 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						OR = {
							has_character_modifier = verena_lorekeeper_vigil
							has_character_modifier = verena_scalebearer_oath
						}
					}
					set_character_flag = divine_summoning_success
				}
				25 = { set_character_flag = verena_summon_good_bonus }
				40 = { set_character_flag = verena_summon_bonus }
				15 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 has_character_flag = used_ingredients_for_summoning }
					set_character_flag = verena_summon_penalty
				}
				5 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 has_character_flag = used_ingredients_for_summoning }
					set_character_flag = verena_summon_bad_penalty
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { learning = 14 }
			random_list = {
				10 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						OR = {
							has_character_modifier = verena_lorekeeper_vigil
							has_character_modifier = verena_scalebearer_oath
						}
					}
					set_character_flag = divine_summoning_success
				}
				20 = { set_character_flag = verena_summon_good_bonus }
				35 = { set_character_flag = verena_summon_bonus }
				20 = { set_character_flag = verena_summon_penalty }
				10 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 has_character_flag = used_ingredients_for_summoning }
					set_character_flag = verena_summon_bad_penalty
				}
				5 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 has_character_flag = used_ingredients_for_summoning }
					set_character_flag = divine_summoning_failure
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { learning = 7 }
			random_list = {
				5 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						OR = {
							has_character_modifier = verena_lorekeeper_vigil
							has_character_modifier = verena_scalebearer_oath
						}
					}
					set_character_flag = divine_summoning_success
				}
				10 = { set_character_flag = verena_summon_good_bonus }
				20 = { set_character_flag = verena_summon_bonus }
				35 = { set_character_flag = verena_summon_penalty }
				20 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 has_character_flag = used_ingredients_for_summoning }
					set_character_flag = verena_summon_bad_penalty
				}
				10 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 has_character_flag = used_ingredients_for_summoning }
					set_character_flag = divine_summoning_failure
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			random_list = {
				5 = { set_character_flag = verena_summon_good_bonus }
				15 = { set_character_flag = verena_summon_bonus }
				40 = { set_character_flag = verena_summon_penalty }
				25 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 has_character_flag = used_ingredients_for_summoning }
					set_character_flag = verena_summon_bad_penalty
				}
				15 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 has_character_flag = used_ingredients_for_summoning }
					set_character_flag = divine_summoning_failure
				}
			}
		}
		clr_character_flag = used_ingredients_for_summoning
		character_event = { id = verena.286 days = 15 random = 15 }
	}
}

# verena.286 What the presence said
# Applies the boon or curse of the chosen field for five years, or the
# revelation or the shame for ten. Asking for hidden truths and failing
# leaves the member wondering whose voice it was.
# Scope: ROOT is the member.
character_event = {
	id = verena.286
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = divine_summoning_success } text = EVTDESCverena286 }
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = divine_summoning_failure
			NOT = { has_character_flag = divine_summoning_hidden_truth }
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena286b
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = divine_summoning_failure
			has_character_flag = divine_summoning_hidden_truth
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena286c
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				has_character_flag = divine_summoning_success
				has_character_flag = divine_summoning_failure
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCverena286d
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_shadowy_cabal
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.285

	option = { # A boon or a curse
		name = EVTOPTAverena286
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				has_character_flag = divine_summoning_success
				has_character_flag = divine_summoning_failure
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = divine_summoning_alchemy }
			trigger_switch = {
				on_trigger = has_character_flag
				verena_summon_good_bonus = {
					add_character_modifier = { name = alchemy_good_ste_bonus years = 5 }
				}
				verena_summon_bonus = {
					add_character_modifier = { name = alchemy_ste_bonus years = 5 }
				}
				verena_summon_penalty = {
					add_character_modifier = { name = alchemy_ste_penalty years = 5 }
				}
				verena_summon_bad_penalty = {
					add_character_modifier = { name = alchemy_bad_ste_penalty years = 5 }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = divine_summoning_astrology }
			trigger_switch = {
				on_trigger = has_character_flag
				verena_summon_good_bonus = {
					add_character_modifier = { name = astrology_good_dip_bonus years = 5 }
				}
				verena_summon_bonus = {
					add_character_modifier = { name = astrology_dip_bonus years = 5 }
				}
				verena_summon_penalty = {
					add_character_modifier = { name = astrology_dip_penalty years = 5 }
				}
				verena_summon_bad_penalty = {
					add_character_modifier = { name = astrology_bad_dip_penalty years = 5 }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = divine_summoning_open_mind }
			trigger_switch = {
				on_trigger = has_character_flag
				verena_summon_good_bonus = {
					add_character_modifier = { name = open_mind_good_lea_bonus years = 5 }
				}
				verena_summon_bonus = {
					add_character_modifier = { name = open_mind_lea_bonus years = 5 }
				}
				verena_summon_penalty = {
					add_character_modifier = { name = open_mind_lea_penalty years = 5 }
				}
				verena_summon_bad_penalty = {
					add_character_modifier = { name = open_mind_bad_lea_penalty years = 5 }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = divine_summoning_hidden_truth }
			trigger_switch = {
				on_trigger = has_character_flag
				verena_summon_good_bonus = {
					add_character_modifier = { name = hidden_truth_good_int_bonus years = 5 }
				}
				verena_summon_bonus = {
					add_character_modifier = { name = hidden_truth_int_bonus years = 5 }
				}
				verena_summon_penalty = {
					add_character_modifier = { name = hidden_truth_int_penalty years = 5 }
				}
				verena_summon_bad_penalty = {
					add_character_modifier = { name = hidden_truth_bad_int_penalty years = 5 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # A revelation
		name = EVTOPTBverena286
		trigger = { has_character_flag = divine_summoning_success }
		prestige = 100
		piety = 25
		add_character_modifier = { name = divine_summoning_succeeded years = 10 }
		add_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
	}
	option = { # The shame
		name = EVTOPTCverena286
		trigger = { has_character_flag = divine_summoning_failure }
		prestige = -100
		add_character_modifier = { name = divine_summoning_failed years = 10 }
	}

	after = {
		clr_character_flag = divine_summoning_success
		clr_character_flag = divine_summoning_failure
		clr_character_flag = verena_summon_good_bonus
		clr_character_flag = verena_summon_bonus
		clr_character_flag = verena_summon_penalty
		clr_character_flag = verena_summon_bad_penalty
		clr_character_flag = divine_summoning_alchemy
		clr_character_flag = divine_summoning_astrology
		clr_character_flag = divine_summoning_open_mind
		clr_character_flag = divine_summoning_hidden_truth
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Part 16: the theory paper
# A member of the Order of Mysteries or of the cult of Tzeentch writes a
# treatise for the brethren and sends it to up to five of them for review.
# Verena's scholars write on law, justice and the Wisdom of Verena, the
# Changer's on fate and the Winds of Magic, both on alchemy, the stars and
# old Nehekhara. Copied work may be found out.
###############################################################################

# verena.300 A paper for the brethren
# The writer chooses how to work: a decent effort, help from others
# (diplomacy 15), copying from other works (intrigue 15) or no expense
# spared, which can make the writer ambitious or stressed.
# Scope: ROOT is the writer.
character_event = {
	id = verena.300
	desc = EVTDESCverena300
	picture = GFX_evt_scientist
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # decision hermetics_write_theory_paper

	option = { #Normal effort
		name = EVTOPTAverena300
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_paper_effort_tt }
		hidden_effect = {
			set_character_flag = theory_paper_normal_effort
			character_event = { id = verena.301 days = 90 random = 90 }
		}
	}
	option = { #Get help from others
		name = EVTOPTBverena300
		trigger = { diplomacy = 15 }
		tooltip_info = diplomacy
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_paper_help_tt }
		hidden_effect = {
			set_character_flag = theory_paper_asked_help
			character_event = { id = verena.301 days = 90 random = 90 }
		}
	}
	option = { #Steal another's work
		name = EVTOPTCverena300
		trigger = { intrigue = 15 }
		tooltip_info = intrigue
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_paper_copy_tt }
		hidden_effect = {
			set_character_flag = theory_paper_stole_work
			character_event = { id = verena.301 days = 90 random = 90 }
		}
	}
	option = { #Invest heavily in research
		name = EVTOPTDverena300
		trigger = { NOT = { trait = content } }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = ambitious trait = content } }
			random = { chance = 10 add_trait = ambitious }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = stressed } }
			random = { chance = 10 add_trait = stressed }
		}
		scaled_wealth = { value = -1 min = -25 }
		piety = -50
		add_character_modifier = { name = theory_paper_heavy_investment years = 1 }
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_paper_expense_tt }
		hidden_effect = {
			set_character_flag = theory_paper_heavy_investment
			character_event = { id = verena.301 days = 180 random = 180 }
		}
	}
}

# verena.301 How good is it?
# Rolls the quality of the paper (simplistic, average, excellent or
# revolutionary) by learning, effort and traits.
# Scope: ROOT is the writer.
character_event = {
	id = verena.301
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.300

	trigger = {
		is_alive = yes
	}

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			15 = {
				modifier = { factor = 2 learning = 25 }
				modifier = { factor = 2 has_character_flag = theory_paper_heavy_investment }
				modifier = { factor = 1.4 has_character_flag = theory_paper_asked_help }
				modifier = { factor = 1.6 has_character_flag = theory_paper_stole_work }
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = genius }
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = imbecile }
				modifier = { factor = 1.6 trait = quick }
				modifier = { factor = 0.7 trait = slow }
				modifier = { factor = 0.6 trait = inbred }
				modifier = { factor = 1.4 trait = shrewd }
				modifier = { factor = 0.8 trait = dull }
				modifier = { factor = 1.3 trait = mastermind_theologian }
				modifier = { factor = 1.4 trait = scholar }
				modifier = { factor = 1.4 trait = mystic }
				modifier = { factor = 1.4 trait = theologian }
				modifier = { factor = 1.6 trait = diligent }
				modifier = { factor = 0.7 trait = slothful }
				modifier = { factor = 1.6 trait = ambitious }
				modifier = { factor = 0.7 trait = content }
				modifier = { factor = 1.6 trait = erudite }
				set_character_flag = theory_paper_revolutionary
			}
			25 = {
				modifier = { factor = 2 AND = { learning = 17 NOT = { learning = 25 } } }
				modifier = { factor = 2 has_character_flag = theory_paper_heavy_investment }
				modifier = { factor = 1.3 has_character_flag = theory_paper_asked_help }
				modifier = { factor = 1.6 has_character_flag = theory_paper_stole_work }
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = genius }
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = imbecile }
				modifier = { factor = 1.6 trait = quick }
				modifier = { factor = 0.7 trait = slow }
				modifier = { factor = 0.6 trait = inbred }
				modifier = { factor = 1.4 trait = shrewd }
				modifier = { factor = 0.8 trait = dull }
				modifier = { factor = 1.3 trait = scholarly_theologian }
				modifier = { factor = 1.4 trait = scholar }
				modifier = { factor = 1.4 trait = mystic }
				modifier = { factor = 1.4 trait = theologian }
				modifier = { factor = 1.6 trait = diligent }
				modifier = { factor = 0.7 trait = slothful }
				modifier = { factor = 1.6 trait = ambitious }
				modifier = { factor = 0.7 trait = content }
				modifier = { factor = 1.6 trait = erudite }
				set_character_flag = theory_paper_excellent
			}
			35 = {
				modifier = { factor = 2 AND = { learning = 9 NOT = { learning = 17 } } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.3 trait = martial_cleric }
				set_character_flag = theory_paper_average
			}
			25 = {
				modifier = { factor = 2 NOT = { learning = 9 } }
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 has_character_flag = theory_paper_heavy_investment }
				modifier = { factor = 0.8 has_character_flag = theory_paper_asked_help }
				modifier = { factor = 0.7 has_character_flag = theory_paper_stole_work }
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = genius }
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = imbecile }
				modifier = {
					factor = 2
					OR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 }
				}
				modifier = { factor = 0.7 trait = quick }
				modifier = { factor = 1.6 trait = slow }
				modifier = { factor = 1.8 trait = inbred }
				modifier = { factor = 0.8 trait = shrewd }
				modifier = { factor = 1.4 trait = dull }
				modifier = { factor = 1.3 trait = detached_priest }
				modifier = { factor = 0.8 trait = scholar }
				modifier = { factor = 0.8 trait = mystic }
				modifier = { factor = 0.8 trait = theologian }
				modifier = { factor = 0.7 trait = diligent }
				modifier = { factor = 1.6 trait = slothful }
				modifier = { factor = 0.7 trait = ambitious }
				modifier = { factor = 1.6 trait = content }
				modifier = { factor = 0.7 trait = erudite }
				set_character_flag = theory_paper_simplistic
			}
		}
		remove_character_modifier = theory_paper_heavy_investment
		character_event = { id = verena.302 }
	}
}

# verena.302 It is finished
# Picks the subject: law, justice and the wisdom of Verena for the Order of
# Mysteries, the Changer of Ways and the Winds of Magic for the cult of
# Tzeentch, alchemy, the stars and old Nehekhara for both. Up to five
# brethren in range are asked to review it, or the writer burns it.
# Scope: ROOT is the writer.
character_event = {
	id = verena.302
	desc = { text = EVTDESCverena302_law trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_paper_law } }
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCverena302_justice
		trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_paper_justice }
	}
	desc = { text = EVTDESCverena302_wisdom trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_paper_wisdom } }
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCverena302_hieroglyphs
		trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_paper_hieroglyphs }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCverena302_distillation
		trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_paper_distillation }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCverena302_fermentation
		trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_paper_fermentation }
	}
	desc = { text = EVTDESCverena302_elixir trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_paper_elixir } }
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCverena302_warpstone
		trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_paper_warpstone }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCverena302_planets
		trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_paper_planets }
	}
	desc = { text = EVTDESCverena302_stars trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_paper_stars } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCverena302_moons trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_paper_moons } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCverena302_sun trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_paper_sun } }
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCverena302_changer
		trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_paper_changer }
	}
	desc = { text = EVTDESCverena302_winds trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_paper_winds } }
	picture = GFX_evt_scientist
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.301

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = verena_paper_law
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { society_member_of = cult_of_verena } }
				modifier = { factor = 3 trait = theologian }
			}
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = verena_paper_justice
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { society_member_of = cult_of_verena } }
				modifier = { factor = 3 trait = theologian }
			}
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = verena_paper_wisdom
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { society_member_of = cult_of_verena } }
				modifier = { factor = 3 trait = theologian }
			}
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = verena_paper_hieroglyphs
				modifier = { factor = 3 trait = scholar }
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = erudite }
			}
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = verena_paper_distillation
				modifier = { factor = 3 trait = mystic }
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = erudite }
			}
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = verena_paper_fermentation
				modifier = { factor = 3 trait = mystic }
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = erudite }
			}
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = verena_paper_elixir
				modifier = { factor = 3 trait = mystic }
			}
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = verena_paper_warpstone
				modifier = { factor = 3 trait = mystic }
			}
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = verena_paper_planets
				modifier = { factor = 3 trait = scholar }
				modifier = { factor = 3 trait = diligent }
			}
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = verena_paper_stars
				modifier = { factor = 3 trait = scholar }
			}
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = verena_paper_moons
				modifier = { factor = 3 trait = scholar }
			}
			10 = { set_character_flag = verena_paper_sun modifier = { factor = 3 trait = scholar } }
			20 = {
				set_character_flag = verena_paper_changer
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { society_member_of = cult_of_tzeentch } }
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = schemer }
			}
			20 = {
				set_character_flag = verena_paper_winds
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { society_member_of = cult_of_tzeentch } }
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = mystic }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { which = peer_review value = 0 }

		society = {
			random_society_member = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					is_within_diplo_range = ROOT
					NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
				}
				save_event_target_as = peer1
				set_character_flag = peer_review_temp
			}
			random_society_member = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					is_within_diplo_range = ROOT
					NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
					NOT = { has_character_flag = peer_review_temp }
				}
				save_event_target_as = peer2
				set_character_flag = peer_review_temp
			}
			random_society_member = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					is_within_diplo_range = ROOT
					NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
					NOT = { has_character_flag = peer_review_temp }
				}
				save_event_target_as = peer3
				set_character_flag = peer_review_temp
			}
			random_society_member = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					is_within_diplo_range = ROOT
					NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
					NOT = { has_character_flag = peer_review_temp }
				}
				save_event_target_as = peer4
				set_character_flag = peer_review_temp
			}
			random_society_member = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					is_within_diplo_range = ROOT
					NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
					NOT = { has_character_flag = peer_review_temp }
				}
				save_event_target_as = peer5
				set_character_flag = peer_review_temp
			}
			event_target:peer1 = { clr_character_flag = peer_review_temp }
			event_target:peer2 = { clr_character_flag = peer_review_temp }
			event_target:peer3 = { clr_character_flag = peer_review_temp }
			event_target:peer4 = { clr_character_flag = peer_review_temp }
			event_target:peer5 = { clr_character_flag = peer_review_temp }
		}
	}

	option = { #send it out for review!
		name = EVTOPTAverena302

		event_target:peer1 = {
			character_event = { id = verena.303 days = 15 }
			set_character_flag = show_tooltip
			clr_character_flag = show_tooltip
		}
		event_target:peer2 = {
			character_event = { id = verena.303 days = 18 }
			set_character_flag = show_tooltip
			clr_character_flag = show_tooltip
		}
		event_target:peer3 = {
			character_event = { id = verena.303 days = 21 }
			set_character_flag = show_tooltip
			clr_character_flag = show_tooltip
		}
		event_target:peer4 = {
			character_event = { id = verena.303 days = 24 }
			set_character_flag = show_tooltip
			clr_character_flag = show_tooltip
		}
		event_target:peer5 = {
			character_event = { id = verena.303 days = 27 }
			set_character_flag = show_tooltip
			clr_character_flag = show_tooltip
		}

		custom_tooltip = {
			text = verena_paper_send_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				set_variable = { which = peer_review_score value = 0 }
				character_event = { id = verena.306 days = 100 }
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #No, this shall never see the light of day!
		name = EVTOPTBverena302

		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_paper_burn_tt }
		hidden_effect = { verena_paper_clear_effect = yes }
	}
}

# verena.303 A paper to review
# A reviewer endorses or refutes the paper. Copied work may be found out,
# more often by a sharp reviewer.
# Scope: ROOT is the reviewer, FROM is the writer.
character_event = {
	id = verena.303
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCverena303_law
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = verena_paper_law } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCverena303_justice
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = verena_paper_justice } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCverena303_wisdom
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = verena_paper_wisdom } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCverena303_hieroglyphs
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = verena_paper_hieroglyphs } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCverena303_distillation
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = verena_paper_distillation } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCverena303_fermentation
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = verena_paper_fermentation } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCverena303_elixir
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = verena_paper_elixir } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCverena303_warpstone
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = verena_paper_warpstone } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCverena303_planets
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = verena_paper_planets } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCverena303_stars
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = verena_paper_stars } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCverena303_moons
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = verena_paper_moons } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCverena303_sun
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = verena_paper_sun } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCverena303_changer
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = verena_paper_changer } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCverena303_winds
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = verena_paper_winds } }
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_gathering_of_minds
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.302

	trigger = { # The paper is still under review
		FROM = { verena_paper_under_review_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = theory_paper_stole_work } }
			random_list = {
				10 = { # Discovered knowledge theft
					modifier = { factor = 1.3 intrigue = 2 learning = 2 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.3 intrigue = 4 learning = 4 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.3 intrigue = 6 learning = 6 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.3 intrigue = 8 learning = 8 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.3 intrigue = 10 learning = 10 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.3 intrigue = 12 learning = 12 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.3 intrigue = 14 learning = 14 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.3 intrigue = 16 learning = 16 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.3 intrigue = 18 learning = 18 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.3 intrigue = 20 learning = 20 }

					set_character_flag = discovered_knowledge_theft
				}
				1000 = { #no discovery
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Endorse
		name = EVTOPTAverena303
		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = discovered_knowledge_theft } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 70

			modifier = { factor = 1.3 opinion = { who = ROOT value = 20 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.3 opinion = { who = ROOT value = 40 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.3 opinion = { who = ROOT value = 60 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.3 opinion = { who = ROOT value = 80 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 NOT = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = 1 } } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 NOT = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = -19 } } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 NOT = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = -39 } } }

			modifier = {
				factor = 1.2
				FROM = {
					OR = {
						has_character_flag = theory_paper_average
						has_character_flag = theory_paper_excellent
						has_character_flag = theory_paper_revolutionary
					}
				}
				learning = 7
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.2
				FROM = {
					OR = {
						has_character_flag = theory_paper_excellent
						has_character_flag = theory_paper_revolutionary
					}
				}
				learning = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.2
				FROM = { has_character_flag = theory_paper_revolutionary }
				learning = 13
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.6
				FROM = { has_character_flag = theory_paper_simplistic }
				learning = 7
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				FROM = { has_character_flag = theory_paper_simplistic }
				NOT = { learning = 7 }
			}
		}

		custom_tooltip = {
			text = verena_paper_endorse_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				FROM = {
					change_variable = { which = peer_review_score value = 1 }
					character_event = { id = verena.304 }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Refute
		name = EVTOPTBverena303
		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = discovered_knowledge_theft } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 30 }

		custom_tooltip = {
			text = verena_paper_refute_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				set_character_flag = refuted_paper_@FROM
				FROM = { character_event = { id = verena.305 } }
			}
		}

		tooltip = {
			opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_academic_failure years = 15 }
			reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_academic_grudge years = 15 }
		}
	}

	option = { # Discovered plagiarism
		name = EVTOPTCverena303
		trigger = { has_character_flag = discovered_knowledge_theft }
		add_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
		prestige = 125
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = verena_paper_plagiarism_tt
			hidden_effect = { FROM = { character_event = { id = verena.308 } } }
		}
	}
}

# verena.306 The verdict
# Three endorsements publish the paper; the reviewers who endorsed it hear
# how it went.
# Scope: ROOT is the writer.
character_event = {
	id = verena.306
	desc = { text = EVTDESCverena306 trigger = { has_character_flag = paper_success } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCverena306b trigger = { has_character_flag = paper_failure } }
	picture = GFX_evt_gathering_of_minds
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.302

	immediate = {
		#Calc success/failure
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = peer_review_score value = 3 } }
			set_character_flag = paper_success
		}
		else = { set_character_flag = paper_failure }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAverena306
		trigger = { has_character_flag = paper_success }

		prestige = 75
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = published_theorem years = 5 }
		add_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			event_target:peer1 = { verena_paper_notify_reviewer_effect = yes }
			event_target:peer2 = { verena_paper_notify_reviewer_effect = yes }
			event_target:peer3 = { verena_paper_notify_reviewer_effect = yes }
			event_target:peer4 = { verena_paper_notify_reviewer_effect = yes }
			event_target:peer5 = { verena_paper_notify_reviewer_effect = yes }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBverena306
		trigger = { has_character_flag = paper_failure }

		prestige = -75
		detract_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			event_target:peer1 = { verena_paper_notify_reviewer_effect = yes }
			event_target:peer2 = { verena_paper_notify_reviewer_effect = yes }
			event_target:peer3 = { verena_paper_notify_reviewer_effect = yes }
			event_target:peer4 = { verena_paper_notify_reviewer_effect = yes }
			event_target:peer5 = { verena_paper_notify_reviewer_effect = yes }
		}
	}
	after = {
		clr_character_flag = paper_success
		clr_character_flag = paper_failure
		verena_paper_clear_effect = yes
	}
}

# verena.307 The paper you endorsed
# Scope: ROOT is a reviewer who endorsed it, FROM is the writer.
character_event = {
	id = verena.307
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_paper_endorsed_success }
		text = EVTDESCverena307
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_paper_endorsed_failure }
		text = EVTDESCverena307b
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_gathering_of_minds
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena_paper_notify_reviewer_effect from verena.306

	option = { # Published
		name = EVTOPTAverena307
		trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_paper_endorsed_success }
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = endorsed_theorem years = 5 }
	}
	option = { # Turned down
		name = EVTOPTBverena307
		trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_paper_endorsed_failure }
	}

	after = {
		clr_character_flag = verena_paper_endorsed_success
		clr_character_flag = verena_paper_endorsed_failure
	}
}

# verena.308 Found out
# A reviewer found the copied work and told the brethren: the paper is
# dead and everyone thinks less of the writer.
# Scope: ROOT is the writer, FROM is the reviewer.
character_event = {
	id = verena.308
	desc = EVTDESCverena308
	picture = GFX_evt_scandal
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.303

	trigger = {
		verena_paper_under_review_trigger = yes
	}

	immediate = {
		clear_delayed_event = { id = verena.306 }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAverena308

		prestige = -75
		detract_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
		society = {
			any_society_member = {
				limit = { NOT = { character = ROOT } }
				opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_knowledge_theft years = 20 }
			}
		}
	}

	after = {
		event_target:peer1 = { clr_character_flag = refuted_paper_@ROOT }
		event_target:peer2 = { clr_character_flag = refuted_paper_@ROOT }
		event_target:peer3 = { clr_character_flag = refuted_paper_@ROOT }
		event_target:peer4 = { clr_character_flag = refuted_paper_@ROOT }
		event_target:peer5 = { clr_character_flag = refuted_paper_@ROOT }
		verena_paper_clear_effect = yes
	}
}

# verena.323 The signs or the wandering stars
# The writer narrows the work to one of the two books of the field.
# Scope: ROOT is the writer.
character_event = {
	id = verena.323
	desc = EVTDESCverena323
	picture = GFX_evt_comet
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.321

	option = { # The star signs
		name = EVTOPTAverena323
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					has_artifact = magnum_opus_astrology_stars_1
					has_artifact = magnum_opus_astrology_stars_2
					has_artifact = magnum_opus_astrology_stars_3
					has_artifact = magnum_opus_astrology_stars_4

			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_opus_stars_tt }
		hidden_effect = {
			set_character_flag = verena_opus_stars
			character_event = { id = verena.325 days = 90 random = 60 }
		}
	}
	option = { # The wandering stars
		name = EVTOPTBverena323
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					has_artifact = magnum_opus_astrology_planets_1
					has_artifact = magnum_opus_astrology_planets_2
					has_artifact = magnum_opus_astrology_planets_3
					has_artifact = magnum_opus_astrology_planets_4
				}
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena_opus_planets_tt }
		hidden_effect = {
			set_character_flag = verena_opus_planets
			character_event = { id = verena.325 days = 90 random = 60 }
		}
	}
}

# verena.325 It is finished
# The book is better the more learned the writer: learning 12, 18 and 25
# make the second, third and fourth grade, with 50 to 200 prestige.
# Scope: ROOT is the writer.
character_event = {
	id = verena.325
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_opus_ritual } text = EVTDESCverena325_ritual }
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_opus_contemplation }
		text = EVTDESCverena325_contemplation
	}
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_opus_stars } text = EVTDESCverena325_stars }
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_opus_planets }
		text = EVTDESCverena325_planets
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_opus_panacea }
		text = EVTDESCverena325_panacea
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_opus_transmutation }
		text = EVTDESCverena325_transmutation
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_scientist
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.322 - 324

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAverena325
		trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_opus_ritual }
		if = { limit = { learning = 25 } prestige = 200 add_artifact = magnum_opus_theurgy_ritual_4 }
		else_if = { limit = { learning = 18 } prestige = 150 add_artifact = magnum_opus_theurgy_ritual_3 }
		else_if = { limit = { learning = 12 } prestige = 100 add_artifact = magnum_opus_theurgy_ritual_2 }
		else = { prestige = 50 add_artifact = magnum_opus_theurgy_ritual_1 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBverena325
		trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_opus_contemplation }
		if = { limit = { learning = 25 } prestige = 200 add_artifact = magnum_opus_theurgy_contemplation_4 }
		else_if = {
			limit = { learning = 18 }
			prestige = 150
			add_artifact = magnum_opus_theurgy_contemplation_3
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { learning = 12 }
			prestige = 100
			add_artifact = magnum_opus_theurgy_contemplation_2
		}
		else = { prestige = 50 add_artifact = magnum_opus_theurgy_contemplation_1 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCverena325
		trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_opus_stars }
		if = { limit = { learning = 25 } prestige = 200 add_artifact = magnum_opus_astrology_stars_4 }
		else_if = { limit = { learning = 18 } prestige = 150 add_artifact = magnum_opus_astrology_stars_3 }
		else_if = { limit = { learning = 12 } prestige = 100 add_artifact = magnum_opus_astrology_stars_2 }
		else = { prestige = 50 add_artifact = magnum_opus_astrology_stars_1 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDverena325
		trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_opus_planets }
		if = { limit = { learning = 25 } prestige = 200 add_artifact = magnum_opus_astrology_planets_4 }
		else_if = { limit = { learning = 18 } prestige = 150 add_artifact = magnum_opus_astrology_planets_3 }
		else_if = { limit = { learning = 12 } prestige = 100 add_artifact = magnum_opus_astrology_planets_2 }
		else = { prestige = 50 add_artifact = magnum_opus_astrology_planets_1 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTEverena325
		trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_opus_panacea }
		if = { limit = { learning = 25 } prestige = 200 add_artifact = magnum_opus_alchemy_panacea_4 }
		else_if = { limit = { learning = 18 } prestige = 150 add_artifact = magnum_opus_alchemy_panacea_3 }
		else_if = { limit = { learning = 12 } prestige = 100 add_artifact = magnum_opus_alchemy_panacea_2 }
		else = { prestige = 50 add_artifact = magnum_opus_alchemy_panacea_1 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTFverena325
		trigger = { has_character_flag = verena_opus_transmutation }
		if = { limit = { learning = 25 } prestige = 200 add_artifact = magnum_opus_alchemy_transmutation_4 }
		else_if = {
			limit = { learning = 18 }
			prestige = 150
			add_artifact = magnum_opus_alchemy_transmutation_3
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { learning = 12 }
			prestige = 100
			add_artifact = magnum_opus_alchemy_transmutation_2
		}
		else = { prestige = 50 add_artifact = magnum_opus_alchemy_transmutation_1 }
	}

	after = {
		remove_character_modifier = writing_magnum_opus
		clr_character_flag = verena_opus_ritual
		clr_character_flag = verena_opus_contemplation
		clr_character_flag = verena_opus_stars
		clr_character_flag = verena_opus_planets
		clr_character_flag = verena_opus_panacea
		clr_character_flag = verena_opus_transmutation
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Part 18: the night under the stars
# A member invites a brother or sister to a night of study under the sky.
# Before nightfall the host shows off an observatory, instruments or a hill,
# or holds a feast or a study; the night may bring a theory, a comet, a
# little progress, clouds or Morrslieb waxing green, and the two may part as
# friends or rivals.
###############################################################################

# verena.400 The invitation
# A member of the Order invites a brother or sister to a night of study under
# the stars.
# Scope: ROOT is the guest, FROMFROM is the host.
letter_event = {
	id = verena.400
	desc = EVTDESCverena400
	border = GFX_event_letter_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # decision invite_hermetic_to_stargazing

	option = { # Accept
		name = EVTOPTAverena400

		save_event_target_as = stargazing_guest
		hidden_tooltip = { set_variable = { which = stargazing_satisfaction value = 0 } }
		FROMFROM = { letter_event = { id = verena.401 } }

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 20
			modifier = { factor = 2 OR = { trait = scholar trait = mystic trait = theologian } }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = gregarious }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = trusting }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 OR = { trait = genius trait = quick } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = shrewd }
			modifier = { factor = 2 opinion = { who = FROM value = 30 } }
			modifier = { factor = 3 opinion = { who = FROM value = 60 } }
			modifier = { factor = 10 is_friend = FROM }
		}
	}

	option = { # Decline
		name = EVTOPTBverena400

		FROMFROM = { letter_event = { id = verena.402 } }

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = content }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = shy }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = paranoid }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = slothful }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = envious }
			modifier = { factor = 2 OR = { trait = imbecile trait = slow } }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = dull }
			modifier = { factor = 3 NOT = { opinion = { who = FROM value = -25 } } }
			modifier = { factor = 3 NOT = { opinion = { who = FROM value = -50 } } }
			modifier = { factor = 20 is_rival = FROM }
		}
	}
}

# verena.401 The invitation is accepted
# Scope: ROOT is the host, FROM is the guest.
letter_event = {
	id = verena.401
	desc = EVTDESCverena401
	border = GFX_event_letter_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.400

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAverena401

		reverse_opinion = { modifier = opinion_stargazing who = FROM years = 1 }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			save_event_target_as = stargazing_host
			character_event = { id = verena.405 days = 30 random = 30 }
		}
	}
}

# Invitation declined
# verena.402 The invitation is declined
# Scope: ROOT is the host, FROM is the guest.
letter_event = {
	id = verena.402
	desc = EVTDESCverena402
	border = GFX_event_letter_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.400

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAverena402 # That is disappointing

		opinion = { modifier = opinion_disappointed who = FROM years = 1 }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			clr_character_flag = inviting_guests_to_stargazing_timer
			clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb
			remove_character_modifier = inviting_to_stargazing
		}
	}
}

# Check if able to start stargazing
# verena.405 Can the night go ahead?
# A host at war, incapable or no longer of the Order cancels.
# Scope: ROOT is the host.
character_event = {
	id = verena.405
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.401

	immediate = {
		clr_character_flag = inviting_guests_to_stargazing_timer
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = { war = yes trait = incapable NOT = { society_member_of = cult_of_verena } }
			}
			character_event = { id = verena.406 } # Cancelled
		}
		else = {
			character_event = { id = verena.408 } # The guest arrives
		}
	}
}

# Cancel stargazing
# verena.406 Cancelled
# Scope: ROOT is the host.
character_event = {
	id = verena.406
	desc = EVTDESCverena406
	picture = GFX_evt_comet
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.405

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAverena406

		custom_tooltip = { text = verena406_a_tt }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb
			remove_character_modifier = inviting_to_stargazing
			event_target:stargazing_guest = { letter_event = { id = verena.407 } }
		}
	}
}

	desc = EVTDESCverena407
	border = GFX_event_letter_frame_religion

	}
}

# Guest arrives - What to do before nightfall?
# verena.408 The guest arrives
# Before nightfall the host shows the observatory, the instruments or a good
# hill, holds a feast, or studies with the guest.
# Scope: ROOT is the host.
character_event = {
	id = verena.408
	desc = EVTDESCverena408
	picture = GFX_evt_comet
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.405

	immediate = {
		character_event = { id = verena.425 days = 20 random = 10 }
	}

	option = { # Show observatory
		name = EVTOPTAverena408

		trigger = { has_character_flag = built_observatory }
		tooltip_info = yes
		custom_tooltip = { text = AVAILABLE_DUE_TO_OBSERVATORY }
		custom_tooltip = { text = verena408_a_tt }
		event_target:stargazing_guest = { character_event = { id = verena.410 } }

		ai_chance = { factor = 10 modifier = { factor = 2 trait = scholar } }
	}

	option = { # Show fancy equipment
		name = EVTOPTBverena408

		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = owns_observatory_equipment
			NOT = { has_character_flag = built_observatory }
		}
		tooltip_info = yes
		custom_tooltip = { text = AVAILABLE_DUE_TO_OBSERVATORY_EQUIPMENT }

		custom_tooltip = { text = verena408_b_tt }
		event_target:stargazing_guest = { character_event = { id = verena.413 } }

		ai_chance = { factor = 10 modifier = { factor = 2 trait = scholar } }
	}

	option = { # Show nearby location to use for stargazing
		name = EVTOPTCverena408
		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = built_observatory } }

		custom_tooltip = { text = verena408_c_tt }
		event_target:stargazing_guest = { character_event = { id = verena.416 } }

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = gardener }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = gardener }
		}
	}

	option = { # Sit down for a drink/conversation
		name = EVTOPTDverena408

		custom_tooltip = { text = verena408_d_tt }
		event_target:stargazing_guest = { character_event = { id = verena.419 } }

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
			modifier = { factor = 4 trait = gluttonous }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = hedonist }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = drunkard }
		}
	}

	option = { # Spend some time to study together
		name = EVTOPTEverena408

		custom_tooltip = { text = verena408_e_tt }
		event_target:stargazing_guest = { character_event = { id = verena.422 } }

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = erudite }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = patient }
		}
	}
}

# Show observatory
# verena.410 The observatory
# Scope: ROOT is the guest, FROM is the host.
character_event = {
	id = verena.410
	desc = EVTDESCverena410
	picture = GFX_evt_experiment
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.408

	option = { # Guest is impressed
		name = EVTOPTAverena410

		reverse_opinion = { modifier = opinion_pleased who = FROM years = 1 }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = { trait = scholar trait = mystic trait = theologian trait = humble }
				}
				change_variable = { which = stargazing_satisfaction value = 4 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOR = { trait = scholar trait = mystic trait = theologian trait = humble }
				}
				change_variable = { which = stargazing_satisfaction value = 3 }
			}
			event_target:stargazing_host = {
				character_event = { id = verena.411 days = 7 random = 7 }
			}
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = scholar }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = mystic }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = theologian }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = ambitious }
		}
	}

	option = { # Guest is not impressed
		name = EVTOPTBverena410

		reverse_opinion = { modifier = not_feeling_apreciated who = FROM years = 1 }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:stargazing_host = {
				character_event = { id = verena.412 days = 7 random = 7 }
			}
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 5
			modifier = { factor = 5 trait = envious }
			modifier = { factor = 3 trait = arbitrary }
		}
	}
}

# Sit down for a drink/conversation
# verena.419 The feast
# A temperate or shy guest, or a faithful of Araby who is not decadent, may
# find it too much.
# Scope: ROOT is the guest, FROM is the host.
character_event = {
	id = verena.419
	desc = EVTDESCverena419
	picture = GFX_evt_feast
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.408

	option = { # Guest enjoyed it
		name = EVTOPTAverena419

		reverse_opinion = { modifier = opinion_pleased who = FROM years = 1 }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						trait = hedonist
						trait = socializer
						trait = gregarious
						trait = gluttonous
						trait = drunkard
					}
				}
				change_variable = { which = stargazing_satisfaction value = 2 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOR = {
						trait = hedonist
						trait = socializer
						trait = gregarious
						trait = gluttonous
						trait = drunkard
					}
				}
				change_variable = { which = stargazing_satisfaction value = 1 }
			}
			event_target:stargazing_host = {
				character_event = { id = verena.420 days = 7 random = 7 }
			}
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
			modifier = { factor = 3 trait = gluttonous }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = hedonist }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = socializer }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = gregarious }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = drunkard }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = decadent }
		}
	}

	option = { # Guest did not enjoy it
		name = EVTOPTBverena419

		reverse_opinion = { modifier = not_feeling_apreciated who = FROM years = 1 }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:stargazing_host = {
				character_event = { id = verena.421 days = 7 random = 7 }
			}
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 5
			modifier = { factor = 5 trait = temperate }
			modifier = {
				factor = 6
				AND = { religion_group = ormazd_gods_group NOT = { trait = decadent } }
			}
			modifier = { factor = 3 trait = shy }
		}
	}
}

# Nightfall - Time to stargaze
# verena.425 Nightfall
# Rolls what the night brings: the patterns of the stars, the star signs, a
# comet, a little progress, nothing, Morrslieb waxing or clouds. An
# observatory or instruments help.
# Scope: ROOT is the host.
character_event = {
	id = verena.425
	desc = EVTDESCverena425
	picture = GFX_evt_comet
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.408

	immediate = {
		character_event = { id = verena.441 days = 10 random = 5 }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAverena425

		custom_tooltip = {
			text = PREPARE_STARGAZING

			hidden_tooltip = {
				random_list = {
					10 = { # Make observation about stars
						modifier = { factor = 3 has_character_flag = built_observatory }
						modifier = {
							factor = 2
							has_character_flag = owns_observatory_equipment
							NOT = { has_character_flag = built_observatory }
						}

						character_event = { id = verena.427 days = 5 random = 5 }
					}
					10 = { # Make observation about zodiacs
						modifier = { factor = 3 has_character_flag = built_observatory }
						modifier = {
							factor = 2
							has_character_flag = owns_observatory_equipment
							NOT = { has_character_flag = built_observatory }
						}

						character_event = { id = verena.430 days = 5 random = 5 }
					}
					10 = { # Saw a comet cross the sky
						modifier = { factor = 2 has_character_flag = built_observatory }
						modifier = {
							factor = 1.5
							has_character_flag = owns_observatory_equipment
							NOT = { has_character_flag = built_observatory }
						}

						character_event = { id = verena.433 days = 5 random = 5 }
					}
					20 = { # Learned something new - Nothing major
						character_event = { id = verena.435 days = 5 random = 5 }
					}
					20 = { # Could not see anything of interest
						character_event = { id = verena.437 days = 5 random = 5 }
					}
					5 = { # Morrslieb waxes
						character_event = { id = verena.450 days = 5 random = 5 }
					}
					10 = { # Bad weather ruined the night
						modifier = { factor = 2 OR = { month = 11 NOT = { month = 3 } } }

						character_event = { id = verena.439 days = 5 random = 5 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Make observation about stars
# verena.427 The patterns of the stars
# Scope: ROOT is the host.

	id = verena.427
	desc = EVTDESCverena427
	picture = GFX_evt_comet
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.425

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAverena427

		random_list = {
			10 = { military_techpoints = 100 }
			10 = { economy_techpoints = 100 }
			10 = { culture_techpoints = 100 }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = stargazer } }
			add_character_modifier = { modifier = stargazer duration = 1825 }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			set_character_flag = stargazing_good_outcome
			event_target:stargazing_guest = { character_event = { id = verena.428 } }
		}
	}
}

# Check outcome
# verena.441 Friends or rivals?
# The guest's satisfaction over the night decides.
# Scope: ROOT is the host.
character_event = {
	id = verena.441
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.425

	immediate = {
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				10 = { # Become friends
					modifier = {
						factor = 2
						event_target:stargazing_guest = {
							check_variable = { which = stargazing_satisfaction value = 3 }
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 2
						event_target:stargazing_guest = {
							check_variable = { which = stargazing_satisfaction value = 2 }
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						event_target:stargazing_guest = {
							check_variable = { which = stargazing_satisfaction value = 1 }
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						event_target:stargazing_guest = {
							NOT = { check_variable = { which = stargazing_satisfaction value = 0 } }
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 2
						event_target:stargazing_guest = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = 50 } }
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0 event_target:stargazing_guest = { is_friend = ROOT } }
					character_event = { id = verena.442 }
				}
				5 = { # Become rivals
					modifier = {
						factor = 4
						event_target:stargazing_guest = {
							NOT = { check_variable = { which = stargazing_satisfaction value = 0 } }
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 4
						event_target:stargazing_guest = {
							NOT = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = -25 } }
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						event_target:stargazing_guest = {
							check_variable = { which = stargazing_satisfaction value = 1 }
						}
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0 event_target:stargazing_guest = { is_friend = ROOT } }
					character_event = { id = verena.445 }
				}
				50 = { } # Nothing happens
			}
			character_event = { id = verena.448 days = 10 random = 5 }
		}
	}
}

# Morning arrives - Stargazing is over
# verena.448 Dawn
# Scope: ROOT is the host.
character_event = {
	id = verena.448
	picture = GFX_evt_comet
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes # verena.441

	desc = { text = EVTDESCverena448 trigger = { has_character_flag = stargazing_good_outcome } }
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCverena448b
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = stargazing_bad_outcome
			NOT = { has_character_flag = stargazing_become_friends }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCverena448c
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = stargazing_bad_outcome
			has_character_flag = stargazing_become_friends
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		event_target:stargazing_guest = { character_event = { id = verena.449 } }
	}

	option = {
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTAverena448
			trigger = { has_character_flag = stargazing_good_outcome }
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTAverena448b
			trigger = {
				has_character_flag = stargazing_bad_outcome
				NOT = { has_character_flag = stargazing_become_friends }
			}
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTAverena448c
			trigger = {
				has_character_flag = stargazing_bad_outcome
				has_character_flag = stargazing_become_friends
			}
		}

		hidden_tooltip = {
			add_character_modifier = { name = stargazing_timer duration = 1095 hidden = yes }
			clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb
			clr_character_flag = stargazing_good_outcome
			clr_character_flag = stargazing_bad_outcome
			clr_character_flag = stargazing_become_friends
			remove_character_modifier = inviting_to_stargazing
		}
	}
}
